# Bizora frontend — verified API reference

Generated from five read-only code scans of `origin/main` **`5f176a2`** on 2026-11-09. This is the citation-level companion to
[`frontend.md`](frontend.md); read that first for the readiness picture (the backend does not currently start, and five
API modules are not mounted).

**How to read it.** Every row cites `path:line`. Status words: **LIVE** = routable in `app/urls.py` (works only after the P0 repair in
`frontend.md` §5); **UNMOUNTED** = code exists, no URL includes it; **SERVICE-ONLY** = a Python function with no HTTP route;
**NO ENDPOINT FOUND** = the specification describes it but nothing implements it. Markers: **[LIB]** = Django/DRF library behaviour read
from the library, not executed; **NOT VERIFIED** / *inferred* = derived by reading code, never run. Nothing in the repository was executed by
the scans. The scan agents' remarks about checkouts, scratch folders and "ground truth" refer to facts already stated in `frontend.md` §0.
`<repo>` stands for the repository root.

| Part | Scope | Endpoint ids |
|---|---|---|
| A | Platform, authentication and the canonical domain API (Business Data, Identity & Resolution, Business Operations, Integrations) | `P1`, `D1`–`D26` |
| B | AI Intelligence and Business Memory & Knowledge | `E0`–`E19` |
| C | Integration & Data Observability and Overview & Monitoring (UNMOUNTED) | Observability and Overview route tables |
| D | Business Configuration and Prototype Simulation (UNMOUNTED / CLI only) | `CFG #1`–`#30` |
| E | Business Intelligence and Business Graph APIs (UNMOUNTED) | `BI-1`–`BI-11`, `BG-1`–`BG-11` |

# Part A — Platform, authentication and the canonical domain API (Business Data, Identity & Resolution, Business Operations, Integrations)

## SCAN A: Platform, authentication and the generic domain API
Repo: <repo>, commit 5f176a2 (origin/main). Read-only scan, nothing executed, nothing imported.
Citation form `path:line`. `NOT VERIFIED` = needs a running stack or library source that is not in this checkout (DRF/Django are not installed locally, so DRF/Django library behaviour is quoted from DRF 3.15.2 / Django 4.2 defaults and labelled `[LIB]`).

### 0. HEADLINE FINDINGS (read first)

H1. **The domain API cannot import at 5f176a2, beyond the three breakages already known.** 11 model classes that the domain code uses are NOT defined in `app/models.py` at HEAD:
`AdAccount, CallbackRequest, CampaignAd, CampaignAdSet, CampaignCreative, ConversationEvent, DomainAudit, IdentityHistory, ProcessingReceipt, ProductImage, SupportTeam`.
Evidence: `app/domains/registry.py:5-38` builds `RESOURCES` at import time with `m.ProductImage`, `m.AdAccount`, `m.CampaignAdSet`, `m.CampaignCreative`, `m.CampaignAd`, `m.CallbackRequest`; `app/domain_views.py:13` imports that module; `app/urls.py:85` imports `domain_views`. `app/models.py:673` also has `Campaign.ad_account = ForeignKey('AdAccount')` pointing at the missing class. The classes exist in `git show e487b0b:app/models.py` (lines 1635-1770, a "Domain extensions" block; `BusinessMembership` at 1635, `SupportTeam` 1646, `ProductImage` 1758) and are gone from 396593f/5f176a2 (`git log -m -S"class DomainAudit" -- app/models.py`). The migrations that create their tables still exist (`app/migrations/0002_domain_foundations.py`, `0004_product_images.py`, `0007_requested_callbacks.py`).
The same lost block redefined `BusinessMembership` with role choices `VIEWER/OPERATOR/MANAGER/OWNER`; at HEAD only the older definition remains (`app/models.py:84-93`, `role` default `"MEMBER"`, no choices, `db_table business_memberships`).
Consequence: because `app/urls.py:85` imports `domain_views` at module level, the **entire `app.urls` URLconf fails to load** (so `GET /api/` and the AI routes break too, not only the domain routes). All field facts below for those 11 models come from the e487b0b definitions plus the migration files and are marked `[e487b0b]`.

H2. **Migration graph has 3 parallel lineages and no merge migration** (verified by reading every `dependencies`): AI `0002_aiaction_approved_by_and_more -> ... -> 0007_businessgoal_category_businessgoal_priority_and_more`; domain `0002_domain_foundations -> 0003_domain_integrity -> 0004_product_images -> 0005_variant_deduplication -> 0006_message_location -> 0007_requested_callbacks -> 0008_identity_confidence_unknown`; observability `0002_integration_data_observability -> 0003_overview_monitoring -> 0004_business_configuration`. Both `0002_domain_foundations` (VIEWER..OWNER roles, no created_at) and `0004_aiaction_rejected_at_...` (role default MEMBER, db_table business_memberships) execute `CreateModel('BusinessMembership')`. Whether `migrate` fails is NOT VERIFIED (not run), but three leaf nodes exist.

H3. **A separate-origin SPA will NOT work today.** Only Session + Basic auth, no token/JWT, no CORS middleware, no CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS, no JSON login endpoint, no endpoint that sets the CSRF cookie, no "who am I / my businesses" endpoint. See section 1.

H4. **No PATCH/PUT/DELETE anywhere in the domain API**, and no generic "edit product / edit customer" command. Writes are: idempotent POST upsert (ingest), POST transition, and ~12 task commands. See sections 2.3 and 2.4.

H5. Missing/related objects return **400 INVALID_RELATION** on most command/context endpoints, not 404; only `GET {name}/{pk}/` and `POST {name}/{pk}/transition/` return 404 for a missing record. See section 2 conventions.

### 1. SCOPE, AUTHENTICATION AND CONVENTIONS (assignment items a, b, e)

Scope: platform wiring (settings/urls), authentication, the `businesses/{id}/...` domain API served by `app/domain_views.py` (generic resource routes, identity, operations, contracts), the `/api/` status endpoint, and the Django-rendered `/ops/` UI that the new frontend replaces. AI endpoints in `app/views.py` (app/urls.py:24-82) are another scan; only shared helpers are noted.

#### 1a. How a client authenticates today
- URL mounts: `bizora/urls.py:22` `api/` -> app.urls, `:25` `api/v1/` -> app.urls (same views twice), `:23` `ops/` -> app.operations_urls, `:24`/`:26` `admin/` (listed twice).
- DRF settings `bizora/settings.py:168-180`: renderers JSON (+Browsable only if `DEBUG`), parsers JSON/Form/MultiPart, `DEFAULT_PERMISSION_CLASSES=[IsAuthenticated]`. **No `DEFAULT_AUTHENTICATION_CLASSES`** -> DRF default `[SessionAuthentication, BasicAuthentication]` `[LIB]`. The same pair is written out explicitly only in `bizora/local_settings.py:18-23` (a settings module that `manage.py:9`, `wsgi.py:14`, `asgi.py:14`, `docker-compose.yml:12` do NOT select; they select `bizora.settings`).
- **Login**: there is no JSON/API login. Two HTML logins exist: `POST /ops/login/` (`app/operations_urls.py:5`, `django.contrib.auth.views.LoginView`, template `operations/login.html`, form fields `username`, `password`, hidden `next`, `csrfmiddlewaretoken`; success redirects to `next` or `/ops/`) and Django admin `/admin/login/` (staff users only). Logout: `POST /ops/logout/` only (`require_POST`, CSRF required; redirects to `/ops/login/`). `LOGIN_URL` is not set anywhere (grep), views pass `login_url='/ops/login/'` explicitly (`app/operations_ui.py:92`).
- **Session cookie**: Django defaults (settings.py has no SESSION_*/CSRF_* overrides: grep of `bizora/*.py` finds none): cookie `sessionid`, DB-backed session (`django.contrib.sessions` installed, `settings.py:63`), `SameSite=Lax`, not `Secure`. CSRF cookie `csrftoken`, header `X-CSRFToken` `[LIB defaults]`.
- **CSRF**: `CsrfViewMiddleware` is installed (`settings.py:75`). For session-authenticated unsafe requests (POST) DRF `SessionAuthentication` enforces CSRF `[LIB]` -> 403 `{"detail":"CSRF Failed: ..."}` if the `X-CSRFToken` header is missing. Docs agree: "Session writes require CSRF" (docs/implementations/TEAM_LEAD_HANDOFF.md, contracts/README.md:65). Evidence the ops UI enforces it: `app/test_integration_readiness.py:289-299`. **Nothing in the JSON API sets the `csrftoken` cookie** (no `ensure_csrf_cookie`/`get_token` use outside templates; grep; only HTML pages that render `{% csrf_token %}`, including the DEBUG-only Browsable API `[LIB]`). The cookie is only issued when a page renders `{% csrf_token %}`, i.e. `GET /ops/login/` (`templates/operations/login.html`) or the admin login.
- **Basic fallback**: `Authorization: Basic base64(user:pass)` is accepted by DRF default `[LIB]`; CSRF is not enforced for Basic. contracts/README.md:65 says "Basic auth is supported for localhost/HTTPS".
- **Unauthenticated response** `[LIB]`: DRF `IsAuthenticated` with Session first (no `WWW-Authenticate`) -> HTTP **403** `{"detail":"Authentication credentials were not provided."}`, not 401. The repo's own test accepts either (`app/test_domains.py:599-601`). Wrong Basic credentials also yield 403 for the same reason `[LIB]`.
- **Token/JWT/CORS**: none. `INSTALLED_APPS` (`settings.py:60-69`) = django.contrib.{admin,auth,contenttypes,sessions,messages,staticfiles}, rest_framework, app. `MIDDLEWARE` (`:71-79`) has no CORS middleware. grep for corsheaders/authtoken/simplejwt/CSRF_TRUSTED/SESSION_COOKIE finds nothing outside docs. `ALLOWED_HOSTS` default `localhost,127.0.0.1,[::1]` (`settings.py:51-55`).
- **Public endpoint**: only `GET /api/` and `/api/v1/` (MessageView, `AllowAny`).
- **Users / memberships are not creatable over the API.** No registration, password reset, membership or team CRUD endpoint. `BusinessMembership` is not registered in admin (`app/admin.py` registers only observability models). A membership is created by `python manage.py demo_domains --username X` (`app/management/commands/demo_domains.py:28-37`, role OWNER, refuses the tracked db.sqlite3 and non-SQLite) or the shell.

**Verdict, separate-origin SPA (e.g. https://app.example or http://localhost:5173 calling the API host):** will NOT work as shipped. (1) No CORS headers: browsers block reading responses and the preflight is unanswered. (2) Session cookie is `SameSite=Lax`: cross-site fetch/XHR does not send it (same-site-but-cross-origin, e.g. localhost:5173 -> localhost:8000, does send it but CORS still blocks the read). (3) Django's CSRF origin check would reject cross-origin HTTPS POSTs unless the SPA origin is in `CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS` (unset). (4) No API route issues the CSRF cookie and no login route returns JSON. What does work today: (a) a **same-origin** deployment (reverse proxy serving SPA and `/api/` from one host; dev: Vite/webpack proxy) using `/ops/login/` or `/admin/login/` to obtain `sessionid` and `csrftoken`, then sending `X-CSRFToken` on every POST; or (b) HTTP Basic over TLS from a server-side/proxy client (no browser CORS). The new frontend needs server work for a standalone SPA: JSON login/logout/CSRF-bootstrap + "me" endpoint, and CORS + CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS + cookie SameSite/Secure settings (or token auth).

#### 1b. Business scoping, membership and roles (`businesses/{business_id}/...` domain routes)
Single choke point: `DomainAPIView.business()` `app/domain_views.py:27-33`.
1. DRF auth + `IsAuthenticated` (`:25`).
2. Membership lookup: `BusinessMembership(user=request.user, user__is_active=True, business_id=<url id>, is_active=True)`. None -> `DomainError('NOT_FOUND','Business not found.')` -> **HTTP 404 `{"code":"NOT_FOUND","detail":"Business not found."}`**. A non-member, a member of another business only, an inactive membership and a nonexistent business are indistinguishable (comment `:28`). **Superusers get no bypass** (test `app/test_domains.py:616-620`).
3. `authorize(user, business, write, manager)` `app/domains/common.py:123-130` by role string on the membership:
   - read (default): `OWNER, MANAGER, OPERATOR, VIEWER`
   - write (`write=True`): `OWNER, MANAGER, OPERATOR`
   - manager (`manager=True`): `OWNER, MANAGER`
   - otherwise `DomainError('FORBIDDEN','Your business role cannot perform this operation.')` -> **HTTP 403 `{"code":"FORBIDDEN","detail":"Your business role cannot perform this operation."}`**. A role not in {VIEWER,OPERATOR,MANAGER,OWNER} (e.g. the model default `MEMBER`, or `ADMIN` used by the AI views) is denied everything, including reads.
4. The service functions re-run `authorize` and lock the business row (`select_for_update`) before writing (`data.py:128-131`, `operations.py`, `identity.py:lock`).
Required role per route is given in the table (R/W/M). Identity (`identity/*`, GET and POST), `actions/{pk}/review/`, `identity/merge-candidates/{pk}/dry-run/` need **M**. Reads of everything else need **R**; all other POSTs need **W**; `contracts/integrations/*` needs **W**, other `contracts/*` need **R**.
Role vocabularies differ between surfaces: AI action views allow `{"OWNER","ADMIN","OPERATOR"}` (`app/views.py:75,102,120`) and use `require_business_access` (superuser bypass, membership-only for `BusinessScopedAPIView`, `app/services/authorization.py:6-15`); AI business-scoped views return DRF 403 `{"detail":...}` for non-members and 404 for unknown business id (`app/views.py:50-57`), the AI action views return `{"error": "..."}`. The new `app/observability` model `BusinessAccess` has roles `READER/MANAGER` (`app/observability/models.py:12-15`), a third vocabulary. The frontend must not assume one role set.

#### 1c. `/api/` status endpoint (assignment item e)
`GET /api/` and `GET /api/v1/` -> `MessageView` (`app/views.py:60-64`, `app/urls.py:23`, name `message`): `AllowAny`, no auth, no CSRF. 200 body exactly `{"message": "Bizora API is running."}` (MessageSerializer, `app/serializers/base.py:4-5`). Test: `app/tests/test_status.py`, `app/test_domains.py:593-598`. Only GET is defined; other methods 405 `[LIB]`.

#### 1d. Common conventions used in every table row below
- Prefix **B** = `/api/businesses/{business_id}/` (identical under `/api/v1/`). `business_id`, `pk` are `<int:...>` path converters (`app/urls.py`).
- Status LIVE = routable from `app/urls.py` today, **subject to H1 (module will not import)**. UNMOUNTED = urlconf exists, no include. SERVICE-ONLY = Python function, no route.
- **Error envelopes** (all produced by `DomainAPIView.handle_exception`, `app/domain_views.py:35-45`):
  - E-DOM: `{"code": "<CODE>", "detail": "<message>"}`; HTTP 403 if code=FORBIDDEN, 404 if NOT_FOUND, **409 if STALE_EVENT / STALE_STATE / IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT / HANDOVER_CONFLICT**, otherwise **400** (including INVALID_RELATION, INVALID_FILTER, INVALID_DATA, INVALID_KEY, STALE_PLAN, IMMUTABLE_*, IDENTITY_CONFLICT, ...).
  - `ObjectDoesNotExist` -> 404 `{"code":"NOT_FOUND","detail":"Record not found."}`; Django `ValidationError`/`ValueError`/`TypeError` (e.g. `int('abc')` in query params, bad boolean/ID filter values) -> 400 `{"code":"INVALID_DATA","detail":"Invalid field value."}`; `IntegrityError` -> 409 `{"code":"CONSTRAINT_CONFLICT","detail":"The operation conflicts with existing domain state."}`.
  - E-DRF (serializer failures, `raise_exception=True`): 400 with DRF's own shape `{"<field>": ["msg", ...]}` (or a list) - **not** `{code,detail}`. contracts/README.md:34 says "HTTP error shape is {code, detail}"; that is incomplete.
  - E-AUTH: 403 `{"detail": "Authentication credentials were not provided."}` `[LIB]`; CSRF failure 403 `{"detail":"CSRF Failed: ..."}` `[LIB]`; 405 `{"detail":"Method \"X\" not allowed."}` `[LIB]`; bad page 404 `{"detail":"Invalid page."}` `[LIB]`.
  - Unhandled exceptions are Django 500 HTML (not JSON).
- **DomainError subclasses ValueError** (`common.py:11`) so it is matched first. Error precedence on a write: auth -> 404 business -> 403 role -> 400 serializer -> service-level DomainErrors.
- **Related/missing records**: `related()` (`common.py:107-114`) raises `INVALID_RELATION` -> **400** for a missing or foreign-tenant id and for non-integer/bool ids. Used by 360, contexts, assign, handover, priority, message read, callback schedule, dry-run, journey, graph-context, FK fields in bodies. 404 `NOT_FOUND` only on `GET {name}/{pk}/` (via `.get()` DoesNotExist) and `POST {name}/{pk}/transition/` (`data.py:477`).
- **Serialization** (`projections.serialize`, `app/domains/projections.py:10-13`): every model field, key = field name, value = DB attname value. Foreign keys render as the **plain integer id under the field name** (`"customer": 5`, `"business": 1`, `"integration_connection": 3`). Fields `source_metadata`, `config`, `recording_url` are **never returned** (writable but write-only). Decimals -> JSON numbers `[LIB: DRF JSONEncoder returns float(Decimal)]`; datetimes ISO-8601 with `Z`. `section(qs)` = `{"count": n, "results": [...<=50...], "truncated": bool}` (`projections.py:16-19`).
- **Pagination** (`DomainPagination`, `domain_views.py:18-21`, DRF PageNumberPagination): `page`, `page_size` (default 25, max 100) -> `{"count","next","previous","results"}` with absolute next/previous URLs `[LIB]`. `?page=last` accepted `[LIB]`. Applies to: resource lists, support-queue (plus extra `capabilities` key), identity GETs, financial-timeline, visitor journey. Detail/360/overview use 50-row `section`s with `truncated` instead.
- Money input: send strings like `"12.50"` (max 2 decimals, >=0, < 1e16, else `INVALID_MONEY`, `common.py:22-30`). Timestamps inside `data` must carry a UTC offset (`INVALID_TIME`, `data.py:50-51`); envelope-level `occurred_at` and the `scheduled_at` field use DRF `DateTimeField`, which accepts naive values as UTC `[LIB]`.

### 2. ENDPOINT TABLE (assignment items c, d, e)
B = `/api/businesses/{business_id}/` (also `/api/v1/businesses/{business_id}/`). Roles: R read, W write, M manager (section 1b). Status LIVE is subject to H1. Source = `app/urls.py` route line / `app/domain_views.py` view lines / service.
Route-order note: `app/urls.py:96` (`identity/<command>/`) and `:97-100` (generic) precede `:103-122`; no later route is shadowed because every specific route has a different segment count or a literal segment, and `<int:pk>` rejects words like `assess`.

| # | Method | Path | Status | Auth | Query params | Request body | Response | Errors | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | GET | `/api/`, `/api/v1/` | LIVE | none | - | - | 200 `{"message":"Bizora API is running."}` | 405 | app/urls.py:23; app/views.py:60-64 |
| D1 | GET | B`{name}/` | LIVE | R | `page,page_size,q,since,until,ordering,contact,contact_type,tag,external_id,connection_id` + per-resource exact-match filters (section 2.1) | - | 200 paginated `{count,next,previous,results:[record...]}`; row = `serialize()` | 404 NOT_FOUND "Unknown resource."; 400 INVALID_FILTER (unsupported key; unsupported search; since/until not ISO-with-offset or model has no date field; ordering not newest/oldest; external_id/connection_id not given together; contact on non-customers; contact_type without contact; source lookup unsupported); INVALID_CONTACT; INVALID_RELATION (connection not owned); INVALID_DATA (non-int connection_id, bad bool/id value) | app/urls.py:100; domain_views.py:59-96; queries.py:8-33 |
| D2 | POST | B`{name}/` | LIVE | W | - | header **`Idempotency-Key`** (required, 1-180 chars); JSON `{"data":{...canonical fields...}, "connection_id":int>=1 (opt), "occurred_at":ISO ts (opt, default now)}` | **201** created, **200** replay / upsert of existing record; flat `serialize(record)`; orders return the order only (items via detail) | 400 E-DRF if `data` missing or not object; 400 INVALID_KEY (header missing/too long); UNKNOWN_FIELD (field unknown or server-managed); INVALID_DATA; INVALID_RELATION; INVALID_STATUS; INVALID_MONEY; INVALID_TIME; INVALID_IDENTIFIER; INVALID_CURRENCY; CURRENCY_MISMATCH; CONNECTION_UNAVAILABLE (connection status is not CONNECTED); IMMUTABLE_RECORD/IMMUTABLE_PAYMENT/IMMUTABLE_RELATION; PRODUCT_REVIEW_REQUIRED; resource-specific (section 2.3); **409** IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT (same key, different payload), STALE_EVENT (older occurred_at than stored receipt); 403 FORBIDDEN (VIEWER); 404 NOT_FOUND unknown resource | app/urls.py:100; domain_views.py:98-103; data.py:126-370 |

SERVICE-ONLY inventory (Python functions, no HTTP route, in or adjacent to this scope):
- `app/domains/mock_transport.py:10,38,54` MockCommerceTransport.receive (product/customer fixtures -> `data.ingest`), MockMessageTransport.send (marks a mock-connection OUTGOING message SENT via `data.transition`), MockCanonicalTransport.receive (`bizora.ingest.v1` envelope -> `data.ingest`, requires `is_mock` connection). Used by `manage.py demo_domains` and tests only.
- `app/integrations/payloads.py:12` `capture_payload` (immutable payload capture, 256 KiB cap); `app/integrations/operations.py` `operations_for`, `register_adapter`, `MockIntegrationOperations` (PRODUCT only; uses `app/business_data/products.py` `persist_product`/`confirm_product`).
- `app/domains/merge.py:17` `plan` (also exposed read-only through D24), `app/domains/boundaries.py:55` `exchange` (exposed through D26), every public function in `identity.py`, `operations.py` and `data.py` (`ingest`, `transition`, `update_variant`) is exposed by a route above; `identity.conflict`, `identity.history`, `operations.unavailable` are internal helpers.

Not mounted by contrast: `GET B` (business detail), `GET /api/businesses/` (my businesses), any "me" endpoint, teams, memberships, users, connections list, audit log, delete or PATCH/PUT of any record: **NO ENDPOINT FOUND**.

Why contracts are always UNAVAILABLE today: `BIZORA_DOMAIN_ADAPTERS` is read via `getattr(settings, ..., {})` (`boundaries.py:36-38`) and is defined nowhere in the repo's settings modules (grep: only docs and tests `override_settings`). The docs' own adapter paths are examples (TEAM_LEAD_HANDOFF.md:66 "not shipped services").

#### 2.1 Resource registry behind `B{name}/`, `B{name}/{pk}/`, `B{name}/{pk}/transition/` (assignment item c)
Exactly **32** names are accepted (`app/domains/registry.py:5-38`, "explicit allowlist, no arbitrary Django model CRUD"). Anything else -> 404 `NOT_FOUND "Unknown resource."` on all three routes. `[e487b0b]` = model class missing at HEAD (H1). All 32 support: list (GET), detail (GET), ingest/upsert (POST). **No resource supports PATCH/PUT/DELETE.**
List filters are exact-match, one value each (no ranges/multi-value). Allowed filter keys per resource = `{status,priority,channel,customer,order,product,campaign,conversation,direction,provider,payment_method,category,sku,tag,visitor,session,payment,contact_type,assignee_type,traffic_source,traffic_medium,utm_campaign,device_type,browser,operating_system,event_type,is_anonymous}` intersected with that model's field names (`domain_views.py:63-67`); the intersection per resource is in column "filters". NOT filterable on any resource: `message`, `ad_set`, `call`, `shipment`, `platform`, `utm_source/medium/term/content`, `assignee_type`, `creative`, `product_variant`.
Control params on every list: `page`, `page_size`(<=100), `q`, `since`, `until` (ISO-8601 **with offset**; encode `+` as `%2B` or use `Z`), `ordering=newest|oldest` (default newest; sorts by the date field then pk), `contact`, `external_id`, `connection_id`. `since/until/ordering` use the first existing field of `occurred_at, started_at, ordered_at, created_at` (column "date field"); none -> `since/until` 400 and ordering by pk.

| name | model (business scope) | source-id field (ingest upsert key) | filters | `q` searches | date field | lifecycle (transition) | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| products | Product | via nested `external_reference` | status,category,sku | name,sku | created_at | yes | `external_id`+`connection_id` lookup via ProductExternalReference |
| variants | ProductVariant (product) | (product,sku) | product,sku,status | name,sku | created_at | yes | also `variants/{pk}/update/` |
| product-images | ProductImage `[e487b0b]` (product) | (product,url) | product | none -> 400 | created_at | no | url must be http(s) |
| product-references | ProductExternalReference (product) | external_product_id (+external_variant_id) | product | none -> 400 | created_at | no | needs envelope `connection_id` |
| customers | Customer | via nested `external_reference` | status,tag,contact_type (+`contact`) | display_name | created_at | yes | `contact`(+`contact_type`, default EMAIL) = normalized exact contact lookup; `tag` = exact, **upper-cased by the server** |
| customer-references | CustomerExternalReference (customer) | external_customer_id | customer | none -> 400 | created_at | no | needs `connection_id` |
| contacts | CustomerContact (customer) | (customer,contact_type,normalized_value) | customer,contact_type | none -> 400 | created_at | no | |
| tags | CustomerTag (customer) | (customer,tag) | customer,tag | none -> 400 | created_at | no | tag stored as sent here (nested `customers.tags` are upper-cased) - see gaps |
| orders | Order | external_order_id | customer,status | order_number | **ordered_at** | yes | immutable snapshot after create |
| payments | Payment | external_payment_id | order,customer,payment_method,status | none -> 400 | created_at | yes | |
| refunds | Refund (payment) | external_refund_id (per payment) | payment,order,status | none -> 400 | created_at | yes (special-cased, not in TRANSITIONS) | |
| conversations | Conversation | external_conversation_id | customer,channel,status,priority | subject, customer display_name, message content (`queries.py:35-38`) | **started_at** | yes | |
| participants | ConversationParticipant (conversation) | none | conversation,customer | display_name | created_at | no | |
| messages | Message (conversation) | external_message_id | conversation,customer,direction | content | created_at | yes (field `delivery_status`) | immutable after create; customer-sender content is wrapped, see 5 |
| attachments | MessageAttachment (message) | none | (none) | none -> 400 | created_at | no | |
| notes | ConversationNote (conversation) | none (always creates) | conversation | content | created_at | no | author fields are server-set |
| campaigns | Campaign | external_campaign_id | status | name | **started_at** | yes | `platform`/`provider` are not filters |
| campaign-metrics | CampaignMetric (campaign) | (campaign,metric_date) | campaign | none -> 400 | created_at | no | |
| conversions | CampaignConversion (campaign) | external_conversion_id (per campaign) | campaign,customer,order | none -> 400 | created_at | no | attribution_type restricted |
| ad-accounts | AdAccount `[e487b0b]` | external_account_id | (none) | name | created_at | no | |
| ad-sets | CampaignAdSet `[e487b0b]` (campaign) | external_id (per campaign) | campaign,status | name | **none (pk)** | no | since/until -> 400 |
| creatives | CampaignCreative `[e487b0b]` | external_id | (none) | `content` JSON field icontains (NOT VERIFIED) | none (pk) | no | |
| ads | CampaignAd `[e487b0b]` (ad_set->campaign) | external_id (per ad set) | status | name | none (pk) | no | |
| visitors | WebsiteVisitor | visitor_key | is_anonymous,customer | none -> 400 | created_at | no | always anonymous on create |
| sessions | WebsiteSession | external_session_id | visitor,customer,traffic_source,traffic_medium,utm_campaign,device_type,browser,operating_system | none -> 400 | **started_at** | no | |
| website-events | WebsiteEvent (session) | none (always creates) | session,visitor,customer,product,order,event_type | none -> 400 | **occurred_at** | no | immutable |
| shipments | Shipment | external_shipment_id | order,customer,provider,status | tracking_code | created_at | yes | |
| shipment-events | ShipmentEvent (shipment) | none | status,event_type | none -> 400 | **occurred_at** | no | |
| calls | Call | external_call_id | customer,conversation,order,direction,status | none -> 400 | **started_at** | yes | `recording_url` write-only |
| call-events | CallEvent (call) | none | event_type | none -> 400 | created_at | no | |
| transcripts | CallTranscript (call) | none | provider | none -> 400 | created_at | no | |
| callbacks | CallbackRequest `[e487b0b]` | none | customer,conversation,order,status | none -> 400 | created_at | yes | also `callbacks/{pk}/schedule/` |

`external_id`+`connection_id` source lookup (both required together; connection must be owned) is supported only for: products, customers, product-references, customer-references, orders, payments, conversations, messages, campaigns, ad-accounts, sessions, shipments, calls (needs `integration_connection` field and a source id). Others -> 400 INVALID_FILTER "Source lookup is unsupported for this resource." (`queries.py:21-34`).

#### 2.2 `GET B{name}/{pk}/` response keys (`projections.detail`, projections.py:78-133)
Always `record` (serialized row), `intelligence` (authority context: sections `fact_evidence, issue_evidence, recommendation_evidence, facts, issues, relationships, graph_nodes, recommendations, metric_definitions, metrics` and UNAVAILABLE placeholders `ai_answers, attribution_analysis, freshness`; `projections.py:22-46`), and, if the row has a non-null `customer`/`order`/`conversation`/`product` FK, the **expanded related record under that same key**. Child sections (each `{count,results<=50,truncated}`):
- products: `variants, product-images, orders, external_references, customers, shipments, calls, related_conversations`
- orders: `payments, refunds, shipments, calls, items, history(OrderStatusHistory), related_conversations`
- payments: `refunds`; conversations: `messages(asc), participants, notes, calls, assignments, history(ConversationEvent)`; messages: `attachments`
- campaigns: `campaign-metrics, conversions, ad-sets`; visitors: `sessions, website-events`; sessions: `website-events`; shipments: `shipment-events`; calls: `call-events, transcripts` + `recording_access: UNAVAILABLE("Calling Integration: authorized recording transport")`
- every other resource: `record`, FK expansions, `intelligence` only.
Sections cap at 50 rows; for more, page the child list (`messages/?conversation={id}`, `notes/?conversation=`, `website-events/?session=`...).

#### 2.3 Writable fields for `POST B{name}/` (`data` object; assignment item c)
Rule (`data.py:18-54`): allowed keys = the model's non-pk fields minus `READ_ONLY` = `{id,business,created_at,updated_at,total_amount,subtotal,normalized_value,ai_handover_state,last_message_at,resolved_at,author_type,author_id,is_ai_generated,is_verified}` (registry.py:54-56) plus the nested keys `{products:variants,external_reference,deduplication; customers:contacts,tags,external_reference; orders:items; messages:attachments}`. Any other key -> 400 `UNKNOWN_FIELD "Unknown or server-managed field: x, y"`. FK fields take a **positive JSON integer** id owned by the same business (strings/bools -> INVALID_RELATION). Text fields reject numbers (except `external_*` ids may be int and are stored as text). Booleans must be real booleans. Integers must be real integers. Money = text/number with <=2 dp (`ctr,cpc,cpm,roas` and `confidence` are plain non-negative decimals, confidence <=1). Timestamps need an offset. Enum-like text (`direction,sender_type,message_type,channel,contact_type,priority,customer_type`) is trimmed, upper-cased and spaces->underscores before validation (`data.py:169-171`). `currency` defaults to the business currency, must be 3 letters, is upper-cased. If `data.integration_connection` is sent it must equal the envelope `connection_id` (`data.py:151-155`); simplest is to omit it and send `connection_id`. `source_metadata`/`metadata`/`properties`/`audience`/`content`(JSON) accepted as JSON objects; `source_metadata` is never echoed back.
R = required by code or by a non-null model column without default.

| resource | required | optional / special |
|---|---|---|
| products | name | sku, description, category, status, price, currency, inventory_quantity(>=0), image_url(http/https), source_metadata; nested `variants[]` (variant fields), `external_reference{external_product_id(R), metadata?}` (needs `connection_id`), `deduplication{decision:"CREATE_DISTINCT",reason}` (M only). SKU already used by another product with no source mapping -> 400 `PRODUCT_REVIEW_REQUIRED`, nothing created |
| variants | product | name, sku, attributes(JSON), price, inventory_quantity, status |
| product-images | product, url | alt_text, position |
| product-references | product, external_product_id, `connection_id` | product_variant (then external_variant_id R), external_variant_id, external_sku, source_metadata |
| customers | (none) | first_name, last_name, display_name, status, customer_type(default KNOWN), notes, source_metadata; nested `contacts[]{contact_type(R),value(R),is_primary}`, `tags[]` (strings 1-100, stored UPPER), `external_reference{external_customer_id}` (needs `connection_id`) |
| customer-references | customer, external_customer_id, `connection_id` | external_username, source_metadata |
| contacts | customer, contact_type, value | is_primary. (`normalized_value`, `is_verified` read-only; verify via `identity/verify-contact`). contact_type values: section 3 |
| tags | customer, tag(<=100) | (`source` forced MANUAL) |
| orders | external_order_id, `items[]` (non-empty) each {unit_price(R), product_name(R unless `product`), quantity(int>0, default 1), product, product_variant, external_product_id, sku, discount_amount, metadata} | customer, order_number, status, currency, discount_amount, shipping_amount, tax_amount, ordered_at, source_metadata. `subtotal`/`total_amount` computed server-side. Item `product_name`/`sku` overwritten from product when `product` given; order and product currency must match |
| payments | external_payment_id, amount(>0) | order, customer, transaction_reference, payment_method, status, currency, paid_at, source_metadata. status PARTIALLY_REFUNDED/REFUNDED rejected (derived from refunds). Order and payment currency must match |
| refunds | payment, amount(>0), external_refund_id | order(default payment.order), currency, status, reason, refunded_at, source_metadata. Payment must be PAID/PARTIALLY_REFUNDED, sum of non-FAILED/CANCELLED refunds <= payment.amount (EXCESSIVE_REFUND) |
| conversations | external_conversation_id, channel | customer, status, priority, subject, started_at(default occurred_at), source_metadata |
| participants | conversation, participant_type | customer, external_user_id, display_name, joined_at, left_at, metadata |
| messages | conversation, external_message_id, direction, sender_type, and one of content / `attachments[]` / message_type LOCATION | customer, message_type(default TEXT), content, `location{latitude,longitude,label?}` (LOCATION only), delivery_status(default PENDING), read_status(null/UNREAD/READ), sent_at, delivered_at, read_at, source_metadata; nested `attachments[]{attachment_type(R),url(R http/https, no credentials),file_name(no path chars),mime_type,size_bytes,external_attachment_id,metadata}` |
| attachments | message, attachment_type, url | file_name, mime_type, size_bytes(>=0), external_attachment_id, metadata |
| notes | conversation, content | metadata (author set to AGENT/current user) |
| campaigns | external_campaign_id, name, platform | ad_account, objective, status, budget, currency, started_at, ended_at, source_metadata |
| campaign-metrics | campaign, metric_date(YYYY-MM-DD) | impressions, reach, clicks, conversions (ints>=0), spend, conversion_value, ctr, cpc, cpm, roas, metadata |
| conversions | campaign, conversion_type, converted_at, external_conversion_id | customer, order, website_session_id(int), conversion_value, currency, attribution_type (null/PLATFORM_REPORTED/DIRECT_SOURCE_REPORTED only), metadata |
| ad-accounts | external_account_id, name | audience; connection from envelope |
| ad-sets | campaign, external_id, name | audience, budget, status(ACTIVE/PAUSED/COMPLETED) |
| creatives | external_id | content(JSON) |
| ads | ad_set, external_id, name | creative, status(ACTIVE/PAUSED/COMPLETED) |
| visitors | visitor_key | first_seen_at(default occurred_at), last_seen_at(forced to occurred_at), is_anonymous(only true), source_metadata. Sending `customer` -> 400 IDENTITY_REQUIRED |
| sessions | visitor, external_session_id, started_at | customer, traffic_source, traffic_medium, utm_source/medium/campaign/term/content, landing_page_url, exit_page_url, device_type, operating_system, browser, country, city, ended_at, duration_seconds(>=0), is_converted, source_metadata |
| website-events | session, visitor, event_type, occurred_at | customer, product, order, page_url, page_title, event_value, currency, properties. PERFORMANCE needs properties.latency_ms or loading_ms (>=0 number); EXPERIENCE needs properties.signal in RAGE_CLICK/DEAD_CLICK/QUICK_BACK |
| shipments | order, external_shipment_id, provider | customer, tracking_code, status, delivery_charge, cod_amount, currency, recipient_name, recipient_phone, delivery_address, submitted_at, picked_up_at, delivered_at, returned_at, source_metadata |
| shipment-events | shipment, event_type, occurred_at | status, description, location, metadata |
| calls | external_call_id, direction | customer, conversation, order, caller_number, receiver_number, status(default INITIATED), started_at, answered_at, ended_at, duration_seconds, recording_url(write-only), source_metadata |
| call-events | call, event_type, event_at | metadata |
| transcripts | call | transcript, summary, language, provider, confidence(0..1), generated_at, metadata |
| callbacks | customer | conversation, order, scheduled_at (must be future), status(default PENDING), reason |

Type legend for 2.3 (from the model fields): money (<=2 dp, >=0): price, discount_amount, shipping_amount, tax_amount, unit_price, amount, budget, spend, conversion_value, event_value, delivery_charge, cod_amount. Plain non-negative decimals: ctr, cpc, cpm, roas, confidence(<=1). Integers: inventory_quantity, quantity, duration_seconds, size_bytes, impressions, reach, clicks, conversions, position, website_session_id. Booleans: is_primary, is_anonymous, is_converted. Datetimes (offset required): ordered_at, paid_at, started_at, ended_at, answered_at, sent_at, delivered_at, read_at, joined_at, left_at, converted_at, first_seen_at, last_seen_at, occurred_at, event_at, submitted_at, picked_up_at, returned_at, refunded_at, generated_at, scheduled_at. Date: metric_date. JSON objects: source_metadata, metadata, attributes, properties, audience, content (creatives), location. All other fields are text (max lengths are the model CharField limits, e.g. name 255, sku 150, currency 10 but must be 3 letters).

Upsert / immutability semantics (`data.py:309-339`): a resource with a source-id upserts on (source id [+ parent/connection]); orders, refunds, messages, notes, website-events cannot be changed by re-sending (400 `IMMUTABLE_RECORD` unless the identical payload was already received); payments cannot change amount/currency (`IMMUTABLE_PAYMENT`); FK parents cannot change (`IMMUTABLE_RELATION`); a status in the body on an existing record is applied through the transition path and must be a legal move (`INVALID_TRANSITION`); `occurred_at` older than the latest stored receipt for that record -> **409** `STALE_EVENT`. Every ingest writes a `ProcessingReceipt`, a `DomainAudit` row (`INGESTED`/`UPDATED`) and, on create, a `CustomerTimelineEvent` when a customer is known.

#### 2.4 `B identity/{command}/` (all **M**; assignment item d)
Body = `IdentityCommandSerializer` (serializers/domains.py:29-52). Unknown JSON keys are silently dropped. Field types: `status` ACTIVE|INACTIVE|SUPERSEDED, `metadata` object, `observed_at` datetime, `verification` bool, `external_identity_ids` list of ints>=1, ids (`*_id`) ints>=1, `entity_type`/`identity_type` str<=50, `value` str<=500, `external_id` str<=255, `verified` bool (default false), `decision` str<=30, `reason` str<=2000. The route then requires the command's keys (else 400 `INVALID_DATA "Required fields: a, b"`, `domain_views.py:207-210`). Allowed `{command}` values and key lists (`domain_views.py:189-206`):

| command | required body keys | optional | response (HTTP 200) |
|---|---|---|---|
| canonical | entity_type, entity_id | | CanonicalIdentity row (get-or-create) |
| set-status | canonical_identity_id, status, reason | | CanonicalIdentity row; non-ACTIVE invalidates its ACTIVE links |
| register | connection_id, entity_type, identity_type, value, external_id | verified | `{"result":ExternalIdentity,"conflict":IdentityConflict or null,"status":"RESOLVED"/"CONFLICT"}` ("RESOLVED" here only means no conflict; CONFLICT = EXTERNAL_ID_COLLISION) |
| enrich-external | external_identity_id, metadata, observed_at, reason | verification | ExternalIdentity row (metadata may not contain key `verified`) |
| resolve | external_identity_id | | `{"result":IdentityLink or null,"conflict":...,"status":"RESOLVED"/"CONFLICT"/"NO_MATCH"}` |
| resolve-many | external_identity_ids (2-20 distinct ints, customer identities, >=2 distinct identifier kinds) | | `{"result":CanonicalIdentity or null,"conflict":...,"status":...}` |
| link | external_identity_id, canonical_identity_id, reason | | tuple envelope with IdentityLink; conflict MULTIPLE_CANONICAL_MATCHES |
| unlink | link_id, reason | | IdentityLink row (status INVALIDATED) |
| verify-contact | contact_id, reason | | CustomerContact row (`is_verified:true`) |
| identified-link | visitor_id, identification_event_id, external_identity_id | | WebsiteVisitor row (login/signup/form proof) |
| anonymous-link | visitor_id, purchase_event_id | | WebsiteVisitor row (purchase proof) |
| validate-links | (none) | | JSON **array** of IdentityConflict rows (type INVALID_LINK) |
| detect-merges | (none) | | JSON **array** of IdentityMergeCandidate rows (customers sharing a verified contact; products sharing a SKU) |
| review-merge | candidate_id, decision APPROVED/REJECTED, reason | | IdentityMergeCandidate row |
| execute-merge | candidate_id (must be APPROVED) | | `{"status":"BLOCKED","code":"BI_GRAPH_AI_MERGE_CONTRACT","candidate_id":n}`; never merges, logs history `MERGE_EXECUTION_BLOCKED` |
| review-conflict | conflict_id, decision REVIEWING/RESOLVED/IGNORED, reason | | IdentityConflict row |

Unknown command -> 404 `NOT_FOUND "Unknown identity command."` (after body validation). Effective GET filters per table (a key must be a real field of that model, else 400 `INVALID_FILTER`): canonical `status,entity_type`; external `entity_type`; conflicts `status,canonical_identity,external_identity`; merge-candidates `status`; history `canonical_identity,external_identity,rule_key`; links `canonical_identity,external_identity,status,rule_key`. `POST identity/merge-candidates/...` with a body is not a command; the dry-run is D24. GET tables (D13) return plain rows: CanonicalIdentity {id,business,entity_type,entity_id,canonical_key,status,created_at,updated_at}; ExternalIdentity {id,business,integration_connection,provider,entity_type,external_id,identity_type,identity_value,normalized_value,metadata,first_seen_at,last_seen_at,...}; IdentityLink {id,canonical_identity,external_identity,match_type,confidence(number or null),status,resolved_by,rule_key,linked_at,unlinked_at,metadata,created_at}; IdentityMergeCandidate {id,business,primary_identity,candidate_identity,match_reason,confidence(null for SKU),status,evidence,detected_at,resolved_at,...}; IdentityConflict {id,business,external_identity,canonical_identity,conflict_type,description,conflicting_data,severity,status,detected_at,resolved_at,resolution_note,...}; IdentityHistory `[e487b0b]` {id,business,canonical_identity,external_identity,actor,action,rule_key,evidence,created_at}. Verified from `app/models.py:882-993` and `[e487b0b]`.

#### 2.5 Merge dry-run response (D24; `merge.py:17-80`)
`{version:"bizora.merge.v1", business_id, candidate_id, source_identity_id, target_identity_id, entity_type, source_entity_id, target_entity_id, candidate_status, relationships:[{model,table,owner,paths,count,digest}], safety_errors:[{code:"CROSS_TENANT_RELATIONSHIP"/"APPROVAL_REQUIRED"/"INACTIVE_IDENTITY", model?}], dependencies:[7 owner names], fingerprint(sha256), plan_id:"merge-<24 hex>", execution:"BLOCKED", execution_reason:"BI_GRAPH_AI_MERGE_CONTRACT", limitations:[...], readiness, acknowledgements:[7 contract envelopes], executed:false}`. With no adapters configured every acknowledgement is an UNAVAILABLE envelope, so `readiness` is `WAITING_FOR_OWNERS` (or `UNSAFE` when the candidate is not APPROVED/identities inactive/cross-tenant). Other readiness values: `REJECTED`, `SIMULATED_PREPARED`, `PREPARED_REQUIRES_EXECUTOR_REVIEW`. Merge can never execute (`identity.py:308-317`).

#### 2.6 Contract owners (D26; `domain_views.py:345-346`, `boundaries.py:9-11`)
| `{owner}` slug | display owner | `{capability}` | required `payload` keys | AVAILABLE `result` must contain |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| integrations | Integrations | prepare_operation | operation, entity_type, entity_id (exactly these) with pairs SHIPMENT_SUBMIT/SHIPMENT_CANCEL->SHIPMENT, CALL_INITIATE->CALL, MESSAGE_SEND->MESSAGE | `request`(object), `execution` == "NOT_EXECUTED" |
| bi | Business Intelligence | entity_context | entity_type, entity_id | facts[], metrics[], issues[], evidence[] |
| graph | Business Graph | entity_context | entity_type, entity_id | nodes[], edges[] |
| ai | AI Intelligence | entity_context | entity_type, entity_id | recommendations[], answers[], evidence[] |
| memory | Business Memory | policy_context | query | policies[] |
| configuration | Configuration | policy | policy_key | policy{} |
| monitoring | Monitoring | freshness | resource | freshness{} |
Payload rules (`boundaries.py:55-80`): required keys non-null; keys ending `_id` = JSON int >0 (not bool); other required keys = non-empty strings <=2000 chars; `entity_type` in {CUSTOMER,PRODUCT,ORDER,PAYMENT,CAMPAIGN,SESSION,CONVERSATION,CALL,SHIPMENT,VISITOR,MESSAGE} and the entity must belong to the business; payload must be finite JSON; nesting <=20, lists <=1000. `request_id` is a correlation token only (no receipt stored). Integrations replaces the payload by the canonical prepared request. `prepare_merge` is rejected over HTTP. Because no adapter is configured, the only answer a client can currently get is the UNAVAILABLE envelope above (or a 400 error); adapter-backed shapes are documented in docs/implementations/contracts/README.md and examples.json but not reachable.

#### 2.7 The `/ops/` Django UI (reference for the new frontend; assignment item f)
Routes (`app/operations_urls.py:5`, all HTML, login required, `login_url='/ops/login/'`; membership required via `membership()` `operations_ui.py:20-23`, non-member -> Django 404):
| route | view | purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `/ops/` | index | lists the user's active memberships (business, role) |
| `/ops/login/`, `/ops/logout/`(POST) | LoginView / LogoutView | session login (section 1a) |
| `/ops/business/{id}/{section}/` | section | the sections below |
| `/ops/business/{id}/financial-timeline/` | financial_timeline | wraps D6 (filters customer, order) |
| `/ops/business/{id}/resources/{name}/` | resources | generic list of any of the 32 registry names, wraps D1 |
| `/ops/business/{id}/records/{name}/{pk}/` | detail | `load_detail`: customers -> customer360 + graph_context(CUSTOMER) + owner_context; others -> `projections.detail` + owner_context; conversations add `support_context`; customers/products/orders add `graph_context`; also detail of `conflicts`, `merge-candidates`, `actions` (M only) |
| `/ops/business/{id}/records/{name}/{pk}/{command}/` (POST) | command | executes the commands below via the same services as the API; CSRF required; redirect back on success |

`SECTIONS` has **14** keys (`operations_ui.py:18`); `identity` is shown/allowed only to MANAGER/OWNER (`:29-30` nav filter, `:112-113` 404), so VIEWER/OPERATOR see **13** (matches spec headings and TEAM_LEAD_HANDOFF "All 13 sections").
| section key | title | list resource / API equivalent | extra panels | filter inputs |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| overview | Business overview | `projections.overview` = D8 | all overview keys rendered as panels | none |
| customers | Customer 360 | `customers` list (D1); detail = D9 + D22 | | contact, contact_type, tag, q, status |
| products | Product catalog | `products` (D1) | | sku, category, q, status |
| orders | Order operations | `orders` | | customer, q, status |
| payments | Payments & refunds | `payments` | link to financial timeline | customer, order, payment_method, q(**400**, see gaps), status |
| inbox | Unified inbox | `conversations` | | channel, priority, q, status |
| queue | Support queue | D10 | | channel, priority, assigned, status |
| assignment | Conversation assignment | `conversations` | assign form on detail | as inbox |
| handover | AI / human handover | `conversations` | handover form on detail | as inbox |
| marketing | Marketing operations | `campaigns` | AiAction panel (M only, `safe_records`), `campaign-metrics`, `conversions` sections | provider (**400**: Campaign has no `provider` field), q, status |
| website | Website observed data | `visitors` | `sessions` and `website-events` sections (first 50) | none (status/search hidden) |
| delivery | Delivery operations | `shipments` | | customer, order, provider, q, status |
| calls | Call operations | `calls` | `callbacks` section | customer, order, direction, q(**400**), status |
| identity (M) | Identity review | D13 `conflicts` | `merge-candidates` and `identity_history` sections | status filter via list |
Detail-page commands (`command_forms`, `operations_ui.py:49-95`; hidden for VIEWER; ChoiceField values quoted from code) and the API call each corresponds to:
- `transition` (any resource with allowed next states): `status`(choices = states reachable from current), `reason` -> D4 with `expected_status` = current status.
- conversations: `assign`(assignee_type AGENT/TEAM/AI/UNASSIGNED, assignee_id, reason) -> D11; `handover`(target HUMAN/AI, assignee_id, reason, summary) -> D12; `priority` -> D17; `note`(content<=10000) -> D2 `notes`; `reply`(content<=10000) -> D2 `messages` (OUTGOING, AGENT, key `external_message_id="pending-ui-<token>"`, saved pending, **not sent**); `media-reply`(attachment_type IMAGE/VIDEO/AUDIO/FILE/OTHER, url, file_name?, mime_type?, size_bytes?) -> D2 `messages` with `attachments`. The page also prints agents (`user_id: username`) and teams (`id: name`) - data for which the JSON API has **no endpoint**.
- messages: `read` -> D16. products: `add-variant`(name, sku, price, inventory_quantity) -> D2 `variants`; `map-product`(connection_id, external_product_id) -> D2 `product-references`. variants: `variant`(name?, price, inventory_quantity) -> D5. payments: `refund`(amount>=0.01, reason) -> D2 `refunds` (key `external_refund_id="ui-<token>"`). calls (with customer): `callback`(reason, scheduled_at?) -> D2 `callbacks`. callbacks: `schedule`(scheduled_at) -> D23.
- actions (M): `review-action`(decision APPROVED/REJECTED, reason) -> D15. conflicts: `review-conflict`(REVIEWING/RESOLVED/IGNORED, reason) -> D14. merge-candidates: `review-merge`(APPROVED/REJECTED, reason) -> D14; `merge-plan` -> D24.
Idempotency: every ops form embeds `submission_id` (`uuid4().hex`, 32 hex chars, template tag `submission_id`) and the view sends `Idempotency-Key = "ui-" + submission_id` (`operations_ui.py:237-259`, tag at `templatetags/operations.py:17`). A new frontend should generate one UUID per user action and send it as `Idempotency-Key`.

### 3. ENUMS (quoted from code; assignment items c, d)

**3.1 Roles** - domain API accepts `VIEWER, OPERATOR, MANAGER, OWNER` (`app/domains/common.py:128`). AI action views: `OWNER, ADMIN, OPERATOR` (`app/views.py:75`). Observability `BusinessAccess.role`: `READER, MANAGER` (`app/observability/models.py:15`). Membership model default `"MEMBER"` (`app/models.py:88`), accepted by none of them.

**3.2 Lifecycle transitions** (`app/domains/states.py:2-28`; value on the left may move to the set on the right; same-status request is a no-op 200; status text is upper-cased before checking; unknown -> 400 INVALID_STATUS "Unsupported canonical status.", illegal -> 400 INVALID_TRANSITION "Invalid status transition."):
- **orders**: PENDING->{CONFIRMED,CANCELLED}; CONFIRMED->{PROCESSING,CANCELLED}; PROCESSING->{SHIPPED,CANCELLED}; SHIPPED->{DELIVERED,RETURNED}; DELIVERED->{RETURNED,REFUNDED}; RETURNED->{REFUNDED}; CANCELLED->{}; REFUNDED->{}. ->REFUNDED additionally needs completed refunds >= order total (REFUND_LEDGER_REQUIRED). Side effect: OrderStatusHistory row source `OPERATOR`.
- **payments**: PENDING->{AUTHORIZED,PAID,FAILED,CANCELLED}; AUTHORIZED->{PAID,FAILED,CANCELLED}; PAID, FAILED, CANCELLED, PARTIALLY_REFUNDED, REFUNDED -> {} (the two refund states are only set from the refund ledger). ->PAID sets `paid_at`.
- **conversations**: OPEN->{PENDING,RESOLVED,CLOSED}; PENDING->{OPEN,RESOLVED,CLOSED}; RESOLVED->{OPEN,CLOSED}; CLOSED->{OPEN}. ->RESOLVED sets `resolved_at`, any other clears it; writes ConversationEvent `STATUS`.
- **shipments**: PENDING->{SUBMITTED,CANCELLED}; SUBMITTED->{PICKED_UP,CANCELLED}; PICKED_UP->{IN_TRANSIT,RETURNING}; IN_TRANSIT->{OUT_FOR_DELIVERY,FAILED,RETURNING}; OUT_FOR_DELIVERY->{DELIVERED,FAILED,RETURNING}; FAILED->{OUT_FOR_DELIVERY,RETURNING}; RETURNING->{RETURNED}; DELIVERED, RETURNED, CANCELLED->{}. Writes a ShipmentEvent and sets submitted_at/picked_up_at/delivered_at/returned_at.
- **calls**: INITIATED->{RINGING,FAILED,CANCELLED}; RINGING->{ANSWERED,MISSED,BUSY,FAILED,CANCELLED}; ANSWERED->{COMPLETED,FAILED}; COMPLETED, MISSED, BUSY, FAILED, CANCELLED->{}. Writes a CallEvent; sets answered_at / ended_at.
- **messages** (field `delivery_status`): PENDING->{SENT,FAILED}; SENT->{DELIVERED,FAILED}; DELIVERED, FAILED->{}. Sets sent_at / delivered_at. No transport happens.
- **customers**: ACTIVE->{INACTIVE,BLOCKED}; INACTIVE->{ACTIVE,BLOCKED}; BLOCKED->{ACTIVE}.
- **products**, **variants**: ACTIVE->{INACTIVE,ARCHIVED}; INACTIVE->{ACTIVE,ARCHIVED}; ARCHIVED->{ACTIVE}.
- **campaigns**: ACTIVE->{PAUSED,COMPLETED}; PAUSED->{ACTIVE,COMPLETED}; COMPLETED->{}.
- **callbacks**: PENDING->{COMPLETED,CANCELLED}; COMPLETED, CANCELLED->{}.
- **refunds** (special case, `data.py:484-485`): PENDING->{COMPLETED,FAILED,CANCELLED}; recalculates payment status (PAID / PARTIALLY_REFUNDED / REFUNDED).
- **ads, ad-sets** status vocabulary (ingest only, no transition route): `ACTIVE, PAUSED, COMPLETED`.
- Aliases accepted (upper-cased first, `states.py:31-32`): orders `ON-HOLD`->PENDING; payments `SUCCEEDED`->PAID; calls and shipments `CANCELED`->CANCELLED. Every transition writes DomainAudit `STATUS_CHANGED` and a CustomerTimelineEvent `<RESOURCE>_<STATUS>` (e.g. `ORDERS_SHIPPED`).
- Resources with no lifecycle (transition -> 400 INVALID_STATUS): all names not listed above.

**3.3 Field vocabularies at ingest** (`data.py`): channel `MESSENGER, WHATSAPP, INSTAGRAM, TELEGRAM, WEBSITE_CHAT, OTHER`; priority `LOW, NORMAL, HIGH, URGENT` (default NORMAL); message direction / call direction `INCOMING, OUTGOING`; sender_type and participant_type `CUSTOMER, AGENT, AI, SYSTEM`; message_type `TEXT, IMAGE, VIDEO, AUDIO, FILE, LOCATION, OTHER`; delivery_status above; read_status `UNREAD, READ`; attachment_type `IMAGE, VIDEO, AUDIO, FILE, OTHER`; payment_method `CASH, COD, CARD, MOBILE_BANKING, BANK_TRANSFER, WALLET, OTHER` (aliases `CASH_ON_DELIVERY`->COD, `MOBILE_BANK`->MOBILE_BANKING; spaces/hyphens -> underscores); refund status `PENDING, COMPLETED, FAILED, CANCELLED`; conversion attribution_type `PLATFORM_REPORTED, DIRECT_SOURCE_REPORTED` (or null); website experience signals `RAGE_CLICK, DEAD_CLICK, QUICK_BACK`; website event types handled specially `PERFORMANCE, EXPERIENCE` (others free text, upper-cased; identity code recognises `PURCHASE, LOGIN, SIGNUP, FORM_SUBMISSION`); customer_type default `KNOWN` (free text, upper-cased); call default status `INITIATED`; customer tag source `MANUAL`.

**3.4 Contact / identity-type values** (`common.py:33-56`): `EMAIL` (lower-cased, validated), `PHONE` and `WHATSAPP_PHONE` (spaces/()/- stripped, `00`->`+`, Bangladesh `01[3-9]xxxxxxxx` -> `+88...`, else `+[1-9]` 8-15 digits), and verbatim ids `PSID, TELEGRAM_USER_ID, INSTAGRAM_USER_ID, CLIENT_ID, EXTERNAL_CUSTOMER_ID, EXTERNAL_PRODUCT_ID, EXTERNAL_ORDER_ID, EXTERNAL_PAYMENT_ID, EXTERNAL_CAMPAIGN_ID, EXTERNAL_SESSION_ID, EXTERNAL_CONVERSATION_ID, EXTERNAL_CALL_ID, EXTERNAL_SHIPMENT_ID, SKU`. Anything else -> 400 INVALID_CONTACT.

**3.5 Operations**: assignee_type `AGENT, TEAM, AI, UNASSIGNED`; ConversationAssignment.status `ACTIVE, RELEASED`; `ai_handover_state` `AI, HUMAN` or null; handover target `HUMAN, AI`; ConversationEvent.event_type `STATUS, ASSIGNMENT, HANDOVER, PRIORITY`; action review decision `APPROVED, REJECTED` (AiAction must be `SUGGESTED`); outbound operations `SHIPMENT_SUBMIT, SHIPMENT_CANCEL, CALL_INITIATE, MESSAGE_SEND` (prepare gates: shipment submit needs status PENDING + recipient_phone + delivery_address; cancel needs PENDING/SUBMITTED; call needs OUTGOING + INITIATED + receiver_number; message needs OUTGOING + delivery_status PENDING); queue `assigned` `me, unassigned, team`; graph-context kinds / canonical entity types `CUSTOMER, PRODUCT, ORDER, PAYMENT, CAMPAIGN, SESSION, CONVERSATION, CALL, SHIPMENT, VISITOR` (`identity.py:7-11`; `MESSAGE` additionally in contract payloads); product assessment decision `REUSE, CREATE, REVIEW`, rules `SOURCE_PRODUCT_REFERENCE, REVIEWED_IDENTITY_LINK, AUTHORIZED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT, SHARED_SKU_REVIEW, NO_DETERMINISTIC_MATCH`.

**3.6 Identity**: CanonicalIdentity.status `ACTIVE, INACTIVE, SUPERSEDED` settable (`MERGED` exists in code only as a protected value); IdentityLink.status `ACTIVE, INVALIDATED, SUPERSEDED`, match_type `MANUAL, EXACT, ANONYMOUS_TO_KNOWN, DETERMINISTIC`, resolved_by `MANUAL, SYSTEM`, confidence number 0-1 or null (null for MANUAL); rule_key values `AUTHORIZED_MANUAL, SOURCE_CUSTOMER_REFERENCE, VERIFIED_EXACT_CONTACT, SOURCE_PRODUCT_REFERENCE, EXPLICIT_VERIFIED_SKU_V1, SOURCE_ORDER_REFERENCE, SOURCE_ENTITY_REFERENCE, PURCHASE_ORDER_VISITOR, VERIFIED_IDENTIFIER_VISITOR, MULTI_FIELD_CORROBORATION, INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE, IDENTITY_UNAVAILABLE`; merge candidate status `PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED`, match_reason `SHARED_VERIFIED_CONTACT, SHARED_SKU_REVIEW`; conflict_type `EXTERNAL_ID_COLLISION`(CRITICAL), `INVALID_LINK`(CRITICAL), `MULTIPLE_MATCHES`(MEDIUM), `MULTIPLE_CANONICAL_MATCHES`, `CONTRADICTORY_IDENTIFIERS` (both HIGH); conflict status `OPEN, REVIEWING, RESOLVED, IGNORED`; IdentityHistory.action `REGISTERED, LINKED, UNLINKED, SUPERSEDED, CONFLICT_DETECTED, CONFLICT_REVIEWING, CONFLICT_RESOLVED, CONFLICT_IGNORED, NO_MATCH, CONTACT_VERIFIED, ANONYMOUS_TO_KNOWN, MERGE_CANDIDATE, MERGE_APPROVED, MERGE_REJECTED, MERGE_EXECUTION_BLOCKED, STATUS_CHANGED, EXTERNAL_ENRICHED, MULTI_FIELD_MATCH`.

**3.7 Contracts**: version `bizora.domain.v1`, merge `bizora.merge.v1`, outbound `bizora.outbound.v1`; contract status `AVAILABLE, REJECTED, UNAVAILABLE`; execution `NOT_EXECUTED`; merge readiness `UNSAFE, REJECTED, SIMULATED_PREPARED, PREPARED_REQUIRES_EXECUTOR_REVIEW, WAITING_FOR_OWNERS`; merge ack decision `PREPARED, REJECTED`; merge `execution` `BLOCKED`.

**3.8 Audit / timeline** (visible in `overview.recent_activity`, detail `history`): DomainAudit.action `INGESTED, UPDATED, STATUS_CHANGED, VARIANT_UPDATED, ASSIGNED, HANDOVER, PRIORITY_CHANGED, MESSAGE_READ, CALLBACK_SCHEDULED, ACTION_APPROVED, ACTION_REJECTED`; customer timeline event_type `<RESOURCE>_CREATED` (e.g. `ORDERS_CREATED`, `WEBSITE-EVENTS_CREATED`) and `<RESOURCE>_<STATUS>`.

**3.9 Error codes -> HTTP** (`domain_views.py:36-37`): 403 `FORBIDDEN`; 404 `NOT_FOUND`; 409 `STALE_EVENT, STALE_STATE, IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT, HANDOVER_CONFLICT` and (separately) `CONSTRAINT_CONFLICT`; everything else 400. Codes found in the domain layer: INVALID_DATA, UNKNOWN_FIELD, INVALID_FILTER, INVALID_KEY, INVALID_RELATION, INVALID_STATUS, INVALID_TRANSITION, INVALID_MONEY, INVALID_TIME, INVALID_CURRENCY, INVALID_PAYMENT_METHOD, INVALID_IDENTIFIER, INVALID_CONTACT, INVALID_REFERENCE, INVALID_ORDER, INVALID_REFUND, EXCESSIVE_REFUND, REFUND_LEDGER_REQUIRED, CURRENCY_MISMATCH, IMMUTABLE_RECORD, IMMUTABLE_PAYMENT, IMMUTABLE_RELATION, PRODUCT_REVIEW_REQUIRED, DUPLICATE_VARIANT, INVALID_IMAGE, INVALID_ATTACHMENT, INVALID_LOCATION, INVALID_MESSAGE, INVALID_SIGNAL, IDENTITY_REQUIRED, ATTRIBUTION_OWNER_REQUIRED, CONNECTION_UNAVAILABLE, INVALID_SOURCE, INVALID_ASSIGNEE, INVALID_HANDOVER, INVALID_PRIORITY, INVALID_CALLBACK, INVALID_REVIEW, EVIDENCE_REQUIRED, INVALID_OPERATION, INVALID_SHIPMENT, INVALID_CALL, INVALID_ENTITY, INVALID_METADATA, INVALID_EVIDENCE, INVALID_MERGE, INVALID_ACK, STALE_PLAN, APPROVAL_REQUIRED, INACTIVE_IDENTITY, IDENTITY_CONFLICT, MERGE_CONTRACT_REQUIRED, INVALID_CONTRACT, UNSUPPORTED_CONTRACT, CONTRACT_MISMATCH, INVALID_ADAPTER, INVALID_DIRECTION, UNSUPPORTED_MOCK_EVENT.

### 4. SPEC COVERAGE TABLE (headings only)
Labels: **ENDPOINT** = reachable HTTP route in this scope (LIVE per app/urls.py, subject to H1); **ENDPOINT-PARTIAL** = some sub-features have a route, the rest are named in Notes; **SERVICE-ONLY** = Python code, no route; **ABSENT** = `NO ENDPOINT FOUND`. D# / P# refer to section 2.

| Spec | Heading | Coverage | Endpoint(s) / service | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| business-data | 1 Product | ENDPOINT-PARTIAL | D1-D3 `products,variants,product-images,product-references`; D4; D5; D25 | no edit of product fields (no PATCH); provider-payload normalization is SERVICE-ONLY (`mock_transport.MockCommerceTransport`); `source_metadata` write-only |
| business-data | 2 Customer | ENDPOINT-PARTIAL | D1-D4 `customers,contacts,tags,customer-references`; D9 (timeline section) | no profile edit; no standalone timeline list; verification only via D14 `verify-contact` |
| business-data | 3 Order | ENDPOINT | D1-D4 `orders`; items/history in D3 | orders immutable once created (status via D4 only); no item-level routes |
| business-data | 4 Payment | ENDPOINT | D1-D4 `payments,refunds`; D6 | refund states derive from the ledger |
| business-data | 5 Communication | ENDPOINT-PARTIAL | D1-D4 `messages,attachments`; D16; D21 `message_send` | incoming/outgoing provider processing and connectors: SERVICE-ONLY mocks (`MockMessageTransport`, `MockCanonicalTransport`), none live |
| business-data | 6 Customer Support | ENDPOINT | D1-D4 `conversations,participants,notes`; D10, D11, D12, D17, D18 | AI reply is an UNAVAILABLE placeholder in D18 |
| business-data | 7 Marketing | ENDPOINT-PARTIAL | D1-D4 `campaigns,campaign-metrics,conversions,ad-accounts,ad-sets,creatives,ads`; D20 | attribution limited to platform/source-reported; no platform connectors |
| business-data | 8 Website Behavior | ENDPOINT-PARTIAL | D1-D3 `visitors,sessions,website-events`; D19; D7; D14 `identified-link,anonymous-link` | analytics integration ABSENT; funnel/abandonment metrics UNAVAILABLE |
| business-data | 9 Courier | ENDPOINT-PARTIAL | D1-D4 `shipments,shipment-events`; D21 `shipment_submit,shipment_cancel` | submission/tracking are prepare-only (`NOT_EXECUTED`); no courier connectors |
| business-data | 10 Calling | ENDPOINT-PARTIAL | D1-D4 `calls,call-events,transcripts,callbacks`; D21 `call_initiate`; D23 | recording URL never exposed, `recording_access` UNAVAILABLE; summary is a stored field only |
| identity-resulation | 1 Canonical Identity | ENDPOINT | D13/D14 `canonical`, `set-status` | M |
| identity-resulation | 2 External Identity | ENDPOINT | D13 `external`; D14 `register`, `enrich-external` | M |

### 5. FRONTEND NOTES

1. **Deploy same-origin.** Serve the SPA and `/api/` from one origin (proxy in dev). Bootstrap: `GET /ops/login/` (sets `csrftoken` because the template renders `{% csrf_token %}`), then `POST /ops/login/` as `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` with `username`, `password`, `csrfmiddlewaretoken` (cookie value), `next`; on success the response is a 302 and `sessionid` is set. Then send `X-CSRFToken: <csrftoken cookie>` on every POST. Logout is `POST /ops/logout/` (CSRF). A 403 `{"detail": ...}` means not logged in / CSRF; a 403 `{"code":"FORBIDDEN",...}` means logged in but role too low.
2. **There is no discovery endpoint**: no "me", no list of the user's businesses, no role echo, no teams, no agents (for assignment pickers), no connections list, no user list. The `/ops/` index and the assign form read these directly from the DB. The SPA must be told `business_id` out of band and can only infer a role from 403s. `GET B overview/` returns the business name/currency/timezone once the id is known.
3. **Pagination** is DRF PageNumber on lists: `?page=`, `?page_size=` (max 100, default 25), body `{count,next,previous,results}`, `next`/`previous` are absolute URLs. A page beyond the end returns 404 `{"detail":"Invalid page."}` `[LIB]`. Detail, 360, overview, context payloads are NOT paginated: they carry capped `section`s (`results` <=50, `count` is the true total, `truncated` flag) and the UI must fetch the child list route (e.g. `messages/?conversation={id}&ordering=oldest`) for more.
4. **Ordering/filtering**: only `ordering=newest|oldest`; every filter is a single exact value; resource lists, support-queue, identity GETs, financial-timeline and `conversations/{pk}/context` reject unknown query keys with 400, while `visitors/{pk}/journey`, 360, overview, graph-context and retargeting ignore them. Search `q` is per-resource and returns 400 for 18 resources (section 2.1). `since`/`until` need an ISO timestamp with offset; a literal `+` in a query string becomes a space, so send `Z` or `%2B`. Boolean filter `is_anonymous` is passed to the ORM as a string; use `True`/`False` (or `1`/`0`), lowercase `true` is a Django validation error -> 400 `INVALID_DATA` `[LIB: BooleanField.to_python]`.
5. **Idempotency**: every `POST B{name}/` needs an `Idempotency-Key` header (<=180 chars). Same key + same body -> 200 with the existing record (a UI retry is safe); same key + changed body -> 409 `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`; the receipt is scoped to (business, connection or "manual"), so a key may not be reused for another resource with different data. Generate one UUID per user action (the ops UI does `ui-<uuid4 hex>`). Other POST commands carry no key; they are state-idempotent (same assignment, same priority, same handover target, repeated review decision, same transition target return the current object with 200).
6. **POST is create-or-update, not create.** 201 = new, 200 = replay/existing/updated. There is no PATCH/PUT/DELETE (405). To change data: state moves -> `transition`; variants -> `variants/{pk}/update/`; conversations -> assign/handover/priority; callbacks -> schedule; message read; everything else only by re-posting with the same source id (needs `connection_id` + a source reference for products/customers). Editing a product's name/price or a customer's name from a UI has no direct command (gap).
7. **Concurrency tokens**: `transition.expected_status` (409 STALE_STATE), `variants/update.expected_updated_at` (exact equality with the row's `updated_at`, 409), `dry-run.expected_fingerprint` (400 STALE_PLAN). Use them to implement "record changed, reload".
8. **Everything is synchronous and in-request.** No jobs, polling or webhooks. Writes take a per-business row lock (`select_for_update`). Nothing is sent to a provider: transitions only record state; `reply`/`media-reply` in the ops UI just save a PENDING outgoing message; `operations/.../prepare/` returns `execution:"NOT_EXECUTED"`; action review returns `execution: UNAVAILABLE`; merge never executes. The UI must label these as "recorded", not "sent".
9. **Decimals** come back as JSON numbers `[LIB]`; send money as strings (`"12.50"`). Percent-like decimals (`ctr,cpc,cpm,roas,confidence`) are plain non-negative numbers.
10. **Customer message text is wrapped**: for `sender_type == CUSTOMER`, stored `content` is `"<untrusted_customer_data>\n...\n</untrusted_customer_data>"` (`app/models.py:530-531`, applied on save, also in D2 responses and `search`). Strip the wrapper for display and never render it as HTML.
11. **Never returned**: `source_metadata`, `config`, `recording_url` (`projections.py:12`). Calls expose `recording_access: UNAVAILABLE`; recordings and raw provider data cannot be shown.
12. **Foreign keys are ints under the field name** (`order: 7`, `customer: 3`, `business: 1`), not nested objects; D3 expands customer/order/conversation/product once under the same key. Link by resource name = field name pluralised as in the ops `relation_link` map (`templatetags/operations.py`: customer->customers, order->orders, product->products, product_variant->variants, payment->payments, conversation->conversations, campaign->campaigns, session->sessions, visitor->visitors, shipment->shipments, call->calls).
13. **UNAVAILABLE objects are normal data**, shape `{"status":"UNAVAILABLE","owner":"<domain>","reason":"No authoritative provider contract is registered."}`. They appear in: overview `business_health`, `intelligence.{ai_answers,attribution_analysis,freshness}`, `website-performance` (7 keys), `support-queue.capabilities`, `conversations/{pk}/context` (`policy_context,suggested_reply`), `retargeting-context.audience_recommendation`, `graph-context.generation`, calls `recording_access`, `actions/{pk}/review.execution`, every contract call. Render an empty state with `owner`, not an error. Graph-context also carries `status: "UNAVAILABLE"` when no graph node exists.
14. **Error handling matrix**: 404 NOT_FOUND = business invisible to you / unknown resource name / missing record on detail+transition. 400 INVALID_RELATION = a referenced id (path pk on most commands or body FK) is missing or belongs to another business. 403 FORBIDDEN = role. 409 = reload and retry. Serializer failures are `{field:[msgs]}`; domain failures are `{code,detail}`; both must be parsed. Show `detail` for domain errors; switch on `code`.
15. **Role-aware UI** (mirror `operations_ui.context`, `:29-30`): `can_write = role != VIEWER`; `can_manage = role in (MANAGER, OWNER)`. Hide identity screens, action review, merge dry-run and `deduplication` for VIEWER/OPERATOR; hide all write controls for VIEWER. Role strings are exactly `VIEWER, OPERATOR, MANAGER, OWNER`.
16. **Customer tag case**: nested `customers.tags[]` are stored UPPER and `?tag=` is upper-cased server side, but `POST tags/` stores the tag as sent. Always post upper-case tags, or filters will miss them.
17. **Financial timeline** rows carry `entity_kind` (`PAYMENT`/`REFUND`); `id` repeats across kinds, so key rows by `(entity_kind,id)`. Link to payment or refund detail accordingly.
18. **Prepare-operation URL** uses underscore names (`/operations/shipment_submit/12/prepare/`); hyphens fail with INVALID_OPERATION. Send an empty JSON body. `graph-context/{kind}` accepts lowercase.
19. **Support queue** is a sub-view of conversations restricted to OPEN/PENDING; `assigned=team` needs the caller to be a member of a SupportTeam; ordering is fixed (priority rank, oldest first), no `ordering` param.
20. **Naive datetimes**: inside `data` they are rejected (`INVALID_TIME`); in the envelope `occurred_at`, `scheduled_at`, `observed_at`, `expected_updated_at` DRF treats a missing offset as UTC `[LIB]`. Always send an offset.
21. **No schema endpoint** (grep for openapi/swagger/spectacular finds nothing). This document is the contract.

### 6. GAPS AND AMBIGUITIES

**Blocking / structural**
- G1 (=H1) 11 model classes missing at HEAD; `app/domains/registry.py`, `domain_views.py`, `app/urls.py`, `Campaign.ad_account` cannot load. All "LIVE" statuses above are conditional on restoring the `e487b0b` model block (and its `BusinessMembership` definition) in a merge. Which BusinessMembership shape wins (domain: choices VIEWER..OWNER, FK PROTECT, no created_at; AI: default MEMBER, related_name `memberships`, db_table `business_memberships`, created_at) is unresolved; the domain API only works with the former's role strings.
- G2 (=H2) three migration lineages, no merge migration; two `CreateModel('BusinessMembership')`. NOT VERIFIED by running `migrate`.
- G3 Known: `settings.py` lacks `import sys`/`import copy` (uses them at `:183,:194`); `app/admin.py` lacks `admin` import; `app/models.py` lacks `ValidationError` (raised at `app/models.py:1252,1464,1510` and in AiAction.clean) and `re`.
- G4 `docker-compose.yml:12-14` supplies `POSTGRES_HOST` but `settings.py:112-113` only switches to PostgreSQL when `BIZORA_DATABASE=postgresql`; default is the tracked `db.sqlite3` (docs forbid migrating it). Compose therefore silently uses SQLite unless `.env` sets `BIZORA_DATABASE` (`.env` not read here).
- G5 No endpoint for: current user / my businesses / role; teams and agents lists; memberships; integration connections or sources (domain scope); audit/receipt browsing; stand-alone order-items, customer-timeline, conversation-events lists; any DELETE or PATCH; OpenAPI. Each is `NO ENDPOINT FOUND`.
- G6 `BIZORA_DOMAIN_ADAPTERS` is defined nowhere, so all 7 contract owners and all 7 merge acknowledgements are permanently UNAVAILABLE; the contract UI cannot show data until BI/Graph/AI/Memory/Configuration/Monitoring/Integrations ship adapters.

**Auth / platform**
- G7 DRF default authentication (Session, Basic) and the 403-not-401 behaviour are derived from DRF 3.15.2 defaults, not from repo code (library absent) - NOT VERIFIED by execution. `settings.py` has no `DEFAULT_AUTHENTICATION_CLASSES`; the explicit pair exists only in `bizora/local_settings.py:20-23`.
- G8 No JSON login/logout, no CSRF bootstrap endpoint, no CORS, no token auth (section 1a). `LOGIN_URL` unset. `/admin/login/` requires `is_staff`.
- G9 Superuser bypass is inconsistent: domain API none (`common.py:123-130`, test `test_domains.py:616-620`); AI views bypass (`services/authorization.py:10`).
- G10 Role vocabularies differ across the AI views (`OWNER/ADMIN/OPERATOR`), domain (`OWNER/MANAGER/OPERATOR/VIEWER`) and observability (`READER/MANAGER`). Non-member status also differs: domain 404 `{code,detail}`, AI views 403 `{"detail"}` / `{"error"}` shapes.
- G11 `bizora/urls.py` mounts `admin/` twice and `app.urls` twice (`/api/`, `/api/v1/`); URL names (`message`, `action-approve`, ...) are therefore duplicated; `reverse()` picks one mount. Harmless for HTTP clients.

**Behaviour surprises (verified in code)**
- G12 `q` search returns 400 INVALID_FILTER "Search is unsupported for this resource." for payments, calls and 16 other resources, yet the ops UI shows a search box for `payments` and `calls` (`show_search` at `operations_ui.py:133`, rendered by `templates/operations/page.html`), so the old UI errors on search there. The ops `campaigns` filter `provider` (`operations_ui.py:25`) is also rejected (Campaign has `platform`, and `platform` is not an allowed filter).
- G13 `tags` created via `POST tags/` keep the sent case but `?tag=` upper-cases (section 5.16).
- G14 Missing/foreign related ids give 400 INVALID_RELATION, not 404, on 360/context/commands (section 1d). The cross-business test expects 404 only for the business segment.
- G15 `ProductAssessmentView` accepts but ignores `occurred_at`; contract `UNSUPPORTED_CONTRACT` message "Use the scoped merge dry-run..." is also returned for an unknown owner slug (misleading); `OutboundPreparationView` echoes the operation in `capability` (not the contract capability `prepare_operation`).
- G16 Handover to HUMAN requires an agent `assignee_id` in practice (assign rejects `null` agent) although the serializer marks it optional.
- G17 `creatives` `q` runs `content__icontains` on a JSONField - NOT VERIFIED whether the DB backend accepts it (SQLite and PostgreSQL behaviour not tested).
- G18 `Idempotency-Key` digest covers resource, `data`, `occurred_at` but not `connection_id` (the key scope covers it). Key reuse with a different `connection_id` is therefore a different receipt (no conflict).
- G19 Product SKU intake: re-posting an existing SKU without a source mapping returns 400 `PRODUCT_REVIEW_REQUIRED` (no create); a distinct product needs MANAGER/OWNER with `deduplication`. A normal "add product" form for operators will hit this on duplicate SKUs.
- G20 `source_metadata` can be written but never read; if the UI needs provider diagnostics, no endpoint supplies them (docs: "safe public shipment diagnostic contract" is an open decision, TEAM_LEAD_HANDOFF.md closeout table).

**Docs vs code**
- X-1 docs/implementations/contracts/README.md:34 "HTTP error shape is {code, detail}; validation 400, permission 403, inaccessible business 404" - code also returns DRF field-keyed 400 bodies, `{detail}` auth/CSRF/405/page errors, and 409 for STALE_EVENT/STALE_STATE/IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT/HANDOVER_CONFLICT/CONSTRAINT_CONFLICT.
- X-2 contracts/README.md:65 and TEAM_LEAD_HANDOFF.md "Basic auth is supported" - true only through DRF defaults; the settings module actually used (`bizora.settings`) never states it.
- X-3 TEAM_LEAD_HANDOFF.md closeout ("245 tests", "fresh migrations through 0008", "VIEWER/OPERATOR/MANAGER/OWNER roles", "thirteen operational section routes agree") describes branch `feat/business-domains`; at 5f176a2 the model block it relies on is gone (H1) and the migration graph forks (H2). Section routes themselves do agree: 13 spec headings = 13 non-identity `SECTIONS` keys, plus manager-only `identity`.
- X-4 contracts/README.md:114-119 describes `POST operations/{operation}/{id}/prepare/` without noting that `{operation}` must use underscores (`SHIPMENT_SUBMIT`, any case).
- X-5 TEAM_LEAD_HANDOFF.md: "the future frontend can use the same API contract" for owner context - true for the route, but with no adapters the response is always UNAVAILABLE (G6).
- X-6 Feature specs name capabilities the domain API deliberately returns as UNAVAILABLE (BI analytics, AI replies/summaries, SLA/overdue, recording access, courier/regional performance); these are not defects of this scope but the frontend must plan empty states.
- X-7 docs/implementations/contracts/examples.json matches the code's UNAVAILABLE envelope and capability/owner names (checked).

**NOT VERIFIED (needs a running stack or library source)**: actual HTTP status for unauthenticated requests; exact DRF pagination link format; JSON encoding of Decimal; CSRF cookie issuance details; `creatives` search; behaviour when the project is repaired. Nothing was executed.
