# Part B — AI Intelligence and Business Memory & Knowledge

## SCAN B: AI Intelligence + Business Memory & Knowledge (bizora @ 5f176a2, read-only)

Legend: all paths `path:line` are repo-relative to <repo>. Nothing was executed; DRF/Django library
behaviour was read from the DRF 3.15.2 / Django 4.2.27 sources in the scratchpad venv (`.../scratchpad/venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages`),
cited as `DRF:<file>` / `DJ:<file>`. Status: LIVE = declared in app/urls.py (reachable once the checkout can import, see GAP-1); UNMOUNTED = urlconf exists but not included; SERVICE-ONLY = no HTTP route.
Prefix: every LIVE route below exists at BOTH `/api/...` and `/api/v1/...` (bizora/urls.py:22,25). Paths below are shown relative to `/api/`.

### 1. SCOPE

AI Intelligence has a real, LIVE HTTP surface in app/views.py + app/urls.py:22-83 (12 business-scoped AI/issue routes, 3 action-lifecycle routes, 1 health route),
plus one second action-review route in the domain layer (app/urls.py:97 -> app/domain_views.py:227) and read-only/stub adjuncts (overview intelligence section, contract stubs).
Recommendation and action CREATION has no HTTP route (service-only). Business Memory & Knowledge (profile, policies, product knowledge, delivery/return policy,
brand tone, support knowledge, goals, historical decisions, approved suggestions, knowledge sources, ingestion, processing, chunking, embeddings, vector/keyword/hybrid
search, versioning) has NO HTTP ENDPOINT FOUND in the LIVE urlconf; it exists only as service functions (section 2E). The only HTTP surfaces that touch KnowledgeSource rows
are UNMOUNTED Business Configuration views (section 2F) and a LIVE contract stub that always answers UNAVAILABLE (E18).

**BLOCKER found in addition to ground-truth #3 (verified with git, see GAP-1):** app/models.py at HEAD does not define `BusinessMemory`, `HistoricalDecision`, `ApprovedSuggestion`
(nor `SupportTeam, ProcessingReceipt, DomainAudit, ConversationEvent, IdentityHistory, CallbackRequest, AdAccount, CampaignAdSet, CampaignCreative, CampaignAd, ProductImage`).
`app/services/ai_context.py:21-30` and `app/services/domain_memory.py:17-25` import the first three from app.models, and app/views.py:36 imports app.services.ai, which imports both
(`ai.py:26`, `ai.py:466-475`). So even after fixing settings/admin/models imports, importing app.urls raises ImportError and EVERY /api route (including the health route) is dead until models.py is repaired.

### 2. ENDPOINT TABLE

Common blocks (referenced by the table):
- **AUTH-BIZ** (all `businesses/{business_id}/ai/*` and `businesses/{business_id}/issues/`): `BusinessScopedAPIView.initial` (app/views.py:50-57). Order: DRF authentication + `IsAuthenticated`
  (each view sets `permission_classes=(IsAuthenticated,)`; default authenticators are Session+Basic since settings has no DEFAULT_AUTHENTICATION_CLASSES, bizora/settings.py:168-180) -> unauthenticated gives HTTP 403
  `{"detail":"Authentication credentials were not provided."}` (first authenticator SessionAuthentication has no WWW-Authenticate header, DRF:views.py:455-461) ->
  `get_object_or_404(Business, pk)` -> 404 `{"detail":"No Business matches the given query."}` (DJ:shortcuts.py:88) -> `require_business_access(user, business)` with NO roles
  (app/services/authorization.py:6-16): superuser passes; otherwise needs an ACTIVE `BusinessMembership` of ANY role string; else Django PermissionDenied which DRF renders as 403
  `{"detail":"Business access is required."}` (DRF:views.py exception_handler passes exc.args). Business existence is therefore distinguishable (404 vs 403).
- **AUTH-ACT** (action routes): `IsAuthenticated` + membership with `role in {"OWNER","ADMIN","OPERATOR"}` (or superuser) on `action.business` (views.py:75,102,120; authorization.py:15). Business is NOT in the URL.
- **ERR-DRF400**: invalid body -> 400 with DRF field-error dict `{"<field>": ["This field is required."|"This field may not be blank."|"Ensure this field has no more than N characters."|"A valid integer is required."]}` (DRF:fields.py:292,725-727,889).
- **ERR-404**: `{"detail":"No <Model> matches the given query."}` (Conversation, Order, Campaign, BusinessIssue, Call, AiAction). Entities are always looked up with `business=business`, so another tenant's id gives 404, not 403.
- Query params: NONE of the AI views read `request.query_params`; any query string is silently ignored. No pagination class is configured (settings has no DEFAULT_PAGINATION_CLASS).
- All success responses are JSON, HTTP 200 unless noted. Methods not listed give 405 `{"detail":"Method \"X\" not allowed."}` (DRF:exceptions.py:196; test app/test_ai_endpoint_matrix.py:196-199).

| E11 | GET | `businesses/{business_id}/ai/actions/` | LIVE | AUTH-BIZ | none; no pagination; `ORDER BY created_at DESC` | - | bare JSON array of action objects (2B-E11) | AUTH-BIZ | views.py:138-143; serializers/action.py:10-46 |
| E12 | GET | `businesses/{business_id}/issues/` | LIVE | AUTH-BIZ | none; no pagination; UNORDERED; only `status="OPEN"` rows | - | bare JSON array of BusinessIssue objects (2B-E12) | AUTH-BIZ | views.py:146-152; serializers/action.py:63-83 |
| E13 | POST | `actions/{pk}/approve/` | LIVE | AUTH-ACT | - | `approval_note` str 3-1000 req | AiAction object (2B-E11 shape) | 400 DRF dict; 403 `{"error":"Business access required."}`; 404 `{"detail":"No AiAction matches the given query."}`; 409 `{"error":"<str(ValidationError)>"}` | views.py:68-93; services/actions.py:41-66; serializers/action.py:6-7 |
| E14 | POST | `actions/{pk}/reject/` | LIVE | AUTH-ACT | - | `approval_note` str 3-1000 req (same serializer) | AiAction object | 403 `{"error":"Business access required."}` (checked BEFORE body validation); 400 DRF dict; 404; 409 `{"error":...}` | views.py:96-111; services/actions.py:69-85 |
| E15 | POST | `actions/{pk}/execute/` | LIVE | AUTH-ACT | - | none (body ignored) | AiAction object (status EXECUTED or FAILED) | 403 `{"error":"Business operator access required."}`; 404; 409 `{"error":...}` | views.py:114-126; services/actions.py:88-140 |
| E16 | POST | `businesses/{business_id}/actions/{pk}/review/` | LIVE (domain layer, second approval route) | `DomainAPIView.business(manager=True)`: active membership role in {OWNER, MANAGER} and active user; no superuser bypass | - | `decision` str<=30 req in {APPROVED,REJECTED}; `reason` str 1-2000 req (+ other IdentityCommandSerializer fields are accepted and ignored) | `{"action": <all AiAction columns>, "execution": {"status":"UNAVAILABLE","owner":"AI / Integration execution contract","reason":"No authoritative provider contract is registered."}}` | `{code,detail}`: 404 NOT_FOUND "Business not found."; 403 FORBIDDEN; 400 INVALID_REVIEW / INVALID_RELATION / EVIDENCE_REQUIRED / INVALID_TRANSITION; plus DRF 400 dict | urls.py:97; domain_views.py:24-45,227-233; domains/operations.py:70-86; domains/contracts.py:32-46; domains/common.py:123-130 |

| E18 | POST | `businesses/{business_id}/contracts/{owner}/{capability}/` with owner=`ai`,cap=`entity_context` or owner=`memory`,cap=`policy_context` | LIVE but ALWAYS `UNAVAILABLE` (no adapter configured anywhere) | DomainAPIView read (write only for owner=integrations) | - | EXACTLY keys `request_id` (str 1-180) and `payload` (ai: `entity_type`,`entity_id`; memory: `query`) | `{version,business_id,owner,capability,request_id,status:"UNAVAILABLE",result:null,simulated:false,reason:"No owner adapter is configured."}` | `{code,detail}` 400 INVALID_CONTRACT etc. | urls.py:122; domain_views.py:344-353; domains/boundaries.py:9-11,55-92 |
| E19 | GET | `businesses/{business_id}/conversations/{pk}/context/?order={id}` | LIVE (adjunct) | DomainAPIView read | only `order` | - | includes literal stubs `policy_context: {"status":"UNAVAILABLE","owner":"Business Memory & Knowledge",...}` and `suggested_reply: {"status":"UNAVAILABLE","owner":"AI Intelligence",...}` | `{code,detail}` | urls.py:107; domain_views.py:282-286; domains/projections.py:153-165; domains/operations.py:89-90 |

Routes that do NOT exist (verified by reading app/urls.py in full): no AI conversation list/detail/message-history route; no recommendation detail; no action detail; no
route to create/generate a recommendation or action; no recommendation approve/reject; no issue detail/acknowledge/resolve in the AI module; no knowledge or memory route of any kind.
`GET businesses/{id}/actions/` is NOT the AI actions list; it falls into the generic `ResourceList` (urls.py:100) and returns 404 `{"code":"NOT_FOUND","detail":"Unknown resource."}` because "actions" is not in app/domains/registry.py:5-38.
Server-rendered HTML operator UI exists at `/ops/...` (app/operations_urls.py, app/operations_ui.py); it is HTML/session based and not a JSON API for the frontend.

#### 2B. ENDPOINT DETAIL (fields derived from the code each view executes)

**E1 assistant query.** Validation: `query` CharField(max 2000, required; blank rejected), `conversation_id` IntegerField(optional, null ok), `intent` CharField(max 100, optional, default `business_performance_inquiry`) (serializers/ai.py:14-17).
Behaviour (ai_assistant.py): intent resolution `classify_user_intent` (16-31): if a non-default intent is sent it is used verbatim; if default, keyword routing to `refund_and_cancellation_inquiry | delivery_performance_inquiry | marketing_performance_inquiry | product_inventory_inquiry | customer_behavior_inquiry`, else `business_performance_inquiry`.
Conversation: if `conversation_id` matches an AiConversation of this business with `conversation_type="BUSINESS_ASSISTANT"` AND `status="ACTIVE"` it is reused; **otherwise a NEW conversation is silently created** and its id returned (54-76). Writes a USER AiMessage, then calls the LLM, then writes an ASSISTANT AiMessage with a JSON snapshot of the context (79-146); last 6 prior messages are replayed as history (87-103); sets `conversation.updated_at`.
Context = `assemble_full_ai_context(business, intent)` only (106-108; the view never passes customer/order/product): up to 8 active VERIFIED facts + policies/memory items + up to 5 OPEN issues.
Response keys (152-160): `conversation_id:int`, `message_id:int` (assistant message), `answer:str`, `grounding_facts:[{id,fact_type,statement,confidence:"1.0000"(string),valid_from:iso|null,metadata:{},supporting_evidence:[{evidence_type,description,entity_type,entity_id}<=5]}]<=8` (ai_context.py:34-80),
`open_issues:[{id,issue_type,title,severity}]<=5` (ai_context.py:514-518), `tokens_used:int`, `model_name:str`.
Sync, one blocking LLM HTTP call with 15 s timeout (ai_assistant.py:124-131; models.py:49-78). **LLM provider is read from env `BIZORA_LLM_URL`; if unset the answer is the canned fallback "There is not enough verified evidence to answer this question. Retrieved N active facts and M open issues." and `tokens_used` is the canned 180**; `model_name` is the hard-coded default `"gemini-1.5-pro"` (ai_assistant.py:40,159) regardless of provider. `provider_status` ("ok"|"unavailable"|"error") is stored in AiMessage.response_metadata (145) but NOT returned.
`grounding_facts` is the top-8 fact list by confidence, NOT per-claim citations.

**E2 suggest-reply.** Reply text is a draft only; nothing is sent to the customer. Order must have `customer_id == conversation.customer_id` (views.py:190-196). Context: customer context + order context + `assemble_memory_context(business, intent)` + last 6 messages + `source_metadata.human_agent_notes` (ai_support.py:36-83). LLM call 15 s with template fallback "Hello {name}, thank you for contacting {business}...". Response (100-107): `conversation_id`, `customer_id:int|null`, `suggested_reply:str`, `intent:str` (echo), `applied_policies:[str]` (titles from assemble_memory_context: policy docs, BusinessMemory titles, plus "Approved Recommendation: ...", "Executed Decision: ...", "Business Goal: ..." entries (ai_context.py:169-283), so NOT only policies), `order_referenced:str|null` (order_number).
Side effect: creates AiConversation(type `SUPPORT_ASSISTANT`, status `COMPLETED`) + USER and ASSISTANT AiMessage on EVERY call (ai.py:70-114). Brand-tone enforcement is NOT applied (`enforce_brand_tone_on_output` has no caller, ai.py:399).

**E3 escalate.** Deterministic keyword test, no LLM. Case-insensitive substring match against `lawyer, fraud, police, scam, cheat, court, complaint, broken, emergency, stolen` (ai_support.py:122-133, so "courteous" matches "court"); `should_escalate = any trigger OR conversation.priority == "URGENT"` (138). Response: `conversation_id`, `should_escalate:bool`, `reason:str` ("Trigger keywords detected: a, b" | "Urgent priority flagged" | "Routine customer inquiry within standard AI support boundary."), `triggers_identified:[str]`.
**Side effect on a POST that looks like a read:** when should_escalate, persists `Conversation.priority="URGENT"` and `ai_handover_state="ESCALATION_RECOMMENDED"` (147-150). No audit row, no ConversationEvent.

**E4 handover.** Deterministic template summary, no LLM (ai_support.py:160-194). Response: `conversation_id`, `customer_name:str|null` (null if the customer has no display_name/first/last name; "Customer" if conversation has no customer), `handover_state:"HANDOVER_TO_HUMAN"`, `summary:str`, `generated_at:iso string`. Side effect: persists `ai_handover_state="HANDOVER_TO_HUMAN"`; does NOT create a ConversationAssignment/ConversationEvent (unlike the domain route `conversations/{pk}/handover/`, which writes `AI`/`HUMAN`).

**E5 resume.** Persists `ai_handover_state="ACTIVE_AI"`; if `agent_notes` truthy appends `{"note","recorded_at"}` (no author) to `Conversation.source_metadata["human_agent_notes"]` (ai_support.py:211-221). Response: `conversation_id`, `ai_handover_state:"ACTIVE_AI"`, `agent_notes_retained:str|null` (echo of input), `resumed_at:iso`.

**E6 explain-campaign** (deterministic, no LLM; ai_marketing.py). Attribution uses CampaignConversion rows plus orders whose `source_metadata.utm_campaign` equals the campaign utm/name; with no attribution, revenue = 0 (37-54). Response (129-146), all numerics are STRINGS: `campaign_id:int`, `campaign_name`, `platform`, `spend` (sum of CampaignMetric.spend), `budget`, `attributed_orders_count:int`, `attributed_revenue`, `net_revenue` (after COMPLETED refunds), `calculated_roas` (2 dp; "0.00" when spend is 0), `target_roas:"2.50"` (constant), `customer_repeat_rate_pct` (str of float), `top_products:[str "Name (N units)"]<=3`, `performance_summary:str`, `optimization_suggestions:[str]` (hard-coded rules at ROAS>=2.50 / <1.00 / else; low-repeat rule; goals line), `active_goals:[{id,title,document_type:"BUSINESS_GOAL",content,version}]`, `evaluated_at:iso`.
Side effect: writes AiConversation(type `MARKETING_ASSISTANT`, COMPLETED) + 2 AiMessages each call (118-127).

**E7 diagnose-issue** (ai_diagnosis.py). Accepts ANY issue status (not only OPEN). Evidence = BusinessIssueEvidence.description values, or the single string "Detected by Business Intelligence rules on {issue_type}" when none (28-43). Graph traversal depth 2 around subject entity (46-53). LLM call 15 s with fallback "Diagnosis for '{title}': verified evidence is insufficient for a causal conclusion...". Response (122-134): `issue_id`, `issue_type`, `severity`, `symptom:str`, `diagnostic_summary:str`, `impact_explanation:str`, `contributing_factors:[str]<=5`, `supporting_evidence:[str]`, `historical_context:{previous_resolved_issues:[{id,title,description}]<=3, past_approved_recommendations:[{id,title,description}]<=2}`, `graph_connections:[str]` (node keys such as `customer_12`, from safe_traverse; services/graph.py:17-43), `diagnosed_at:iso`.
Side effect: AiConversation(type `BUSINESS_DIAGNOSIS`, COMPLETED) + 2 AiMessages per call. The full prompt is stored in the USER message.

**E8 summarize conversation** (ai_summarization.py:14-93). `customer_intent` in {`ORDER_STATUS_INQUIRY`(default),`REFUND_REQUEST`,`CANCELLATION_REQUEST`,`PRODUCT_DEFECT_COMPLAINT`,`DELIVERY_DELAY_INQUIRY`} by substring match on subject + all message text; `resolution_status` = `RESOLVED` if conversation.status in {RESOLVED,CLOSED} else `PENDING_AGENT_ACTION`; `key_details:{channel,priority,customer_id,total_messages}`; `summary:str` (LLM 15 s with template fallback; first 4000 chars of thread sent).
Side effect: overwrites `Conversation.source_metadata["latest_summary"]={summary,intent,resolution_status,summarized_at}` (77-85). No AI audit row.

**E9 summarize call** (ai_summarization.py:96-160). Deterministic, no LLM. Uses the newest CallTranscript for the call. If none, empty, or the legacy placeholder (`provider=="BIZORA_AUDIO_AI"` and text `"Call transcript for {direction} call {id}"`) -> returns `call_purpose:"UNKNOWN"`, `outcome:"UNKNOWN"`, `customer_concerns:"Customer concerns are unknown because no transcript is available."`, `transcript_summary:"No transcript is available; the call purpose and outcome are unknown."`, `transcript_id:int|null`, and writes nothing. Otherwise `call_purpose` in {`ORDER_CANCELLATION`,`ORDER_CONFIRMATION`,`CUSTOMER_SUPPORT_INQUIRY`}, `outcome` always `FOLLOW_UP_REQUIRED`, and WRITES `CallTranscript.summary`. Note direction is compared to `"OUTBOUND"` (134) while the domain layer uses `"OUTGOING"` (contracts.py:184).

**E10 recommendations list.** Item fields (serializers/action.py:54): `id, business, business_issue(int|null), recommendation_type, title, description, rationale(str|null), confidence("0.8500" string|null), priority, status, suggested_action_type, generated_at(ISO Z), evidence[]`. NOT returned: `ai_conversation, expires_at, metadata, created_at, updated_at`, and the id(s) of the linked AiAction(s).
Evidence element (action.py:57-60): `{id, evidence_type, description, business_fact_id, business_issue_id, metric_snapshot_id, knowledge_chunk_id}`; NOT returned: `weight, business_relationship_id, graph_node_id, graph_edge_id, metadata`. IDs are not resolvable via any LIVE AI route (no fact/snapshot/chunk detail route is mounted). One extra query per row (N+1). All statuses, including expired, are returned.
How evidence is attached (SERVICE-ONLY): `generate_grounded_recommendation` (ai.py:117-226) creates 1 AiRecommendationEvidence (`evidence_type` `VERIFIED_FACT_GROUNDING` if a currently-active VERIFIED fact tied to the issue/business was found else `ISSUE_GROUNDING`, `weight=1.0000`, `description="Grounded on authoritative evidence for {title}."`) and refuses (ValidationError) when neither a fact nor an issue exists (204-205) or the LLM provider status != "ok" (184-185). `generate_goal_aligned_recommendation` (281-396) creates an evidence row only if a fact or issue was found; otherwise the recommendation has `evidence: []`. Model rule: an evidence row must reference >=1 of fact/issue/snapshot/relationship/graph node/graph edge/knowledge chunk, all in the same business (models.py:1498-1531).

**E11 actions list / action object** (AiActionSerializer, action.py:10-46): `id, business, ai_recommendation(int), action_type, status, target_entity_type, target_entity_id, action_payload(object|null), approval_note, rejected_by(int|null), rejected_at, approved_by(int|null), approved_by_username, approved_at, executed_at, execution_result(object|null), is_simulated(bool), created_at, updated_at`.
`approved_by_username` is OMITTED from the JSON (key absent, not null) when `approved_by` is null (read-only dotted source on None raises AttributeError -> SkipField, DRF:fields.py get_attribute). There is no `rejected_by_username`. `approval_note` holds the latest note from EITHER approve or reject (reject overwrites it, actions.py:82).

**E12 issues list.** Item fields (action.py:66-82): `id, business, issue_type, subject_entity_type, subject_entity_id, title, description, severity, confidence("1.0000" string), status, rule_key, detected_at, resolved_at, created_at, updated_at` (`metadata` not exposed). Only `status="OPEN"` rows (views.py:150).

**E13-E15 action routes** (views.py:68-126, services/actions.py). Details and transitions in 2C. Success body = E11 object, HTTP 200 (no 201/202). Synchronous.
- 409 body is `{"error": str(django ValidationError)}` and `str()` of a Django ValidationError is the repr of a list, i.e. the text looks like `["Action cannot be approved from state 'EXECUTED'."]` (DJ:core/exceptions.py:212-215). Display carefully or parse.
- Messages: approve `Action cannot be approved from state '{status}'.`; reject `Action cannot be rejected from state '{status}'.`; execute `AiAction cannot execute without approval (approved_at must be set).` (also returned for EXECUTING/FAILED/REJECTED/SUGGESTED states) or `A provider executor is required for non-simulated actions.`

**E16 review route** (domain layer): reads `decision`/`reason` from IdentityCommandSerializer (serializers/domains.py:29-52: `decision` CharField<=30, `reason` CharField<=2000, both optional in the serializer but required by `require('decision' in v and 'reason' in v)`). Rules in 2C. `projections.serialize(obj)` returns every AiAction column by field name with raw FK ids (domains/projections.py:11-14). Writes a `DomainAudit` row (`ACTION_APPROVED`/`ACTION_REJECTED`). It never executes anything (response hard-codes `execution: UNAVAILABLE`).

**E17 overview.intelligence** (domains/projections.py:22-47): `recommendations` = only status `SUGGESTED`, not expired, AND `recommendation_supported()` (has evidence, every evidence row tenant-safe, evidence includes >=1 currently VERIFIED fact) -> `{count, results[<=50, all AiRecommendation columns>], truncated}`; `recommendation_evidence` same envelope with ALL AiRecommendationEvidence columns (incl. weight, graph_node, graph_edge, relationship, metadata); `ai_answers: {"status":"UNAVAILABLE","owner":"AI Intelligence",...}`. Section envelope (projections.py:17-20): `{count, results, truncated}` ordered by pk.

**E18 contract stub.** Body must be a dict with EXACTLY the keys `request_id` and `payload` (domain_views.py:351-352). `payload` required keys (boundaries.py:9,58-66): ai/entity_context -> `entity_type` (str, one of CUSTOMER, PRODUCT, ORDER, PAYMENT, CAMPAIGN, SESSION, CONVERSATION, CALL, SHIPMENT, VISITOR (domains/identity.py:7-11) or MESSAGE) and `entity_id` (int>0, must belong to the business); memory/policy_context -> `query` (str 1-2000). Without `settings.BIZORA_DOMAIN_ADAPTERS` (not defined anywhere in the repo's settings) the response is the UNAVAILABLE envelope above.

#### 2C. ACTION LIFECYCLE (AiAction.status, free CharField(30), default `SUGGESTED`, models.py:1537-1575)

States used by code: `SUGGESTED, APPROVED, REJECTED, EXECUTING, EXECUTED, FAILED`.
Creation (SERVICE-ONLY): ai.py:220-224 and 385-394 create `status="SUGGESTED", is_simulated=True`. Every action has a mandatory `ai_recommendation`.

Route A (`actions/{pk}/...`, services/actions.py), row-locked with `select_for_update`:
| From | approve (E13) | reject (E14) | execute (E15) |
|---|---|---|---|
| SUGGESTED | -> APPROVED (sets approved_by, approved_at, approval_note) | -> REJECTED (sets rejected_by, rejected_at, approval_note) | 409 |
| APPROVED | same approver again: 200 no-op (returns row); different user: 409 | -> REJECTED (approved_by/approved_at are NOT cleared) | -> EXECUTING -> EXECUTED or FAILED |
| REJECTED | -> APPROVED (rejected_by/rejected_at NOT cleared) | same user: 200 no-op returning the row; different user: 409 | 409 |
| EXECUTING | 409 | 409 | 409 (message says approval missing; no recovery route, comment actions.py:111 says manual reconciliation) |
| EXECUTED | 409 | 409 | 200 no-op returning the row |
| FAILED | 409 | 409 | 409 |
Execute preconditions (actions.py:99-107): `approved_at` set AND `status=="APPROVED"`; if `is_simulated` is false and no executor -> 409. The HTTP view never passes an executor, so **execution over HTTP is always the simulation**: `execution_result={"status":"success","message":"Simulated execution completed successfully"}`, `executed_at` set, status EXECUTED (115-118,134-137). Result is classed failed if it is not a dict, has status failed/error, `success is False`, or contains `error`/`errors` (119-125), in which case status FAILED; AiAction.save() also forces FAILED whenever execution_result carries `error`/`errors`/`success:false` (models.py:1565-1574). Model `clean()` forbids EXECUTING/EXECUTED without `approved_at` (1557-1560).
Audit side effects: approve/reject/execute each write an AiConversation(type `ACTION_AUDIT`, COMPLETED, title `ACTION_APPROVED|ACTION_REJECTED|ACTION_EXECUTED|ACTION_FAILED`) + one SYSTEM AiMessage with `{action_id,user_id,result}` (actions.py:26-38,65,84,139; the execute audit has no user). Approve also dispatches event `ai.action.approved`, which has no subscribers (events/dispatcher.py; no `subscribe("ai.action.approved")` anywhere).
Who may act: roles `OWNER|ADMIN|OPERATOR` on the action's business, or superuser. Role `MEMBER` (the BusinessMembership default, models.py:87) and `MANAGER`/`VIEWER` are refused (test app/test_ai_endpoint_matrix.py:66-85 asserts MEMBER gets 403, OPERATOR 200). No rule that approver != creator, none that executor == approver, no expiry, no per-action_type payload validation. Approving an action does NOT change the AiRecommendation.status.
Evidence guardrail on Route A: NONE (approve_action never calls `recommendation_supported`).

Route B (`businesses/{id}/actions/{pk}/review/`, domains/operations.py:70-86): needs OWNER or MANAGER; `related()` rejects ids from other businesses with 400 INVALID_RELATION "Related object is unavailable." and `ensure_owned` also re-validates every FK and any `target_entity_type/id` whose type is in {CUSTOMER,PRODUCT,ORDER,PAYMENT,CAMPAIGN,SESSION,CONVERSATION,CALL,SHIPMENT,VISITOR} (domains/common.py:133-156); `decision in {APPROVED, REJECTED}` and non-blank reason; for APPROVED requires `recommendation_supported` (recommendation status in SUGGESTED/APPROVED, not expired, >=1 evidence row, all evidence tenant-safe, evidence includes a currently VERIFIED fact) else 400 `EVIDENCE_REQUIRED` "Approval requires current, tenant-safe verified evidence and a valid recommendation."; only from `SUGGESTED` (or repeating the same decision = 200 no-op) else 400 `INVALID_TRANSITION` "Action already reviewed/executed."; sets `status, approval_note, approved_at (None when REJECTED)`; it does NOT set `approved_by`, `rejected_by` or `rejected_at`. An action approved via Route B can then be executed via E15 (needs status APPROVED + approved_at only).

#### 2D. SERVICE-ONLY AI functions (no HTTP route; frontend must mark "backend-blocked")

| Function (signature) | Source | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `generate_grounded_recommendation(business, issue=None, intent="delivery_optimization", timeout_seconds=15) -> (AiRecommendation, AiAction)` | services/ai.py:117 | The ONLY producer of recommendations+actions with evidence. Raises django `ValidationError` ("AI provider is unavailable; no recommendation was created." when provider_status != "ok", i.e. whenever `BIZORA_LLM_URL` is unset; "LLM confidence must be numeric."; "...between 0 and 1."; "Cannot generate a recommendation without verified evidence or an issue."). Always `priority="HIGH"`, `status="SUGGESTED"`, action `is_simulated=True`. Mapping issue -> (recommendation_type, action_type, target_entity_type, payload): campaign/marketing -> `MARKETING_OPTIMIZATION`,`PAUSE_CAMPAIGN`,`campaign`,`{"action":"adjust_budget","adjustment_pct":-20}`; inventory/stock/product -> `PRODUCT_STRATEGY`,`RESTOCK_PRODUCT`,`product`,`{"reorder_quantity":100}`; support/conversation -> `SUPPORT_IMPROVEMENT`,`CREATE_SUPPORT_TASK`,`conversation`,`{"priority":"URGENT"}`; other issue -> `EVIDENCE_REVIEW`,`REVIEW_ISSUE`,subject type or `business`,`{"requires_human_review":true}`; no issue -> `EVIDENCE_REVIEW`,`REVIEW_EVIDENCE`,`business`. `target_entity_id = issue.subject_entity_id or 1` for campaign/product/conversation (a fabricated id 1 when the issue has no subject; ai.py:148,156,164). |
| `generate_goal_aligned_recommendation(business, issue_type=None, candidate_action_type="CHANGE_COURIER", candidate_entity_type="COURIER", candidate_entity_id=None) -> (AiRecommendation, AiAction)` | ai.py:281 | No LLM. Checks HistoricalDecision conflicts (outcome in REJECTED/INEFFECTIVE/FAILED) -> priority LOW, confidence 0.2000, `metadata.repeat_decision_conflict`; matched BusinessGoal -> HIGH/0.9200; else MEDIUM/0.7500. `recommendation_type` = `candidate_action_type`. Evidence row only if a fact or issue found. |
| `assemble_grounded_memory_context(business, query=None, intent=None, top_k=5) -> dict` | ai.py:229 | Returns `{profile, active_goals[], brand_tone, retrieved_knowledge[{chunk_id,document_id,document_title,content,score,version,source_reference,citations}], query, intent}`. Only caller of `hybrid_retrieval`; no view calls it, so vector/hybrid search never runs in a request. |
| `enforce_brand_tone_on_output(business, text, channel=None) -> str` | ai.py:399 | Not called by any view/service path. |
| `promote_recommendation_to_memory(business, recommendation, user, approved_at=None)` | ai.py:432 | Requires `recommendation.status == "APPROVED"`; nothing in the repo ever sets a recommendation to APPROVED (approving an AiAction does not), so unreachable in real flows. |
| `record_ai_audit_event(business, conversation_type, action_name, prompt, output_content, context_reference=None, customer=None, support_conversation=None, model_name="gemini-1.5-pro", tokens_used=150)` | ai.py:70 | Used by E2/E6/E7. Hard-coded model name and a fake token count (150 split 75/75) are stored in AiMessage. |

Customer/order contexts are used only inside E2. |

#### 2E. BUSINESS MEMORY & KNOWLEDGE: HTTP survey and service functions

HTTP result: **NO ENDPOINT FOUND** for any of: business profile, policies (generic/delivery/return-refund), product knowledge, brand tone, support knowledge, goals, historical decisions, approved suggestions, knowledge sources (LIVE), document upload/ingestion/processing/chunking/versioning, embeddings, vector/keyword/hybrid search/retrieval. Verified by: full read of app/urls.py (no include, only app/views.py and app/domain_views.py classes); `grep` of every Knowledge*/BusinessMemory/hybrid_retrieval/ingest_document/register_knowledge_source usage outside tests shows callers only in services, app/configuration/*, app/overview/evidence.py, app/domains/merge.py|common.py; `app/test_batch*.py` and `app/test_e2e_business_memory_knowledge_validation.py` contain no HTTP calls; app/admin.py registers none of these models.
The only LIVE HTTP touchpoints: (a) E18 `contracts/memory/policy_context` -> always UNAVAILABLE; (b) E19 `policy_context` stub; (c) read-only echoes inside AI responses (`applied_policies` titles in E2, `active_goals` in E6).
Storage models: `KnowledgeSource` (models.py:1287), `KnowledgeDocument` (1306), `KnowledgeChunk` (1354), `KnowledgeEmbedding` (1372, `VectorField`, pgvector type on PostgreSQL else text), `BusinessGoal` (96); `BusinessMemory`, `HistoricalDecision`, `ApprovedSuggestion` exist ONLY in migration app/migrations/0006_historicaldecision_businessmemory_approvedsuggestion.py and in git history (class bodies at 5888772:app/models.py:1560-1620) but are ABSENT from app/models.py at HEAD (GAP-1).

SERVICE-ONLY functions (all take a `Business` instance; tenant check = non-null business, no HTTP caller):
| Feature | Signature | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Profile | `set_business_profile(business, profile: BusinessProfileData) -> BusinessMemory`; `get_business_profile(business) -> dict` (default `{name, description:"", industry:"General", operating_regions:[], target_customers:"", operating_model:{}, ai_context_summary}`) | domain_memory.py:82,100 |
| Profile data | `BusinessProfileData(name, description, industry, operating_regions: list[str], target_customers: str, operating_model: dict, ai_context_summary: str, source_tracking="MANUAL_ENTRY")` | domain_memory.py:32-40 |
| Policies | `set_business_policy(business, policy_category, title, content, applicability="ALL", metadata=None)`; `get_active_policies(business, policy_category=None) -> list[BusinessMemory]` (spec/traceability call this `get_business_policies`: does not exist) | domain_memory.py:126,150 |
| Product knowledge | `set_product_knowledge(business, product, knowledge_type, title, content, metadata=None)`; `get_product_knowledge(business, product_id)` | domain_memory.py:164,194 |
| Delivery policy | `set_delivery_policy(business, policy: DeliveryPolicyData)`; `get_delivery_policy(business) -> dict`; `DeliveryPolicyData(coverage_zones: list[str], sla_hours: dict[str,int], charges: dict[str,float], courier_preferences: list[str], cod_rules: dict, failed_delivery_handling: str)` | domain_memory.py:211,227,44-50 |

| Brand tone | `set_brand_tone(business, tone: BrandToneData)`; `get_brand_tone(business, channel=None)` (default `{primary_tone:"Polite, professional, concise", style_guidelines:[], preferred_terms:[], restricted_words:[], channel_overrides:{}}`); `validate_text_against_brand_tone(business, text, channel=None) -> list[str]`; `BrandToneData(primary_tone, style_guidelines, preferred_terms, restricted_words, channel_overrides: dict[str,dict])` | domain_memory.py:279,295,323,64-69 |
| Support knowledge | `set_support_knowledge(business, category, question_or_topic, approved_answer, troubleshooting_tree=None, escalation_team=None)`; `get_support_knowledge(business, category=None)` | domain_memory.py:340,364 |
| Goals | `set_business_goal(business, name, category, priority, target_value: str, timeframe) -> BusinessGoal` (priority upper-cased; stored in `target_text`); `get_active_business_goals(business, category=None) -> list[BusinessGoal]` (ACTIVE only; HIGH>MEDIUM>LOW then newest) | domain_memory.py:378,399 |
| Historical decisions | `record_historical_decision(business, decision_type, title, rationale, outcome, context=None, target_entity_type=None, target_entity_id=None, decided_by=None, decided_at=None)`; `check_repeat_decision_conflict(business, decision_type, target_entity_type=None, target_entity_id=None)`. No production caller of `record_historical_decision`: approve/reject/execute never record decisions. | domain_memory.py:420,448 |
| Approved suggestions | `promote_approved_suggestion(business, recommendation, approved_by, approved_at) -> ApprovedSuggestion` (needs recommendation.status=="APPROVED", approved_by, approved_at, and approver role in OWNER/ADMIN/OPERATOR; idempotent per recommendation) | domain_memory.py:472 |
| Knowledge source | `register_knowledge_source(business, name, source_type, knowledge_type=None, source_reference=None, metadata=None) -> KnowledgeSource` (status ACTIVE; rejects prohibited source types/keys with `DomainBoundaryViolationError`). `archive_knowledge_source` named in docs DOES NOT EXIST | knowledge_pipeline.py:143 |
| Ingestion | `ingest_document(business, request: IngestionRequest) -> KnowledgeDocument` (creates source by name if absent; refuses ARCHIVED source; document status `PENDING`, version 1); `IngestionRequest(business_id, source_name, source_type, knowledge_type, title, content, document_type="POLICY", file_name=None, file_url=None, mime_type="text/plain", metadata=None)`. No file upload handling anywhere; `file_url` is just a text column. | knowledge_pipeline.py:173,63-74 |
| Processing | `process_document(document) -> KnowledgeDocument` (normalise newlines, SHA-256 `content_hash`, duplicate flag in `metadata.is_duplicate/duplicate_of_id`, status READY, or FAILED if empty) | knowledge_pipeline.py:221 |
| Chunking | `chunk_document(document, chunk_size=500, chunk_overlap=50) -> list[KnowledgeChunk]` (markdown `#`..`####` heading sections, word-based) | knowledge_pipeline.py:287 |
| Versioning | `bump_document_version(current_document, new_content, updated_title=None, updated_metadata=None)` (old doc -> SUPERSEDED, old chunks `is_current=false`, new doc READY version+1); `resolve_active_version(business, knowledge_source_id, target_version=None)` | knowledge_pipeline.py:393,458 |
| Boundary guards | `validate_domain_boundaries(source_type, metadata=None, content="")`; `validate_tenant_isolation(business_id, expected_business=None)` | knowledge_pipeline.py:97,123 |
| Embeddings | `generate_or_store_embedding(chunk, vector, model_name="text-embedding-3-small", expected_dimensions=None) -> KnowledgeEmbedding` (caller must supply the vector; no embedding-provider code exists in the repo) | hybrid_retrieval.py:68 |
| Vector search | `vector_search(business, query_vector, top_k=5, min_score=0.0, model_name="text-embedding-3-small", filter_metadata=None) -> list[RetrievalResultItem]` | hybrid_retrieval.py:131 |
| Keyword search | `keyword_search(business, query, top_k=5, filter_metadata=None)` | hybrid_retrieval.py:238 |
| Hybrid | `hybrid_retrieval(business, query, query_vector=None, top_k=5, rrf_k=60, vector_weight=0.5, keyword_weight=0.5, filter_metadata=None)`; result `RetrievalResultItem(chunk_id, document_id, document_title, content, version, source_reference, metadata, dense_score, sparse_score, rrf_score)`; vector model chosen by vector length (4 -> `test-model-4d`, else `text-embedding-3-small`) | hybrid_retrieval.py:313,25-35,343 |
Retrieval used by the live AI endpoints is NOT this engine: E1/E2/E6 use `assemble_memory_context` (ai_context.py:169-283), a SQL `icontains` match over KnowledgeDocument/KnowledgeChunk + BusinessMemory + approved recs + executed actions + active goals.

#### 2F. UNMOUNTED surfaces that touch knowledge or recommendations (details belong to other scans; cross-reference only)
- Business Configuration urlconf app/configuration/urls.py (original mount `api/businesses/{business_id}/configuration/`, git fd41ea7:app/urls.py): `sources/` (GET paginated list; POST 201), `sources/{pk}/` (GET, PATCH), `sources/{pk}/history/`, `reprocessing/` (GET paginated; POST 201 new / 200 idempotent replay), `knowledge/`, `profile/`, `ai/`, `general/`, `rules/`, `policies/` (+`history/`). These read/write `ConfigurationRecord/Revision` plus mirror fields onto `KnowledgeSource` (configuration/knowledge.py:39-72, views.py:171-204). `configuration/profile` and `configuration/policies` are configuration records, NOT the `BusinessMemory` profile/policy store. `SourceSchema`: `source_id` int, `name` <=255, `source_type` in `MANUAL|UPLOAD|WEBSITE|INTEGRATION`, `knowledge_type` in `PROFILE|POLICY|PRODUCT|SUPPORT|BRAND|INTERNAL|GOALS|DECISIONS`, `source_reference` <=1000 (`https://` required for WEBSITE) (knowledge.py:13-24; schemas.py:20-21). Reprocessing body: `idempotency_key` regex `^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,100}$`, exactly one of `source_id|document_id`, `action` in `REPROCESS|REEMBED`; response always reports `worker_available:false` and nothing consumes `KnowledgeReprocessingRequest` rows (knowledge.py:75-130). There is no document upload/ingest route even there.
- Overview urlconf app/overview/urls.py (original mount `api/businesses/{business_id}/overview/`, git ce41d54:app/urls.py; that prefix is now occupied by the LIVE domain OverviewView E17): `recommendations/` (paginated, status/priority/issue/from/to filters, evidence_summary<=3, action_summary<=3), `recommendations/{pk}/` (adds description, rationale, resolved evidence list, graph, linked actions with preview), `evidence/knowledge/{pk}/` (document_id, document_title, excerpt[:800]) (overview/findings.py:27-124; views.py:189-205; evidence.py:150-152). This is the only code that resolves recommendation evidence ids and links recommendations to actions; it is not reachable.

### 5. FRONTEND NOTES

1. **Base URL**: `/api/` and `/api/v1/` are identical mounts of the same urlconf; pick one. No trailing-slash tolerance beyond Django defaults (all routes end with `/`).
2. **Auth**: DRF default Session+Basic (settings define no authentication classes, no token/JWT, no CORS). Session-authenticated POSTs require a CSRF token (DRF:authentication.py:112-140). Nothing under `/api/` logs a user in; the only login page is the HTML Django LoginView at `/ops/login/` (operations_urls.py). Unauthenticated calls return HTTP 403 (not 401) `{"detail":"Authentication credentials were not provided."}`.
3. **Which business**: AI routes take `business_id` in the URL; the three action routes (E13-E15) take only the action id and derive the business from it. Cross-tenant: business-scoped routes give 403 for a real-but-foreign business and 404 for a missing business; entity ids from another tenant give 404; action ids from another tenant give 403 `{"error":"Business access required."}` (leaks existence).
4. **Error shapes (three coexist)**: (a) DRF `{"detail":"..."}` or field dict `{"approval_note":["..."]}` (400/403/404/405); (b) action routes `{"error":"..."}` for 403 and 409 (409 text is a Python list repr, see E13); (c) domain routes `{"code":"...","detail":"..."}` (E16-E19). A client needs all three parsers.
5. **No pagination, filtering or ordering control** on E10/E11/E12 (bare arrays, unbounded). E11 is newest-first; E10 and E12 have no ORDER BY (treat order as undefined and sort client-side by `generated_at`/`detected_at`). Filter by `status` client-side. E12 shows OPEN issues only.
6. **Number/date formats**: Decimal columns (`confidence`) are JSON strings (`"0.8500"`); all E6 money/ratio values are strings; ModelSerializer timestamps are ISO-8601 with `Z`; timestamps built by services (`generated_at` in E4, `resumed_at`, `evaluated_at`, `diagnosed_at`, `valid_from`) are Python `isoformat()` strings with `+00:00` and microseconds. Foreign keys are bare integer ids (no nested objects, no names).
7. **Latency/async**: everything is synchronous in the request. LLM-backed endpoints (E1, E2, E7, E8) can block up to ~15 s per LLM call; no streaming, no job id, no polling, no throttling configured. Show a spinner and allow >=30 s timeouts. Execution (E15) is also synchronous.
8. **LLM availability is invisible**: with `BIZORA_LLM_URL` unset (nothing in settings sets it) E1/E2/E7/E8 return deterministic template text with HTTP 200, and E1 still reports `tokens_used:180` and `model_name:"gemini-1.5-pro"`. The response has no `provider_status`/`is_fallback` flag, so the UI cannot tell an AI answer from a canned fallback.
9. **Writes hidden behind POSTs**: E3 (priority->URGENT, handover state), E4 (handover state), E5 (state + notes), E8 (`source_metadata.latest_summary`), E9 (`CallTranscript.summary`) mutate data; E2/E6/E7 create AiConversation+AiMessage audit rows on every call; E1 creates conversation+messages. No idempotency key is read by any AI route; double-clicks duplicate rows. Disable buttons while pending.
10. **Assistant conversations**: keep the `conversation_id` returned by E1 and send it back for follow-ups. If the id is unknown/closed/not a BUSINESS_ASSISTANT it is silently replaced by a NEW id, so always overwrite your stored id with the response value. There is no endpoint to list conversations or load past messages: the transcript must be kept client-side. Conversations are not bound to a user; any member of the business can continue any ACTIVE assistant conversation by id.
11. **Action review UI**: Route A needs a note (3-1000 chars) for BOTH approve and reject; reject then approve is allowed; approved actions can still be rejected until executed; executing is allowed only from APPROVED and always runs the simulation (`is_simulated` is true on every service-created action; show a "simulated" badge and render `execution_result`). Handle `FAILED` and a stuck `EXECUTING` (409 on every further call, no recovery route). After any action call, replace the row with the returned object (full AiAction). `approved_by_username` may be absent. Show buttons by role using the server's rule `OWNER|ADMIN|OPERATOR` (Route A) or `OWNER|MANAGER` (Route B); the frontend cannot learn the caller's role from any AI route, so rely on handling 403.
12. **Which approval route to build against**: they differ materially (2C). Route A has no evidence check and works for operators; Route B enforces evidence + manager role but sets neither `approved_by` nor `rejected_by`, cannot reopen a decision, and never executes. A recommendation created by `generate_goal_aligned_recommendation` without a fact/issue has `evidence:[]` and can never pass Route B.
13. **Recommendations/actions are empty by default**: no HTTP route or background job creates them (GAP-3). E10/E11 return `[]` until a service call or demo data seeds rows. Recommendations do not expose their action ids; link them client-side via `action.ai_recommendation`.
14. **Evidence display**: E10 gives only `{evidence_type, description, *_id}`; the generic description is the same sentence for every row ("Grounded on authoritative evidence for {title}."). There is no LIVE route to fetch the referenced fact/issue/snapshot/chunk, so an "evidence drawer" can show type + description + ids only.
15. **Business Memory & Knowledge**: mark every screen (profile, policies, delivery/return, brand tone, support knowledge, goals, historical decisions, approved suggestions, sources, document upload, search) as BACKEND-BLOCKED. Do not invent routes. The only knowledge-derived strings the UI can show today are `applied_policies` (E2) and `active_goals` (E6).
16. **Do not confuse** `ai/actions/` (E11) with `businesses/{id}/actions/{pk}/review/` (E16), `businesses/{id}/issues/` (E12, OPEN only) with the generic resource list, nor the AI `support/handover/` (E4, only sets a flag) with the domain `conversations/{pk}/handover/` (assignment + event + audit).

### 6. GAPS AND AMBIGUITIES

- **GAP-1 (BLOCKER, new): models missing at HEAD.** app/models.py (5f176a2) has no class `BusinessMemory`, `HistoricalDecision`, `ApprovedSuggestion`, `SupportTeam`, `ProcessingReceipt`, `DomainAudit`, `ConversationEvent`, `IdentityHistory`, `CallbackRequest`, `AdAccount`, `CampaignAdSet`, `CampaignCreative`, `CampaignAd`, `ProductImage`, yet code uses them (ai_context.py:25,196; domain_memory.py:17-25; domains/registry.py:6,25-28,37; domains/operations.py:38,41; domains/common.py:118; models.py:673 `Campaign.ad_account = FK('AdAccount')` dangling). Git evidence (`git show <sha>:app/models.py | grep -c '^class X('`): present in e487b0b and d577133 (all 14), absent in 396593f (the merge "Merge remote-tracking branch 'origin/main' into codex/business-configuration") and in 5f176a2; BusinessMemory/HistoricalDecision/ApprovedSuggestion were also present in a327630 and added in 5888772; migrations 0002_domain_foundations.py / 0004_product_images.py (domain tables) and 0006_historicaldecision_businessmemory_approvedsuggestion.py (memory tables) still create them. `git diff e487b0b 5f176a2 -- app/models.py` also shows removal of `import re` and `from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError` (ground-truth item 3). Consequence: `import app.services.ai_context` / `domain_memory` -> ImportError -> `app/views.py` -> `app/urls.py` fail to import, so no `/api/` route works even after settings/admin fixes. NOT VERIFIED by execution (nothing may be run); verified by reading imports and git objects.
- **GAP-2**: `re` is missing in models.py, so `KnowledgeDocument.get_active_policies_for_intent` (models.py:1325-1344) raises NameError at `re.split` (1337) for any truthy `intent`; E1 and E2 always pass a non-empty intent (defaults `business_performance_inquiry` / `refund_inquiry`) so they would 500 once GAP-1 is fixed unless `import re` is restored. Likewise `ValidationError` is undefined where raised (models.py:1252,1464,1510,1512,1560), including `AiAction.clean()`, so the approval/execution guard would raise NameError instead of a 409 when it triggers.
- **GAP-3**: No endpoint, task or command creates AiRecommendation/AiAction in production code (only `app/overview/demo.py:99`, tests and the two service functions). The frontend can list but never produce them.
- **GAP-4**: AiRecommendation.status never leaves `SUGGESTED` (no writer anywhere). Approving/rejecting/executing an AiAction does not update its recommendation, so `promote_recommendation_to_memory` (needs `APPROVED`) and the approved-recommendation memory feed (ai_context.py:242-254) are unreachable in real flows, and `record_historical_decision` is never called, contradicting spec 16 "Post-Execution Context" and "Approved Knowledge Lifecycle".
- **GAP-5**: Two approval routes with different guardrails/roles/transitions (2C, E13-E15 vs E16). Evidence-required rule exists only in E16. Route A lets a recommendation without evidence be approved and executed. Neither route is documented as canonical.
- **GAP-6**: Role vocabulary mismatch: AI action routes accept `OWNER|ADMIN|OPERATOR` (views.py:75; services/actions.py:51,74; domain_memory.py:495); domain/ops layer accepts `OWNER|MANAGER|OPERATOR|VIEWER` (domains/common.py:128); `BusinessMembership.role` has no choices. A `MANAGER` cannot use Route A; an `OPERATOR` cannot use Route B; `ADMIN` is meaningless to the domain layer. Role set for the product is not defined anywhere.
- **GAP-7**: Business-scoped AI POSTs that mutate state (E3, E4, E5, E8, E9) and all LLM routes accept ANY active member role (even a `VIEWER`) because `require_business_access` is called without `roles` (views.py:50-57).
- **GAP-8**: Misleading metadata: `model_name:"gemini-1.5-pro"` and fixed `tokens_used` (180 fallback; 150 in audit rows) are constants, not provider output (ai_assistant.py:40,129; ai.py:79-80). `provider_status` is never surfaced. Support-reply `confidence:"0.90"` fallback value is dropped.
- **GAP-9**: Fabricated target ids: `target_entity_id = issue.subject_entity_id or 1` (ai.py:148,156,164) writes id 1 when the issue has no subject; Route B's `ensure_owned` then fails with 400 INVALID_RELATION if campaign/product/conversation 1 is absent or foreign. Action semantics also disagree: `PAUSE_CAMPAIGN` carries payload `{"action":"adjust_budget","adjustment_pct":-20}` and title "Adjust Campaign Budget" (ai.py:146-150). Casing of `target_entity_type` varies.
- **GAP-10**: Vocabulary clashes: `ai_handover_state` (AI: ESCALATION_RECOMMENDED/HANDOVER_TO_HUMAN/ACTIVE_AI vs domain: AI/HUMAN/null; the domain handover short-circuits on equality, operations.py:52-56); call direction `OUTBOUND` (ai_summarization.py:134) vs `OUTGOING` (contracts.py:184); knowledge source types (`MANUAL_ENTRY...` vs `MANUAL|UPLOAD|WEBSITE|INTEGRATION`).
- **GAP-11**: Recommendation serializer omits `expires_at`, `metadata`, `ai_conversation`, evidence `weight` and graph/relationship ids, and the linked action ids; expired or non-SUGGESTED recommendations are returned unflagged. N+1 evidence queries.
- **GAP-12**: Spec capabilities with NO ENDPOINT FOUND: AI conversation/message history and audit trail read (spec 17); recommendation detail/approve/reject (spec 13-14); execution routing to Business Operations/Integrations (spec 16); product-aware AI (9); standalone retrieval/search over memory (7, MK 16-18); every Business Memory & Knowledge management function (MK 1-19).
- **GAP-13**: Brand tone, restricted-word enforcement and `validate_text_against_brand_tone` are not applied to any reply returned by E2 (spec "Policy Compliance").
- **GAP-14**: Hybrid/vector retrieval is never used by a request; E1/E2 context uses SQL `icontains` on documents/chunks (ai_context.py:227-239). No embedding provider exists (vectors must be supplied to `generate_or_store_embedding`), and the configuration reprocessing queue has no consumer (`worker_available:false`). On SQLite the vector path scores in Python; the pgvector `<=>` branch is PostgreSQL only (hybrid_retrieval.py:176-190).
- **GAP-15**: E1 claims "cross-domain answers" but only facts, policies/memory and 5 open issues are in context; campaign/order/customer/product/graph data are not supplied by the view (views.py:165-172), and `grounding_facts` are not mapped to individual claims.
- **GAP-16**: Concurrency/atomicity relies on `select_for_update`; effective on PostgreSQL, a no-op on the SQLite dev fallback. `EXECUTING` has no timeout/recovery path (actions.py:111 comment).
- **GAP-17**: `approved_by_username` key omission and `approval_note` overwrite semantics (reject overwrites approve's note; approved_* fields are not cleared on reject, rejected_* not cleared on re-approve) mean a REJECTED row can still carry `approved_by/approved_at` and an APPROVED row can carry `rejected_by/rejected_at`. UI must key off `status`, never off those fields.
- **GAP-18**: AI conversations have no user/owner column (models.py:1388-1401); the audit conversations for E2/E6/E7 are never linked back to the requester.
- **GAP-19 (docstring vs code)**: `explain_campaign_performance` docstring promises clicks, conversions and delivery outcomes (ai_marketing.py:24-26) but the response carries only spend, budget, attributed orders/revenue, refunds, repeat rate, top products, rule-based suggestions and goals; `ask_business_assistant` docstring promises cross-domain context (ai_assistant.py:42-47) but only facts, memory items and open issues are assembled; `generate_suggested_reply` docstring promises a "policy-compliant" reply (ai_support.py:187-190) but compliance exists only as a prompt sentence; `Business.execute_llm_with_timeout` docstring speaks of "background workers" (models.py:50-53) while every call is inside the HTTP request; the traceability doc names functions/tests that do not exist (see 4C). No view has a docstring, so request/response shapes above come from serializers and service return dicts only.
- **NOT VERIFIED**: any runtime behaviour (no code was run; status codes for the 400/403/404 cases are derived from reading the views plus DRF/Django sources); the LLM gateway contract beyond what `execute_llm_with_timeout` sends/accepts; the 342-test and PostgreSQL/pgvector claims in the traceability doc; `analytics.py` refund accounting used by E6 (read only for imports); BI-owned fact generation that feeds `grounding_facts`; whether `docs/implementations/test-reports/*` figures hold.
- **Decision for the frontend plan**: build against E0-E15 (AI Intelligence) once GAP-1 is repaired; treat E16 as an optional manager review path; treat everything under Business Memory & Knowledge as backend-blocked; treat E17-E19 and the UNMOUNTED overview/configuration endpoints as not available.

# Part C — Integration & Data Observability and Overview & Monitoring (UNMOUNTED)

## scan_C: Integration & Data Observability + Overview & Monitoring

Repo `<repo>`, checkout == origin/main `5f176a2`. Read-only scan. Nothing in the repo was edited or executed. Library behaviour (DRF 3.15.2, Django 4.2.27) was confirmed by READING the sources in the scratchpad venv at `<venv>/lib/python3.12/site-packages` (cited as `drf:` / `django:`); nothing was imported or run. Items marked NOT RUN were derived by code reading only.

Citation convention: `obs/` = `app/observability/`, `ovw/` = `app/overview/`, `cfg/` = `app/configuration/`.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
### 1. SCOPE

Two domain modules, both fully implemented as Django/DRF code but BOTH UNMOUNTED on origin/main (`app/urls.py:1-122` has no `include()`; `bizora/urls.py:21-27` only mounts `app.urls` at `api/` and `api/v1/`, `app.operations_urls` at `ops/`, admin). (a) Integration & Data Observability (`app/observability/`, 22 URL patterns in `obs/urls.py:7-19`): read-only reliability projections over integration connections, syncs, failed records, webhooks, freshness, provider health, error groups, plus four synchronous operator operations (connection test, sync retry, failed-record retry, webhook replay), two lifecycle PATCHes, a monitoring-policy upsert, and a redacted payload diagnostic. (b) Overview & Monitoring (`app/overview/`, 19 URL patterns in `ovw/urls.py:6-13`): read-only business-monitoring projections (summary, KPIs, trends, issues, health, activity, recommendations, cross-domain, catalog), typed entity and evidence drill-downs, and one writable monitoring configuration resource. `app/configuration/urls.py` (also UNMOUNTED) re-uses three of their views. No routes of either module are LIVE; one LIVE route (`businesses/<id>/overview/`, `app/urls.py:91`) shares the word "overview" but is a different, placeholder endpoint (section 2.7).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
### 1b. MOUNT / PREFIX FACTS (verified with `git show`)

| Module | ORIGINAL PREFIX (from sha) | Mounted by | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Observability | `businesses/<int:business_id>/observability/` (ORIGINAL PREFIX, from 0a6d904) | `app/urls.py` line `path('businesses/<int:business_id>/observability/', include('app.observability.urls'))`, itself under `bizora/urls.py` `path('api/', include('app.urls'))` at 0a6d904 | `git show 0a6d904:app/urls.py`, `git show 0a6d904:bizora/urls.py` |
| Overview | `businesses/<int:business_id>/overview/` (ORIGINAL PREFIX, from ce41d54) | same file, one more line | `git show ce41d54:app/urls.py` |
| Configuration (cross-ref) | `businesses/<int:business_id>/configuration/` (ORIGINAL PREFIX, from fd41ea7) | same file | `git show fd41ea7:app/urls.py` |

- At those commits only `api/` existed. On origin/main `app.urls` is included twice (`bizora/urls.py:22` and `:25`), so IF re-mounted at the original prefix the composed paths would be `/api/businesses/<id>/observability/<route>` AND `/api/v1/businesses/<id>/observability/<route>` (same for overview/configuration). The tables below show the route RELATIVE to the original prefix; compose by prepending `/api/v1/businesses/<business_id>/observability/` (or `.../overview/`).
- The three `include()` lines were lost in merge commit `396593f` ("Merge remote-tracking branch 'origin/main' into codex/business-configuration", author jihadmia35, parents `fd41ea7` and `e487b0b`): `git show fd41ea7:app/urls.py` contains the observability and overview includes (2 matches), `git show e487b0b:app/urls.py` and `git show 396593f:app/urls.py` contain none (0 matches); `git diff fd41ea7 396593f -- app/urls.py` shows the domain-team `app/urls.py` replacing the include-based one, with `include` still imported on line 1 but unused. The domains branch itself never had them (`41c5446`, `d577133`: 0 matches). The same merge brought in `api/v1/` and `ops/` from main.
- Overview payloads hard-code absolute links beginning `/api/businesses/<id>/overview/...` (`ovw/views.py:178`, `ovw/evidence.py:36,59,105`, `ovw/metrics.py:123`, `ovw/activity.py:89`). Re-mounting Overview anywhere other than `businesses/<int:business_id>/overview/` under `api/` makes every `drilldown`/`links` value 404. Observability emits no absolute links.
- Tests in `app/tests/test_observability_*.py` and `test_overview_*.py` build URLs with the old prefix (`test_observability_api.py:14`: `/api/businesses/{pk}/observability/`), so they cannot pass on origin/main as-is.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
### 2. ENDPOINT TABLES

#### 2.0 Shared rules (apply to every route below unless a row says otherwise)

AUTH-R (read) / AUTH-M (write):
- DRF `permission_classes=[IsAuthenticated]` (`obs/views.py:42`, `ovw/views.py:55`). `initial()` then calls `require_access(user, kwargs['business_id'], manage=request.method not in {'GET','HEAD','OPTIONS'})` and `get_object_or_404(Business)` (`obs/views.py:47-50`; `ovw/views.py:57-59`). So every GET needs AUTH-R; every POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE needs AUTH-M. (The same BusinessView logic exists in `cfg/views.py:23-27`.)
- `require_access` (`obs/access.py:6-15`): user must be authenticated + `is_active` else `PermissionDenied('Authentication is required.')`; `is_superuser` passes; otherwise a `BusinessAccess` row (`user`, `business_id`) with `role` in `READER`/`MANAGER` must exist (`MANAGER` only when `manage=True`); else `PermissionDenied('No permission for this business operation.')`. Non-member -> 403 `{"detail":"No permission for this business operation."}`; the 403 happens BEFORE the business-existence check, so a non-superuser cannot distinguish an unknown business from a forbidden one; a superuser gets 404 for an unknown business.
- `BusinessAccess` (`obs/models.py:12-18`): FK business, FK user, `role` choices `READER MANAGER`, unique (business,user). This is NOT `BusinessMembership` (`app/models.py:84`, which the live domain views use at `app/domain_views.py:29`). A user who is only a BusinessMembership member gets 403 on every route here. NO HTTP endpoint creates BusinessAccess rows: only Django admin `app/admin.py:36-39` (superuser-only) and demo/test code (`obs/demo.py:50`, `ovw/demo.py:54,60`, `cfg/demo.py:62-63`).
- Authentication classes are DRF defaults (none configured): SessionAuthentication then BasicAuthentication (`drf:settings.py:40-43`). Unauthenticated requests: `NotAuthenticated` is coerced to HTTP **403** (not 401) because SessionAuthentication defines no `authenticate_header` (`drf:views.py:453-461`). SessionAuthentication enforces CSRF on unsafe methods (`drf:authentication.py:135-148`): cookie-session clients must send `X-CSRFToken` on POST/PUT/PATCH. The repo's own tests accept 401 or 403 (`test_observability_api.py:39` and `test_overview_api.py:27`).

ERROR SHAPES (the code mixes five; verified in `drf:exceptions.py` `ValidationError`/`_get_error_details` and `drf:views.py:93-96`):
- E-DETAIL `{"detail": "<text>"}`: 403 PermissionDenied, 404 Http404/get_object_or_404 (`"Not found."`), 405, overview 409 conflict, pagination invalid page (`{"detail":"Invalid page."}`, 404).
- E-LIST `["<text>"]`: `ValidationError('text')` and converted Django `ValidationError` without field dict (`obs/views.py:52-55`). Most observability and overview business-rule 400s.
- E-DICT-STR `{"<field>": "<text>"}` (string, NOT list): `ValidationError({field: 'text'})`, e.g. `obs/views.py:61` -> `{"query":"Unsupported parameters: [...]"}`, `obs/views.py:126` -> `{"status":"Unsupported value."}`, `ovw/views.py:68-110`.
- E-DICT-LIST `{"<field>": ["<text>"]}`: DRF serializer field errors and Django `ValidationError.message_dict` (policy `full_clean`, record lifecycle).
- Strict-serializer errors (raised from `to_internal_value`, outside DRF's try/except, `drf:serializers.py:442-448`): observability -> `{"non_field_errors":["Unknown request fields."]}` (`obs/serializers.py:130-134`); overview unknown keys -> `{"unknown":["<key>", ...]}`, non-object -> `["Expected an object."]` (`ovw/configuration.py:20-27`).
- There is no `code` field and no machine-readable error code anywhere in these two modules (contrast with the live domain views: `{'code':..., 'detail':...}` at `app/domain_views.py:35-46`).

PAGINATION (class `Pagination`, `obs/views.py:35-38`, also imported by `ovw/views.py:15` and `cfg/views.py:10`): DRF PageNumberPagination, `page_size=25`, `page_size` query param, `max_page_size=100`. Envelope `{"count","next","previous","results"}` (`drf:pagination.py:230-236`); `next`/`previous` are absolute URLs. A page beyond the last returns 404 `{"detail":"Invalid page."}` (`drf:pagination.py:196-218`). `?page=last` is accepted by DRF but both modules reject non-integers with 400. Overview paginated responses add a top-level `as_of` (`ovw/views.py:112-120`). Endpoints not marked "paginated" return a single object or an unpaged `{results: [...]}`.

TIMESTAMPS: ISO-8601 strings (DRF encoder). A literal `+` in an offset must be URL-encoded as `%2B` (otherwise it decodes to a space and parsing fails; inferred from standard query decoding, NOT RUN). Use `Z`.

REDACTION: `obs/policies.py:13-26 safe_details()` is applied to the model serializers (sync, failed record, webhook, error, attempt: `obs/serializers.py:7-9`), to the final detail payloads and the payload diagnostic, but NOT to the connection/summary/freshness/provider/statistics/policy dicts. Dict keys matching `token|secret|password|authorization|api.?key|cookie|credential` (case-insens.) get value `"[REDACTED]"`; strings longer than 2000 chars are cut; lists/dicts cut to 100 items; nesting deeper than 8 -> `"[TRUNCATED]"`; any non-(None/bool/int/float/str/list/dict/datetime) value -> `"[UNSUPPORTED]"`. Overview `public()` (`ovw/views.py:28-39`) redacts sensitive dict keys the same way, converts datetime -> ISO string and Decimal -> string, and passes other scalars through `safe_details` (2000-char cut, secret-text masking, `[UNSUPPORTED]` for other types) but does not cap list/dict length or depth. Frontend must tolerate these literal placeholder strings.

#### 2.1 Observability endpoint table

Relative to ORIGINAL PREFIX `businesses/<int:business_id>/observability/` (from 0a6d904). STATUS of every row = UNMOUNTED. "Success" = HTTP 200 unless stated. Response ids (O-x) are defined in 2.2. All rows additionally inherit the shared auth/error rules above.

| Method | Path (relative) | Status | Auth | Query params (exact) | Request body | Response | Errors (beyond shared) | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| GET | `summary/` | UNMOUNTED | R | `connection`(int>=1, must belong to business), `provider`(int, IntegrationSource id), `status`(connection lifecycle), `from`,`to`(aware ISO, filter connection `created_at`); anything else -> 400 | none | O-SUM, not paginated | 400 `{"query":"Unsupported parameters: [...]"}` (unknown key); 400 `["Unsupported summary filters."]` (known but not allowed key); 400 `["Invalid connection lifecycle status."]`; 400 `["Connection does not belong to this business."]`; 400 `["from must precede to."]` | `obs/views.py:229-234`, `:57-75`, `:77-93`; `obs/selectors.py:162-173` |
| GET | `connections/` | UNMOUNTED | R | `connection`,`provider`,`status`,`ordering`,`from`,`to`,`page`,`page_size` | none | paginated O-CONN | 400 `["One or more filters are not supported for this resource."]`; 400 `["Unsupported connection ordering."]` | `obs/views.py:145-154,95-106,77-93` |
| GET | `connections/<int:pk>/` | UNMOUNTED | R | none (any param -> 400 `["Detail endpoints do not accept collection filters."]`) | none | single O-CONN | 404 if not in business | `obs/views.py:176-184` |
| POST | `connections/<int:pk>/test/` | UNMOUNTED | M | none | `{"idempotency_key": str 1-200}` (required; any other key -> 400) | O-ATT; HTTP 200 for SUCCEEDED/FAILED, **409** for UNSUPPORTED | see 2.3 operation rules | `obs/views.py:219-226`; `obs/operations.py:134-235`; `obs/urls.py:16-19` |
