Any method not listed on a route returns DRF 405 `{"detail":"Method \"X\" not allowed."}`, except PATCH on a `DetailView` route whose resource is not `errors`/`failed-records` (`connections`,`syncs`,`webhooks`,`providers`), which returns `{"detail":"Lifecycle updates are not supported for this resource."}` with 405 (`obs/views.py:209-211`) AFTER the manager check (a READER gets 403 first).

#### 2.2 Observability response schemas (from serializers/selectors the views execute)

O-SUM (`obs/selectors.py:162-173`): `{status, as_of, connection_count, failed_webhooks, active_error_groups, problems:[{connection_id, provider, status, reason, failed_events, freshness:[O-FRESH...], impact:"New source data may be incomplete or outdated."}]}`. `status` = worst `summary_status` among monitoring-enabled connections (`UNKNOWN` if none). `problems` lists only monitoring-enabled connections whose `summary_status != HEALTHY`.

O-CONN (`obs/selectors.py:149-158`): `{id, business_id, provider_id, provider(name str), name, lifecycle_status, is_mock, health:{status, reason, authentication, credential_status, observed_at, latency_ms}, summary_status, summary_reason, freshness:[O-FRESH], rate_limit:{status, remaining, retry_after_seconds, quota_scope}, last_successful_connection, last_failure, errors:{active, recent, [connection_id, last_failure]}, failed_events, monitoring_enabled, as_of, business_timezone}`. NOTE `errors` has two shapes: with data it is the aggregate row `{connection_id, active, recent, last_failure}`; with none it is `{active:0, recent:0}` only (`selectors.py:156`). Connection `config` is never returned.

O-FRESH (`obs/selectors.py:124-135`, `obs/policies.py:31-41`): `{as_of, coverage_at, age_seconds, delayed_seconds, stale_seconds, status, reason, connection_id, provider_id, entity_type, last_received_at, confirmed_at, complete(bool), monitoring_enabled(bool), evidence:{freshness_id}, warning(bool)}`; `warning = status != FRESH or not complete`. A configured-but-unobserved entity appears with `status=UNAVAILABLE`, `reason=NO_CONFIRMED_DATA`.

O-SYNC (`obs/serializers.py:12-51`): `{id, connection_id, entity_type, sync_type, trigger, status, discovered, counter_schema, records_received, records_processed, records_succeeded(null if counter_schema!=V1), records_skipped(null if !=V1), records_failed, started_at, completed_at, duration_seconds, progress_percent(round(100*processed/discovered,2) or null), stalled(bool), cursor, parent_id, initiated_by_id, error_message, metadata{only correlation_id, reason, is_mock}, created_at}`. `duration_seconds` null when `started_at` null (PENDING). `stalled` = PENDING/RUNNING and `now - last_observed_at > policy.sync_timeout_seconds`. No provider id/connection name field.

O-FR (`obs/serializers.py:54-59`): `{id, connection_id, entity_type, external_id, stage, code, message, payload_id, sync_id, webhook_id, error_id, status, first_seen_at, last_seen_at, resolved_at, resolution, resolution_method}`. `payload_id` can be fed to `payloads/<id>/` (manager only).

O-WH (`obs/serializers.py:62-94`): `{id, connection_id, event_id, event_type, entity_type, validation, status, payload_id, provider_event_at, received_at, processed_at, delivery_count, transport_latency_seconds, processing_latency_seconds, latency_reason, error_id, replay_eligible}`. `replay_eligible` = `validation==VALID and status==FAILED and payload exists and payload.signature_verified`.

O-ERR (`obs/serializers.py:97-102`): `{id, connection_id, code, category, severity, entity_type, external_id, stage, message, details, sync_id, webhook_id, status, occurrences, first_seen_at, last_seen_at, resolved_at}`. No resolution note field (notes live only in `history[]` of the detail).

O-ATT (`obs/serializers.py:105-109`): `{id, action, target_id, status, actor_id, result{...}, created_at, completed_at, next_retry_at}`. `result` always includes `is_mock`; see 2.3 for shapes.

O-PROV (`obs/selectors.py:176-208`): `{id, key, name, status, capabilities:[cap], as_of}`; tenant `cap` = `{status, reason, local_sample_count, capability, target, evidence_scope:"AGGREGATED_PROVIDER", as_of, window_seconds}`; superuser+`scope=global` `cap` = `{status, reason, sample_count, error_rate, average_latency_ms, capability, target, evidence_scope, as_of, window_seconds}`. `status` = worst capability status, `UNKNOWN` if none.

O-STAT (`obs/views.py:311-330`): `{runs, failures(status FAILED), partial(status PARTIAL), average_duration_seconds(null if none), timezone, as_of, daily:[{day, runs, failures, average_duration_seconds}]}`; `daily` is newest-first, max 100 buckets, bucketed by `started_at` in the business timezone. Not paginated.

O-POL (`obs/views.py:238-240`): `{id, [updated_at GET only], connection_id, entity_type, delayed_seconds, stale_seconds, observation_ttl_seconds, window_seconds, min_samples, min_connections, error_ratio, latency_ms, max_retries, sync_timeout_seconds, enabled}`. Model defaults (`obs/models.py:25-35`): 900, 1800, 3600, 900, 3, 2, 0.25, 2000, 3, 3600, true. Policy resolution precedence (`obs/selectors.py:16-19`): connection+entity, connection, business+entity, business, defaults.

#### 2.3 Operation endpoints (connection test, sync retry, failed-record retry, webhook replay)

All four share `execute_operation` (`obs/operations.py:134-235`), called by `OperationView.post` (`obs/views.py:219-226`). They are SYNCHRONOUS: processing, DB writes and adapter calls happen inside the HTTP request, inside one `transaction.atomic`; there is no queue, no 202, no polling endpoint. Evaluated in this order (each failure is HTTP 400 E-LIST unless noted):
1. AUTH-M (403).
2. Body: `idempotency_key` required, string 1-200 chars; other keys -> `{"non_field_errors":["Unknown request fields."]}`; missing -> `{"idempotency_key":["This field is required."]}`.
3. Target lookup scoped to the business (syncs via `integration_connection__business_id`): not found -> **400** `["Operation target does not belong to this business."]` (NOT 404).
4. Connection row locked (`select_for_update`). Idempotency: an existing `OperationAttempt` for (connection, key) is returned unchanged if same action+target (HTTP 200, or 409 if that stored attempt was UNSUPPORTED); if bound to another action/target -> `["Idempotency key is bound to another action or target."]`. This check precedes all eligibility checks, so replaying a key is always safe.
5. Connection `status` in `DISABLED`/`DISCONNECTED` -> `["Disabled/disconnected connections cannot execute operations."]` (also blocks connection test).
6. Eligibility (`obs/operations.py:117-131`):
   - `sync_retry`: sync `status` must be `PARTIAL` or `FAILED` -> `["Only partial or failed syncs may be retried."]`.
   - `record_retry`: record `status` must be `OPEN` or `REVIEWING` -> `["This failed record is not eligible for retry."]`; `code == WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_INVALID` or linked webhook `validation != VALID` -> `["Unverified webhook content cannot enter trusted processing."]`; linked webhook payload not `signature_verified` -> `["Signature verification evidence is missing."]`.
   - `webhook_replay`: `validation == VALID` and `status == FAILED` else `["Only validated failed webhook events may be replayed."]`; payload not signature-verified -> `["Signature verification evidence is missing."]`.
   - `connection_test`: no extra eligibility.
7. Non-test actions: `require_processing_scope` (`cfg/integrations.py:165-180`) may raise `["Entity processing is disabled by integration configuration."]`, `["Entity processing is not authorized by the integration scopes."]`, `["Webhook subscription is disabled by integration configuration."]` (only when the connection `config` contains `entities`/`scopes`/`webhooks` keys).
8. Non-test actions: retry cap = count of prior attempts (any status except UNSUPPORTED, including SUCCEEDED) for (connection, action, target) must be `< policy.max_retries` (default 3) -> `["Configured retry limit reached."]`; if the latest such attempt has `next_retry_at` in the future -> `["Retry backoff has not elapsed."]` (no machine-readable retry time in this error; read `attempts[0].next_retry_at` from the target's detail GET).
9. A `RUNNING` attempt row is created, then the adapter runs in a nested savepoint:
   - `operations_for(connection)` (`app/integrations/operations.py:91-97`): `is_mock=True` (the model default, `app/models.py` IntegrationConnection) -> `MockIntegrationOperations`; non-mock connection -> looks up `register_adapter()` registry; **no adapter is registered anywhere in the repo** (grep: only the definition at `app/integrations/operations.py:86`), so every non-mock connection yields UNSUPPORTED.
   - Mock adapter supports `test_connection()` (outcome from `connection.config["mock"]["connection_outcome"]`, default `SUCCESS`, latency 5) and `process()` for `entity_type == "PRODUCT"` only (anything else -> UNSUPPORTED). `config["mock"]["processing_outcome"] == "TIMEOUT"` simulates `PROVIDER_TIMEOUT`.
10. Outcomes (attempt `status`): `SUCCEEDED`; `FAILED` (HTTP still 200); `UNSUPPORTED` (HTTP **409**, `result={"code":"UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY","message":..., "is_mock":...}`; does not count toward retry cap; no `next_retry_at`).
    - `connection_test` `result`: `{health_outcome, is_mock}`; attempt is FAILED iff outcome != `SUCCESS`; writes a `ConnectionCheck` (+ an error group when not SUCCESS).
    - `sync_retry` `result`: `{sync_id(new child run), sync_status, records_succeeded, records_failed, records_skipped, is_mock}`; requires `original.metadata.payload_ids` (list of unique ints, <=1000, owned by the connection): missing/empty -> UNSUPPORTED (409); malformed or foreign ids -> a FAILED attempt with `result.code="INVALID_OPERATION_RESULT"` and HTTP 200 (the ValidationError is raised inside the adapter try-block, `obs/operations.py:77-85,204-210`), not a 400; creates a child `IntegrationSyncLog` (`trigger=RETRY`, `parent`=original, `initiated_by`=actor); processes each payload in its own savepoint; FAILED if any record failed (status PARTIAL if mixed, FAILED if all failed, COMPLETED if none).
    - `webhook_replay` / `record_retry` (via webhook): `result`: `{canonical_id, entity_type, is_mock}`; `record_retry` direct: `{canonical_id, entity_type, source_at, original_received_at, is_mock}`. Requires a captured payload else UNSUPPORTED.
    - FAILED result: `{code, message, is_mock, stage, recoverable}` (processing/validation failure) or `{code:"INTERNAL_PROCESSING_ERROR", message, exception_type, is_mock}` (unexpected adapter exception; savepoint rolled back).
    - FAILED non-test attempts get `next_retry_at = now + min(300, 30 * 2**n)` where `n` is the count of non-UNSUPPORTED attempts INCLUDING the one just created, i.e. first failure -> 60 s, then 120, 240, 300 (code reading; NOT RUN; contradicts the "beginning at 30 seconds" doc claim, see section 6).
11. Side effects: `OperationAttempt` row; `ObservabilityAudit` row (action = operation name); observation receipts (`record_observation`), freshness watermark upsert (`SourceFreshness`), resolution of matching `FailedRecord`/`IntegrationError` rows with `resolution_method="PROCESSING"`, canonical `Product` + `ProductExternalReference` persisted by the mock adapter (`app/business_data/products.py`).

#### 2.4 Overview endpoint table

Relative to ORIGINAL PREFIX `businesses/<int:business_id>/overview/` (from ce41d54). STATUS of every row = UNMOUNTED. AUTH as in 2.0 (GET = READER or MANAGER or superuser; PUT = MANAGER or superuser). `resource` kwarg values come from `ovw/urls.py:6-13`; the view maps them in `CollectionView.get` (`ovw/views.py:122-178`). Allowed query params per resource are the `ALLOWED` table (`ovw/views.py:42-52`); every other or REPEATED query param -> 400 `{"query":"Unsupported or repeated query parameters."}` (`ovw/views.py:68-72`). Response ids (V-x) in 2.5.

| Method | Path (relative) | `resource` kwarg | Status | Query params (exact) | Request body | Response | Errors (beyond shared) | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| GET | `summary/` | `summary` | UNMOUNTED | `metric`(comma list, <=50 unique, each in catalog or business's active BusinessMetric keys), `period`, `dimension_type`+`dimension_value` (together), `from`+`to` (together) | none | V-SUM (not paginated) | see Q-ERR | `ovw/views.py:122-178` |
| GET | `kpis/` | `kpis` | UNMOUNTED | `metric`,`domain`,`period`,`dimension_type`+`dimension_value`,`from`+`to` | none | V-KPIS `{results:[card], window, as_of}` | Q-ERR; 400 `["Business timezone is invalid; correct business configuration."]` | `ovw/views.py:145-149`; `ovw/metrics.py:95-123` |
| GET | `trends/` | `trends` | UNMOUNTED | same as `kpis/` | none | V-TRENDS | Q-ERR | `ovw/views.py:145-147`; `ovw/metrics.py:137-158` |
| GET | `health/` | `health` | UNMOUNTED | `period`,`from`+`to` only (`metric` -> 400) | none | V-HEALTH | Q-ERR | `ovw/views.py:150-155`; `ovw/health.py:41-88` |
| GET | `issues/` | `issues` | UNMOUNTED | `status`,`severity`,`entity_type`,`entity_id`(requires `entity_type`),`from`+`to`(on `detected_at`, `[from,to)`),`page`,`page_size` | none | paginated V-ISSUE (+ top-level `as_of`) | Q-ERR | `ovw/views.py:133-135`; `ovw/findings.py:14-25` |
| GET | `issues/<int:pk>/` | `issues` | UNMOUNTED | none (400 `["Detail endpoints do not accept query parameters."]`) | none | V-ISSUE detail (+ `as_of`) | 404 `{"detail":"Not found."}` (other business, or detected/created in the future); resolved/dismissed issues ARE returned | `ovw/views.py:189-203` |
| GET | `activity/` | `activity` | UNMOUNTED | `domain`,`entity_type`,`entity_id`(requires `entity_type`),`severity`,`from`+`to`,`page`,`page_size` | none | paginated V-ACT (+ `as_of`) | Q-ERR | `ovw/views.py:139-140`; `ovw/activity.py:44-89` |

| GET | `recommendations/<int:pk>/` | `recommendations` | UNMOUNTED | none | none | V-REC detail (+ `as_of`) | 404 as above | `ovw/views.py:197-199` |
| GET | `cross-domain/` | `cross-domain` | UNMOUNTED | none | none | V-XDOM | Q-ERR | `ovw/views.py:141-142`; `ovw/cross_domain.py:5-33` |

| PUT | `configuration/` | `configuration` | UNMOUNTED (AUTH-M) | none (query -> 400) | JSON object, ALL keys optional, unknown keys -> 400 `{"unknown":[...]}`: `version`(int>=0, optimistic lock), `period`(`DAY WEEK MONTH ROLLING_7D ROLLING_30D`), `inherit_reporting_period`(bool), `comparison_mode`(`PREVIOUS_PERIOD TARGET BASELINE NONE`), `snapshot_ttl_seconds`(int 60..31536000), `domains`(non-empty list<=7 of the 7 domains), `kpis`(list<=50 of KpiRule), `issue_severities`(list<=5 of `CRITICAL HIGH MEDIUM LOW INFO`), `recommendation_priorities`(same), `cross_domain`(list<=9 of the 9 scenario keys). KpiRule (unknown keys rejected): `key`(regex `^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,99}$`, required, unique within list, must be in catalog or business's active BusinessMetric), `direction`(`HIGHER LOWER NEUTRAL`, default NEUTRAL), `target_min`,`target_max`,`warning`,`critical`,`target_value`,`baseline_value`(Decimal max 24 digits / 6 places, send as string or number), `warning_severity`(`LOW MEDIUM HIGH`), `critical_severity`(`HIGH CRITICAL`), `goal`(str<=255), `sources`(list<=20 of `{connection_id:int>=1 owned by business, entity_type: regex ^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{0,99}$}`) | V-CFG (the full effective configuration after save); always 200 (also for first create) | **409** `{"detail":"Configuration version changed. Read the current version and retry."}` when `version` != stored version (0 when never saved); 400 `{"unknown":[...]}`, `["Expected an object."]`; field errors E-DICT-LIST; KpiRule rules: `target_min` <= `target_max`; `warning`/`critical` require direction HIGHER or LOWER; HIGHER needs `critical <= warning`, LOWER needs `critical >= warning`; `["KPI keys must be unique."]`, `["KPI must be registered in BI or the display catalog."]`, `["Sources must belong to this business."]` | `ovw/views.py:181-186`; `ovw/configuration.py:20-95,115-136` |
| GET | `entities/<str:entity_type>/<int:pk>/` | (none; `EntityView`) | UNMOUNTED | none (400 `["Entity endpoints do not accept query parameters."]`) | none | V-ENT | **404** for ANY unknown entity_type, foreign id, or created-in-future row (no 400 for bad type); `entity_type` is lower-cased before lookup | `ovw/views.py:206-214`; `ovw/evidence.py:89-108` |
| GET | `evidence/facts/<int:pk>/` | `facts` | UNMOUNTED | none | none | V-EVID (+ `evidence[]`) | 404 if not owned, or fact `status != VERIFIED`, or outside `valid_from`/`valid_to` | `ovw/evidence.py:208-226,110-128` |
| GET | `evidence/snapshots/<int:pk>/` | `snapshots` | UNMOUNTED | none | none | V-EVID | 404 if metric belongs to another business, or `calculated_at`/`period_end` > now | same |
| GET | `evidence/relationships/<int:pk>/` | `relationships` | UNMOUNTED | none | none | V-EVID | 404 if invalid time window or an endpoint entity is unavailable | same |
| GET | `evidence/nodes/<int:pk>/` | `nodes` | UNMOUNTED | none | none | V-EVID | 404 if the node's entity is unavailable | same |
| GET | `evidence/edges/<int:pk>/` | `edges` | UNMOUNTED | none | none | V-EVID | 404 if source/target node or linked relationship not owned/valid | same |
| GET | `evidence/knowledge/<int:pk>/` | `knowledge` | UNMOUNTED | none | none | V-EVID | 404 if chunk/document/source not all in this business | same |

Q-ERR (all GET collection routes; `ovw/views.py:68-110`, `ovw/windows.py:8-14,22-54`): `{"query":"Unsupported or repeated query parameters."}`; `["Query is too large."]` (str(params) > 5000); `{"<f>":"Expected a positive integer."}` / `{"<f>":"Out of range."}` for `page`,`page_size`(>100),`entity_id`,`issue`; `["dimension_type and dimension_value must be supplied together."]`; `["Dimension fields are invalid."]` (type 1-50 chars, value 1-255); `["entity_id requires entity_type."]`; `{"<f>":"Unsupported value."}` for `period`,`priority`,`severity`,`domain`,`entity_type`,`status`; `["from and to must be supplied together."]`; `["Times must be ISO 8601 datetimes with an explicit timezone."]` (also catches impossible dates, `ovw/windows.py:8-14`); `["Window must be positive, at most 366 days, and not in the future."]`; `["Unknown, duplicate, or excessive metric keys."]`. Unlike observability, repeated params are rejected. Unlike observability, ovw wraps `parse_datetime` in try/except so impossible dates give 400, not 500.

Route-resolution note: route order inside `ovw/urls.py` has no ambiguity (`entities/...` vs `evidence/...` vs literal names).

#### 2.5 Overview response schemas

Window object `bounds` (`ovw/windows.py:51-54`): `{start, end, previous_start, previous_end, period, timezone, bounds:"[start,end)", comparison_mode:"PREVIOUS_EQUAL_DURATION"(when from/to given)|"PREVIOUS_CALENDAR_PERIOD"}`. Default window = last completed business-calendar period in `Business.timezone` (DAY, WEEK starting `week_start` (0=Monday), MONTH, ROLLING_7D/30D ending at local midnight).

V-SUM (`ovw/views.py:156-178`): `{business_id, as_of, window, business_status, health(V-HEALTH minus window), kpis:[card], issues:[V-ISSUE<=10], recommendations:[V-REC<=10], recent_activity:[V-ACT<=5], attention:[...], summary:"Business health is <status lowercased>; N prominent active issues and M current recommendations shown.", limits:{issues:10,recommendations:10,recent_activity:5}, links:{summary,kpis,trends,health,issues,recommendations,activity,cross-domain,configuration,catalog -> "/api/businesses/<id>/overview/<key>/"}}`. `business_status` is `REQUIRES_ATTENTION` when health status is CRITICAL or WARNING; else health `trend` if `DECLINING`/`IMPROVING`; else health status (`HEALTHY`/`UNKNOWN`). `attention[]` sorted by `priority` (stable): `{kind:"ISSUE",id,priority:1(CRITICAL)|2,title,drilldown}`, `{kind:"HEALTH_CHANGE",priority:2,status,trend}` (only if trend is DECLINING or MIXED), `{kind:"KPI_DEVIATION",priority:3,key,drilldown}` (AVAILABLE card with comparison.assessment DECLINING), `{kind:"RECOMMENDATION",id,priority:4,title,drilldown}` (priority CRITICAL/HIGH), `{kind:"ACTIVITY",priority:5,id,drilldown}` (severity CRITICAL/HIGH). Issues/recommendations/activity in the summary are NOT affected by `metric`/`period` filters; health inputs ignore `metric`/`dimension_*` filters (`ovw/views.py:150-153`).

KPI card (`ovw/metrics.py:104-123`): `{key, name, domain, unit, value_type, status, reason, value(decimal string|null), snapshot_id, snapshot:{id,value,period_start,period_end,period_type,calculated_at,calculation_rule,dimension_type,dimension_value}|null, comparison:{previous_value,delta,percent_change,direction,assessment,reason,change_unit,mode,reference_value}, previous_snapshot_id, sources:[{connection_id,entity_type,status,complete,coverage_at,reason}], freshness:{age_seconds,ttl_seconds}, goal, rule, drilldown(snapshot evidence href|null)}`. `status`: `UNAVAILABLE` (no exact BI snapshot for the window), `STALE` (older than `snapshot_ttl_seconds` or a configured source is STALE/DELAYED), `AVAILABLE`; a configured source that is neither FRESH nor STALE/DELAYED forces `UNAVAILABLE`. Values/deltas are decimal STRINGS. Only KPIs configured (default nine, see ENUMS) or named in `metric` are returned; `domain` filter / `config.domains` can drop cards.

V-TRENDS (`ovw/metrics.py:137-158`): `{results:[{key,domain,unit,points:[snapshot],status,comparison}], range_start, range_end, truncated, point_limit:5000, window, as_of}`; default range = last 30 days (DAY) else 366 days, or `[from,to]` when supplied; `status` in `AVAILABLE`/`UNAVAILABLE`/`OVERLAPPING_WINDOWS`.

V-HEALTH (`ovw/health.py:41-88`): `{status, domains:{<configured domain>:{status, evidence:[<=20], trend}}, evidence:[<=20 global], evidence_limit:20, trend, trend_basis:"UPSTREAM_ISSUE_TRENDS_AND_COMPARABLE_KPI_MOVEMENT", rules_version(config.version), as_of, reliability:O-SUM, window}`. Evidence items: issue `{issue_id,severity,status,reason:"ACTIVE_BI_ISSUE",drilldown}`; metric `{metric,snapshot_id,status,reason,rule,goal,drilldown,severity}`. No numeric score by design.

V-ISSUE (`ovw/findings.py:65-87`): `{id,title,type,severity,status,confidence(str),detected_at,updated_at,resolved_at,domains:[],trend,trend_source,entity,drilldown,rule_key,context:{region?,courier?,customer_segment?,campaign?,product?},evidence_summary:[<=3] (list only)}`; detail adds `description, evidence[<=50], evidence_limit:50, graph, recommendations:[{id,title,status,drilldown}<=20]` (the detail's `recommendations` are only ACTIVE, non-expired ones, `ovw/findings.py:83-84`). `trend` is the raw BI value `IMPROVING|WORSENING|STABLE|UNKNOWN`. Default list = status in `OPEN NEW ACKNOWLEDGED ONGOING IN_PROGRESS` AND `resolved_at IS NULL` AND severity in configured `issue_severities`; passing `status` removes the resolved_at/status default; passing `severity` overrides the configured severity filter. Order: severity rank CRITICAL>HIGH>MEDIUM>LOW>INFO (unknown last), `-detected_at`, `-pk`.

V-REC (`ovw/findings.py:90-118`): `{id,title,type,priority,status,confidence|null,effective_status,generated_at,expires_at,expired,issue:{kind:"issues",id,drilldown,title,status,severity}|null,goals:[str<=255 x<=20],suggested_action_type,drilldown,evidence_summary:[<=3],action_summary:[{id,type,status}<=3]}` (last two only in list); detail adds `description, rationale, evidence[<=50], evidence_limit, graph, actions:[{id,type,status,target(entity),preview(redacted action_payload),approved_at,executed_at,is_simulated}<=20]`. Default list = status in `SUGGESTED UNDER_REVIEW APPROVED` AND not expired AND priority in configured `recommendation_priorities`. `status=EXPIRED` also returns active-status rows whose `expires_at <= now`. There is NO approve/reject/execute action in this module.

V-ACT (`ovw/activity.py:44-89`): item `{id("<Model>:<kind>:<pk>"), occurred_at, domain, event, severity, source(model name), basis("RECORDED_EVENT"|"LATEST_STATE_OBSERVATION"), entity:{type,id}, drilldown}`. Single SQL UNION, newest first (`-activity_at`, `activity_id`). Default window = last 30 days to now. `severity`: BusinessIssue.severity, AiRecommendation.priority, else `HIGH` for events in `FAILED DELIVERY_FAILED RETURNED ESCALATED REJECTED PAYMENT_FAILED`, else `INFO`. Drilldown: issue/recommendation -> `.../overview/issues|recommendations/<pk>/`, others -> `.../overview/entities/<type>/<pk>/`.

V-XDOM (`ovw/cross_domain.py:5-33`): `{results:[{key,domains:[..],status:"NO_VERIFIED_FINDING"|"REQUIRES_ATTENTION",issues:[V-ISSUE + evidence]}] (one per key in config.cross_domain), as_of, truncated, issue_limit:500, basis:"BI_SCENARIO_TAG_AND_DETERMINISTIC_RELATIONSHIP_EVIDENCE"}`. `NO_VERIFIED_FINDING` does not mean healthy.

V-CFG (`ovw/configuration.py:97-106`): `{period, snapshot_ttl_seconds, domains, kpis:[{key,direction,...rule fields as strings}], issue_severities, recommendation_priorities, cross_domain, inherit_reporting_period, comparison_mode, version, updated_at, updated_by_id, week_start}`. Defaults when no row (`ovw/configuration.py:14-17`): period `DAY`, ttl 172800, all 7 domains, 9 default KPIs (direction NEUTRAL), all severities/priorities, all 9 scenarios, inherit_reporting_period true, comparison_mode `PREVIOUS_PERIOD`, version 0, updated_at/updated_by_id null. When `inherit_reporting_period` is true, `period` is overridden by `ConfigurationRecord(kind=GENERAL,key=default).data.reporting_period` (default `DAY`); `week_start` is read from the same record and is READ-ONLY (a PUT containing `week_start`, `updated_at` or `updated_by_id` fails with `{"unknown":[...]}`). Saving `period` without `inherit_reporting_period` flips inherit to false (`ovw/configuration.py:125-128`). Lists are replaced wholesale, scalars merged.

V-CAT (`ovw/views.py:127-132`): `{results:[{key, domain(null possible for a custom metric with unrecognised category), name, registered(bool: an active BusinessMetric exists), selected(bool: currently in config.kpis)}]}`.

V-ENT (`ovw/views.py:206-214`, `ovw/evidence.py:89-108`): `{type,id,domain,available:true,label,drilldown,status,updated_at, graph:{nodes:[<=10],edges:[<=50]}, as_of}`. `label` fields: order `order_number`, customer `display_name`, product `name`, product_variant `name`, campaign `name`, conversation `subject`, shipment `tracking_code`; payment/refund/call/website_session/website_visitor -> `"<type> #<id>"`. Raw provider payloads, contacts, transcripts are never included.

V-EVID (`ovw/evidence.py:133-155,208-226`): `{kind, id, drilldown, ...kind-specific, as_of}`: snapshots add snapshot fields + `metric`(key),`unit`,`category`,`domain`; facts add `statement,confidence,detected_at,rule_key,status,evidence[<=50]`; relationships add `relationship_type,source(entity),target(entity),confidence,relationship_source,rule_key`; nodes add `label,entity`; edges add `relationship_type,source_node_id,target_node_id,business_relationship_id`; knowledge add `document_id,document_title,excerpt(<=800 chars)`.

Evidence item (`ovw/evidence.py:157-182`): `{id,type,description,weight|null,references:[{kind,id,drilldown,...}],entity}`; items whose references are not tenant-owned/valid are silently dropped (so counts can be smaller than stored rows).

#### 2.6 Cross-references: `app/configuration/urls.py` re-uses these views (also UNMOUNTED)

ORIGINAL PREFIX for the configuration URLconf (from fd41ea7): `businesses/<int:business_id>/configuration/`. Rows below are the ONLY configuration routes that execute observability/overview code (`cfg/urls.py:3-4,10,13,16`):

| Method | Path relative to `.../configuration/` | View (module) | Behaviour | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| GET, PUT | `monitoring/` | `overview.views.ConfigurationView` with kwarg `resource='configuration'` | identical to `overview/configuration/` (V-CFG; PUT body as in 2.4) | `cfg/urls.py:10` |
| GET, PUT | `freshness/` | `observability.views.PolicyView` | identical to `observability/policies/` (O-POL; paginated GET with `connection`,`entity_type`,`page`,`page_size`; PUT upsert). NAME CLASH: under `observability/` the word `freshness/` is the freshness PROJECTION (O-FRESH), under `configuration/` it is the POLICY resource | `cfg/urls.py:13` |
| POST | `connections/<int:pk>/test/` | `observability.views.OperationView` with kwargs `resource='connections', action='connection_test'` | identical to `observability/connections/<id>/test/` (2.3) | `cfg/urls.py:16` |

Related, not duplicates: `cfg/urls.py:11` `monitoring/history/` (`MonitoringHistoryView`, `cfg/views.py:207-213`) lists `MonitoringConfigurationRevision` rows `{version, settings, actor_id, created_at}` written by overview `save_configuration`; `cfg/urls.py:12` `freshness/<int:pk>/history/` shows revisions of the `ConfigurationRecord(kind=FRESHNESS, key=<policy id>)` written by `PolicyView.put`.
Write-side cross effects: `PolicyView.put` calls `app.configuration.services.record_native_change(..., 'FRESHNESS', str(policy.pk), ...)` (`obs/views.py:282-284`; `cfg/services.py:79-89`), creating a ConfigurationRecord + revision + ConfigurationEvent; overview `save_configuration` writes `MonitoringConfiguration` (+`MonitoringConfigurationRevision`) and a `ConfigurationEvent(section='MONITORING', key='default')` via `cfg/services.py:27-29` (visible through `configuration/changes/`). `cfg/views.py:9-10` imports `require_access` and `Pagination` from observability, so configuration shares the BusinessAccess model and page-size rules.
Reverse dependencies: Overview consumes observability in-process, never over HTTP: `ovw/metrics.py:8,77` (`connection_snapshots`) feeds KPI `sources[]` statuses, `ovw/health.py:4` (`reliability_summary`, used at `:87`) feeds `health.reliability`.

#### 2.7 LIVE neighbours that look related but are NOT these modules

- `GET businesses/<int:business_id>/overview/` is LIVE (`app/urls.py:91` -> `domain.OverviewView`, `app/domain_views.py:119-121` -> `projections.overview`, `app/domains/projections.py:49-54`): returns `{business, canonical_counts, recent_activity, intelligence, business_health: unavailable('Overview & Monitoring')}`; authorised via BusinessMembership. `ovw/urls.py` defines no empty route, so re-including `app.overview.urls` at the same prefix creates no duplicate path (checked by comparing the two lists), but the exact URL `.../overview/` (no suffix) would keep returning the domain placeholder. `app/domains/projections.py:46` also hard-codes `'freshness': unavailable('Integration & Data Observability')`.
- `POST businesses/<id>/contracts/monitoring/freshness/` is LIVE (`app/urls.py:122`; `app/domain_views.py:344-352`, `app/domains/boundaries.py:9`): a simulated owner-contract stub, unrelated to the observability API. Not analysed further.
- `ops/business/<id>/overview/` (`app/operations_urls.py`, `app/operations_ui.py:18,108`) is a server-rendered HTML page, not JSON.
- Generic LIVE resources `businesses/<id>/<slug:name>/` and `.../<slug:name>/<int:pk>/` (`app/urls.py:99-100`) are catch-alls; none of the observability/overview relative routes (always >=2 segments after the prefix, second segment non-numeric) would be shadowed by them (reasoned from the patterns, NOT RUN).

#### 2.8 SERVICE-ONLY functions (no HTTP route; trusted-code entry points)

| Function | Purpose / contract | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `record_observation(business_id, connection_id, key, kind, data, observed_at=None, received_at=None)` | Atomic, idempotent write of one observation (kinds: `connection_check`, `provider_request`, `sync`, `failed_record`, `webhook`, `data_confirmed`, `integration_error`); `(connection,key)` unique, same key with different content raises ValidationError; locks connection row | `obs/services.py:19-20,300-365` |
| `publish_after_commit(**observation)` | `transaction.on_commit` -> `dispatch(DomainEvent('integration.observation', observation))`; handler registered in `AppConfig.ready()` | `obs/services.py:368-375`; `obs/handlers.py:1-10`; `app/apps.py:7-9` |
| `capture_payload(connection_id, key, body, *, signature_verified=False, received_at=None, source_at=None)` | Stores immutable provider payload (<=256 KiB JSON object), the only way `payload_id`s come to exist | `app/integrations/payloads.py:12-38` |
| `register_adapter(provider_key, factory)` | Registers a live provider adapter; NEVER called in the repo, so only `is_mock=True` connections can execute operations | `app/integrations/operations.py:86-88` |
| `transition_record`, `execute_operation`, `save_configuration`, `configuration()` | Business logic behind PATCH / POST operations / PUT+GET configuration (already described) | `obs/lifecycle.py:12`, `obs/operations.py:134`, `ovw/configuration.py:97,115` |
| management commands `simulate_observability --seed N --at ISO [--business-slug obs-demo-*]`, `simulate_overview --seed N --at ISO [--business-slug overview-demo-*]` | Create `obs-demo-*` / `overview-demo-*` businesses, users with MANAGER BusinessAccess and fixtures, then validate through the real views | `app/management/commands/simulate_observability.py`, `simulate_overview.py`; `obs/demo.py`, `ovw/demo.py` |

There is NO HTTP endpoint to ingest observations, webhooks, payloads or sync logs, to register providers, to create BusinessAccess grants, or to trigger a brand-new sync (only retry of an existing PARTIAL/FAILED one). Production code has no producer of observation data: the only callers of `record_observation` are the operations module and the two demo modules (grep). On a real deployment every observability collection is therefore empty until an integration owner publishes observations. Connection CRUD is a `configuration/connections/` concern (`cfg/urls.py:14`), not observability.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------
### 3. ENUMS (exact strings; source in parentheses)

#### Observability
- Connection lifecycle `status` filter/values (free-text DB column, default `CONNECTED`): `CONNECTED`, `DISCONNECTED`, `DISABLED`, `EXPIRED`, `SYNCING`, `ERROR` (`obs/views.py:84`; `app/models.py` IntegrationConnection). Only `DISABLED`/`DISCONNECTED` block operations (`obs/operations.py:156`); `EXPIRED` forces health ERROR (`obs/selectors.py:122`).
- `health.status`: `UNKNOWN`, `HEALTHY`, `DEGRADED`, `ERROR`. `health.reason`: `NO_RECENT_OBSERVATION`, the raw latest outcome (`SUCCESS`, `DEGRADED`, `ERROR`, `AUTH_ERROR`, `EXPIRED`, `RATE_LIMIT`, `TIMEOUT`, `PROVIDER_ERROR`, `CONNECTIVITY_ERROR`), `HIGH_LATENCY`, `ELEVATED_REQUEST_ERROR_RATE`, `RATE_LIMIT_APPROACHING`, `RATE_LIMIT_EXHAUSTED`, `WEBHOOK_PROCESSING_FAILURES`, `FAILED_RECORDS`, `SYNC_FAILURES`, `SYNC_STALLED`, `CREDENTIAL_EXPIRED`, and (when disabled/disconnected) `DISABLED`/`DISCONNECTED` (`obs/selectors.py:80-148`). `health.authentication` / `credential_status`: `USABLE`, `INVALID`, `EXPIRED`, `UNKNOWN` (`obs/models.py:89-90`; initial `UNKNOWN`).
- `summary_status` / O-SUM `status`: `ERROR`, `DEGRADED`, `STALE`, `DISCONNECTED`, `UNKNOWN`, `HEALTHY` (`obs/selectors.py:165`). `summary_reason`: any health reason, or `EXPECTED_UPDATE_OVERDUE`, `INCOMPLETE_OR_DELAYED_DATA`, `NO_CONFIRMED_DATA`, or the lifecycle status for DISABLED/DISCONNECTED (`:136-148`).
- `rate_limit.status`: `UNKNOWN`, `AVAILABLE`, `APPROACHING` (remaining 1-10), `EXHAUSTED` (remaining 0 or outcome RATE_LIMIT) (`obs/selectors.py:98-104`). `quota_scope`: `CONNECTION`, `PROVIDER` (`obs/models.py:110`).
- Freshness `status`: `FRESH`, `DELAYED`, `STALE`, `UNAVAILABLE`; `reason`: `NO_CONFIRMED_DATA`, `FUTURE_WATERMARK`, `WITHIN_EXPECTATION`, `EXPECTED_UPDATE_OVERDUE` (`obs/policies.py:31-41`). Thresholds: age <= delayed -> FRESH; <= stale -> DELAYED; else STALE.
- Provider `status`: `UNAVAILABLE` (failure ratio >= 0.8), `DEGRADED`, `HEALTHY`, `UNKNOWN`; `reason`: `INSUFFICIENT_SCOPED_EVIDENCE`, `PROVIDER_REQUEST_FAILURES`, `ERROR_LATENCY_OR_RATE_LIMIT`, `WITHIN_EXPECTATION`; `evidence_scope`: `AGGREGATED_PROVIDER` (`obs/policies.py:59-68`; `obs/selectors.py:198`).
- Sync `status`: `PENDING`, `RUNNING`, `COMPLETED`, `PARTIAL`, `FAILED` (`obs/views.py:127`; `obs/services.py:166`). `sync_type`: `FULL`, `INCREMENTAL`. `trigger`: `MANUAL`, `SCHEDULED`, `REPLAY`, `RETRY` (`obs/serializers.py:120-121`). `counter_schema`: `V1`, `LEGACY` (`obs/serializers.py:21-25`; `obs/services.py:171`). Allowed transitions on write path: PENDING -> PENDING/RUNNING/FAILED; RUNNING -> RUNNING/COMPLETED/PARTIAL/FAILED (`obs/services.py:174`).
- FailedRecord `status`: `OPEN`, `REVIEWING`, `RETRYING`, `RESOLVED`, `IGNORED` (`obs/models.py:184`; `RETRYING` is never assigned by any code, grep). `stage`: `RETRIEVAL`, `MAPPING`, `NORMALIZATION`, `VALIDATION`, `IDENTITY_INPUT`, `PERSISTENCE`, `ROUTING` (`obs/models.py:176-177`). `resolution_method`: `""`, `MANUAL`, `PROCESSING`. `group_by`: `code`, `stage`, `entity_type`.
- Webhook `validation`: `UNKNOWN`, `VALID`, `INVALID`; `status`: `RECEIVED`, `ROUTING`, `PROCESSED`, `FAILED`, `REJECTED` (`obs/models.py:149-150`); `latency_reason`: `NO_PROVIDER_TIME`, `CLOCK_SKEW`, `AVAILABLE` (`obs/serializers.py:78-83`).
- Error `category`: `AUTHENTICATION`, `CONNECTIVITY`, `PROVIDER_API`, `RATE_LIMIT`, `WEBHOOK`, `SYNC`, `MAPPING`, `NORMALIZATION`, `VALIDATION`, `INTERNAL`; `severity`: `LOW`, `MEDIUM`, `HIGH`, `CRITICAL`; `status`: `ACTIVE`, `ACKNOWLEDGED`, `RESOLVED`, `IGNORED` (`obs/models.py:123-133`). System-generated codes seen in code: `AUTHENTICATION_FAILED`, `CONNECTION_DEGRADED`, `CONNECTION_FAILED` (`obs/services.py:329-331`), `SYNC_PARTIAL`, `SYNC_FAILED` (`:228`), `WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_INVALID`, `WEBHOOK_PROCESSING_FAILED` (`:272`), `AUTH_ERROR`/`RATE_LIMIT`/`TIMEOUT`/`PROVIDER_ERROR`/`CONNECTIVITY_ERROR` (provider-request outcomes used as code, `:349`), `INTERNAL_PROCESSING_ERROR`.
- Lifecycle transitions (PATCH): errors `ACTIVE -> ACKNOWLEDGED|RESOLVED|IGNORED`, `ACKNOWLEDGED -> RESOLVED|IGNORED`, `RESOLVED -> ACTIVE`, `IGNORED -> ACTIVE`; failed records `OPEN -> REVIEWING|RESOLVED|IGNORED`, `REVIEWING -> OPEN|RESOLVED|IGNORED`, `RESOLVED -> OPEN`, `IGNORED -> OPEN|REVIEWING`; `RESOLVED`/`IGNORED` require a non-blank `note` (`obs/lifecycle.py:23-31`). New failures reopen RESOLVED errors/records automatically (`obs/services.py:129-131,158-160`).
- OperationAttempt `action`: `connection_test`, `sync_retry`, `record_retry`, `webhook_replay`; `status`: `RUNNING`, `SUCCEEDED`, `FAILED`, `UNSUPPORTED` (`obs/operations.py:21-22`; `obs/models.py:211`). Attempt `result.code` seen: `UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY`, `INVALID_OPERATION_RESULT`, `INTERNAL_PROCESSING_ERROR`, `INVALID_PROCESSING_RESULT`, `UNCONFIRMED_CANONICAL_RESULT`, `PROVIDER_TIMEOUT`, `MISSING_EXTERNAL_ID`, `FUTURE_SOURCE_TIME`, `INVALID_PRODUCT`; `result.stage` also includes `INTERNAL`.
- Audit rows (`history[]`): `{action, actor_id, from_status, to_status, note, created_at}`; `action` values: `TRANSITION`, `REOPEN`, `PROCESSING_RESOLVED`, `DIAGNOSTIC_READ`, and the four operation names (the latter carry the attempt status in `to_status`).
- Sort keys: connections `id name status created_at updated_at` (default `id`); syncs `id started_at status`; failed-records/errors `id last_seen_at status`; webhooks `id received_at status`; prefix `-` for descending; default `-id`; secondary key always `pk` (`obs/views.py:90-93,135-138`).

#### Overview
- Domains (`ovw/catalog.py:2`): `sales`, `customers`, `marketing`, `website`, `support`, `delivery`, `payments`; `activity` adds `calling`, `issues`, `ai` (`ovw/views.py:90`).
- Catalog metric keys by domain (`ovw/catalog.py:3-13`): sales `total_revenue realized_revenue revenue_growth order_count average_order_value completed_orders cancelled_orders`; payments `refunded_revenue successful_payments failed_payments`; customers `new_customers repeat_customers repeat_purchase_rate purchase_frequency`; marketing `marketing_spend marketing_conversions ctr cpc roas attributable_revenue`; website `website_sessions website_visitors website_conversion_rate cart_abandonment_rate checkout_completion_rate`; support `conversation_count unresolved_conversations response_time resolution_time escalations`; delivery `delivery_success_rate delivery_failure_rate return_rate average_delivery_time`. Default selected KPIs (`:15-16`): `total_revenue realized_revenue order_count new_customers marketing_spend website_conversion_rate unresolved_conversations delivery_failure_rate refunded_revenue`. Business-defined active BusinessMetric keys are also accepted.
- Periods: `DAY`, `WEEK`, `MONTH`, `ROLLING_7D`, `ROLLING_30D`. `comparison_mode`: `PREVIOUS_PERIOD`, `TARGET`, `BASELINE`, `NONE`. KPI `direction` (rule): `HIGHER`, `LOWER`, `NEUTRAL`; `warning_severity`: `LOW MEDIUM HIGH`; `critical_severity`: `HIGH CRITICAL` (`ovw/configuration.py:36-47,62-71`).
- Severities/priorities (`ovw/catalog.py:35`): `CRITICAL`, `HIGH`, `MEDIUM`, `LOW`, `INFO`.
- Issue statuses (`:31-32`): `OPEN NEW ACKNOWLEDGED ONGOING IN_PROGRESS` (active) + `RESOLVED DISMISSED`. Recommendation statuses (`:33-34`): `SUGGESTED UNDER_REVIEW APPROVED` (active) + `REJECTED EXECUTED ACTIONED COMPLETED EXPIRED DISMISSED`; response `effective_status` may be `EXPIRED` for an active status past `expires_at`.
- Cross-domain scenario keys (domains) (`:17-27`): `marketing_revenue`(marketing,sales), `marketing_delivery`(marketing,delivery), `website_sales`(website,sales), `product_support`(sales,support), `product_delivery`(sales,delivery), `customer_retention`(customers), `support_customer_outcome`(support,customers), `courier_revenue`(delivery,sales), `campaign_customer_quality`(marketing,customers). Result `status`: `NO_VERIFIED_FINDING`, `REQUIRES_ATTENTION`.
- Entity types (`ovw/evidence.py:10-16`, domain via `ovw/catalog.py:28-30`): `order`, `customer`, `product`, `product_variant`, `payment`, `refund`, `campaign`, `conversation`, `shipment`, `call`, `website_session`, `website_visitor`. The `entity_type` FILTER on issues/activity additionally accepts `issue`, `recommendation` (`ovw/views.py:91`). Evidence kinds (`ovw/evidence.py:18-19`): `facts`, `snapshots`, `relationships`, `nodes`, `edges`, `knowledge`; reference kinds also include `issues`.
- Health `status` (also per domain): `CRITICAL`, `WARNING`, `HEALTHY`, `UNKNOWN`; `reason`: `NO_USABLE_METRIC`, `CRITICAL_THRESHOLD`, `WARNING_THRESHOLD`, `OUTSIDE_GOAL_RANGE`, `WITHIN_CONFIGURED_RANGE`, `ADVERSE_PERIOD_MOVEMENT`, `EXPECTED_PERIOD_MOVEMENT`, `NO_CONFIGURED_HEALTH_RULE`, `ACTIVE_BI_ISSUE` (`ovw/health.py:9-30,48`). Health `trend`: `MIXED`, `DECLINING`, `IMPROVING`, `STABLE`, `UNKNOWN` (`:91-95`). Issue `trend` (raw BI): `IMPROVING`, `WORSENING`, `STABLE`, `UNKNOWN` (`ovw/findings.py:66-69`), mapped `WORSENING -> DECLINING` only inside health (`ovw/health.py:55`).
- `business_status`: `REQUIRES_ATTENTION`, `DECLINING`, `IMPROVING`, `HEALTHY`, `UNKNOWN` (`ovw/views.py:171-172`). `attention[].kind`: `ISSUE`, `HEALTH_CHANGE`, `KPI_DEVIATION`, `RECOMMENDATION`, `ACTIVITY`.
- KPI card `status`: `UNAVAILABLE`, `STALE`, `AVAILABLE`; `reason`: `NO_EXACT_BI_SNAPSHOT`, `CURRENT_BI_SNAPSHOT`, `DATA_OVERDUE_OR_UNCONFIRMED`; `comparison.direction`: `UP`, `DOWN`, `FLAT`, `UNKNOWN`; `comparison.assessment`: `STABLE`, `NEUTRAL`, `IMPROVING`, `DECLINING`, `UNKNOWN`; `comparison.reason`: `MISSING_COMPARABLE_PERIOD`, `COMPARABLE_PERIODS`, `ZERO_BASELINE`, `COMPARISON_DISABLED`, `TARGET_REFERENCE`, `BASELINE_REFERENCE`, `MISSING_TARGET_REFERENCE`, `MISSING_BASELINE_REFERENCE`; `comparison.mode`: as `comparison_mode`; `change_unit`: `PERCENTAGE_POINTS` for unit `%`/`PERCENT`/`PERCENTAGE`, else the metric unit (`ovw/metrics.py:16-31,125-135`). `sources[].status`: `INCOMPLETE`, `UNAVAILABLE`, or a freshness status (`FRESH DELAYED STALE UNAVAILABLE`); `sources[].reason` includes `NO_ACTIVE_CONFIRMED_SOURCE`. Trend series `status`: `AVAILABLE`, `UNAVAILABLE`, `OVERLAPPING_WINDOWS`.
- Activity `basis`: `RECORDED_EVENT`, `LATEST_STATE_OBSERVATION`; `event` literals (from `=` entries, `ovw/activity.py:11-38`): `ORDER_CREATED PAYMENT_CREATED PAYMENT_PAID REFUND CUSTOMER_CREATED CONVERSATION_OPENED CONVERSATION_RESOLVED CONVERSATION_ASSIGNED SHIPMENT_CREATED CAMPAIGN_CREATED CALL_CREATED ISSUE_DETECTED RECOMMENDATION_GENERATED ACTION_CREATED ACTION_APPROVED ACTION_EXECUTED`; other `event` values are copied from source status/event_type columns (order status, timeline/shipment/call/website event types, campaign conversion types, current status strings), so they are open-ended.
- Configuration conflict: HTTP 409.

### 5. FRONTEND NOTES

Availability and access
1. Nothing in sections 2.1/2.4/2.6 resolves today. With the includes missing, `/api/v1/businesses/<id>/observability/...` and `/api/v1/businesses/<id>/overview/<resource>/` match no pattern in `app/urls.py` (reasoned from the pattern list; NOT RUN). Ask backend to restore `include('app.observability.urls')` at `businesses/<int:business_id>/observability/` and `include('app.overview.urls')` at `businesses/<int:business_id>/overview/` (and configuration at `.../configuration/`) in `app/urls.py`; Overview must keep exactly that prefix because payload links are hard-coded.
2. Authorization is `BusinessAccess` (READER/MANAGER) or superuser, not `BusinessMembership`. No endpoint returns the caller's role or grants access (admin only). Plan for 403 `{"detail":"No permission for this business operation."}` on every route for a legitimately logged-in member who lacks a grant, and for logged-out users (403, not 401). Show write controls (retry/replay/test, lifecycle PATCH, policy PUT, configuration PUT) only to managers; there is no API to learn that, so the UI will have to infer it from a 403 or get it from another source.
3. Auth transport is DRF Session or Basic only (no token/JWT). Cookie-session clients must send `X-CSRFToken` on every POST/PUT/PATCH. No CORS app is installed (ground truth #2).
4. Every observability collection is empty until a trusted producer publishes observations (no ingestion endpoint, no worker; only demo commands and the operations themselves write). Build explicit empty states. With no data: `summary/` -> `status:"UNKNOWN", connection_count:0`; `freshness/` -> `count:0`; Overview KPI cards -> `status:"UNAVAILABLE", value:null`.

Pagination / filtering / ordering
5. Paginated (envelope `{count,next,previous,results}`, default 25, `page_size` 1-100): obs `connections/ syncs/ failed-records/ failed-records/groups/ webhooks/ freshness/ providers/ errors/ policies/`; ovw `issues/ recommendations/ activity/` (+ top-level `as_of`). NOT paginated: obs `summary/ syncs/statistics/` and every detail; ovw `summary kpis trends health cross-domain catalog configuration` and details. Use the `next` URL verbatim. Page past the end -> 404 `{"detail":"Invalid page."}`.
6. `freshness/` pages CONNECTIONS (25 default) and then flattens entity rows; `count` = number of connections; `entity_type`/`status` filters are applied after paging, so a page can return fewer rows than `page_size` or even `results: []` with `next` non-null. Keep requesting `next` until null, or filter client-side from `connections[].freshness`.
7. Observability: unknown or resource-inappropriate query keys -> 400; repeated keys silently take the last value; `from`/`to` independent, both bounds INCLUSIVE, applied to a resource-specific time column (connections `created_at`, syncs `started_at` (PENDING runs have none and drop out of ranged queries), failed-records/errors `last_seen_at`, webhooks `received_at`); `ordering` allowed keys are listed in section 3; enum filters (`severity`, `sync_type`, `trigger`, `active`, `group_by`, `scope`) are validated. Overview: unknown/repeated keys -> 400; `from` and `to` must come together, `to` <= now, span <= 366 days, interval half-open `[from,to)`; detail routes reject ANY query string.
8. Timestamps must carry an explicit offset (`Z` recommended; encode `+` as `%2B`).

Operations (connection test, sync retry, failed-record retry, webhook replay)
9. They are synchronous. A 200 response means "attempt recorded", NOT "operation succeeded": read `status` (`SUCCEEDED`/`FAILED`) and `result`. HTTP 409 means `status:"UNSUPPORTED"` (no adapter / no stored payload / non-PRODUCT entity). All preconditions fail with 400 and a bare message list; there is no error code. Send a fresh `idempotency_key` per user action (<=200 chars); re-sending the same key returns the stored attempt (same HTTP status), different action/target with that key -> 400.
10. Today only `is_mock=true` connections (model default) can execute anything, and only `PRODUCT` payloads (sync retry also needs hidden `metadata.payload_ids`; the API's `metadata` output is filtered to `correlation_id/reason/is_mock`, so the UI cannot tell in advance whether a sync is retryable). Every non-mock connection returns 409 UNSUPPORTED.
11. Button gating you CAN derive: sync retry only when `status` is `PARTIAL`/`FAILED`; record retry only when `status` is `OPEN`/`REVIEWING` and `code != WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE_INVALID`; webhook replay only when `replay_eligible` is true; none when the connection `lifecycle_status` is `DISABLED`/`DISCONNECTED`. After a FAILED attempt wait until `next_retry_at`; the retry cap is `max_retries` (default 3) counted across all non-UNSUPPORTED attempts for that target. Reload the target's detail (`attempts[]`, `history[]`) after any operation.
12. A sync retry processes every referenced payload inside the request (up to 1000); allow a long client timeout. (Inferred from `obs/operations.py:76-114`; no timing data.)

Writes
13. `PATCH errors|failed-records/<id>/` body `{status, note}`; legal transitions in section 3; `note` mandatory for `RESOLVED`/`IGNORED`; unknown id -> 400 (not 404); returns the bare record (no `attempts`/`history`).
14. `PUT policies/` (also `PUT configuration/freshness/`) is an upsert keyed by `(connection_id|null, entity_type|"")`; list with `GET policies/` first; response is 200 with the row but WITHOUT `updated_at`; numbers must be JSON integers (not strings, not booleans); an update only changes the keys you send; there is no DELETE.
15. `PUT overview/configuration/` (also `PUT configuration/monitoring/`): do NOT echo the GET payload back (`week_start`, `updated_at`, `updated_by_id` cause `{"unknown":[...]}` 400). Send `version` from the last GET to get optimistic locking (409 `{"detail":"Configuration version changed. Read the current version and retry."}`); omit `version` for a last-writer-wins merge. Submitted lists replace stored lists; send `kpis` as the full desired list. Sending `period` without `inherit_reporting_period` turns inheritance off. Decimal inputs may be strings or numbers; outputs are strings.
16. `GET payloads/<id>/` is a GET with a side effect (writes an audit row) and needs MANAGER; do not prefetch or poll it.

Rendering
17. Responses may contain the literal placeholders `[REDACTED]`, `[TRUNCATED]`, `[UNSUPPORTED]` (section 2.0) and strings cut at 2000 chars.
18. Overview numbers are decimal strings (`"123.450000"`); `null` + `status:"UNAVAILABLE"` means "no exact BI snapshot", never zero. Show `status`, `reason`, `freshness.age_seconds` and `sources[]` rather than computing client-side. `as_of` is server evaluation time; there is no historical as-of parameter.
19. Follow `drilldown` and `links` strings as given: they are absolute paths starting `/api/businesses/<id>/overview/...` (NOT `/api/v1/`). Entity objects with `available:false` have `drilldown:null`. `activity[].id` is a string (`"Order:created:12"`), all other ids are integers.
20. Vocabulary differences to map in the UI: issue `trend` uses `WORSENING`, health `trend` uses `DECLINING`; observability `summary_status` (`HEALTHY DEGRADED ERROR STALE DISCONNECTED UNKNOWN`) differs from health `status` (`CRITICAL WARNING HEALTHY UNKNOWN`) and from freshness `status` (`FRESH DELAYED STALE UNAVAILABLE`) and provider `status` (`HEALTHY DEGRADED UNAVAILABLE UNKNOWN`); connection `lifecycle_status` (`CONNECTED ...`) is separate from `health.status`.
21. `connections[].errors` is `{active,recent}` or `{connection_id,active,recent,last_failure}`; treat `last_failure` inside it as optional. `connections[].last_failure` is a timestamp only; fetch `errors/?connection=<id>` for detail.
22. Display zone: `business_timezone` (connections) and `window.timezone` (overview) are IANA names; `syncs/statistics/` buckets are in that zone.

### 6. GAPS AND AMBIGUITIES

#### 6.1 Structural
- G1 UNMOUNTED (section 1b). Three `include()` lines vanished in merge `396593f` (conflict resolved in favour of main's `app/urls.py`). The Overview hard-coded links make the prefix non-negotiable.
- G2 Migration graph (static read of each migration's `dependencies`; NOT RUN): three leaf nodes with no merge migration: `0004_business_configuration` (chain 0002_integration_data_observability -> 0003_overview_monitoring -> 0004_business_configuration, this scope), `0007_businessgoal_category_businessgoal_priority_and_more` (AI branch) and `0008_identity_confidence_unknown` (domains branch); no file depends on any of the three. Django normally refuses `migrate` with "Conflicting migrations" in that state, so the 13 observability tables and `monitoring_configurations*` tables may not be creatable until a merge migration exists. Ground-truth item 3 (settings/models import errors) is in addition.
- G3 `BusinessAccess` is a third tenancy model alongside `BusinessMembership` (domain views) and the BI/Graph `request.business` approach (ground truth #4). Nobody can grant it over HTTP.
- G4 No in-repo producer of observation data, no ingestion endpoint, no scheduler (grep + `integration-data-observability.md:245`). Observability is only demonstrable through the demo commands.
- G5 No adapter is ever registered (`register_adapter` never called), so live operations are all 409 today.
- G6 No consumer outside Overview/Configuration reads observability (grep): BI, Graph and AI do not use freshness, contradicting the spec's "protect BI / give AI freshness context" intent at integration level.
- G7 Overview "goal-aware" health uses KPI-rule `goal` strings and targets from monitoring configuration; the `BusinessGoal` table is not referenced (grep in `app/overview`, `app/observability`).

#### 6.2 Documentation vs code conflicts (owning engineer's notes)
- D1 `docs/implementations/integration-data-observability.md:118` ("Root: `/api/businesses/<business_id>/observability/`") and `overview-monitoring.md:34` describe live paths; neither is mounted on origin/main (G1).
- D2 `integration-data-observability.md:72` ("Callback errors propagate") and the docstring `obs/services.py:368-375` ("Exceptions propagate to the caller") conflict with `app/events/dispatcher.py:16-35` on origin/main: `_execute_dispatch` wraps every handler in `try/except Exception` and only logs (`logger.exception`), and `dispatch` re-defers through `on_commit` when already inside an atomic block. At commit 0a6d904 `dispatch` did propagate (`git show 0a6d904:app/events/dispatcher.py`); exception isolation was added by `e85f042` (a different branch, not an ancestor of 0a6d904) and now governs. A failing `handle_observation` (e.g. ValidationError) is therefore swallowed after commit.
- D3 `integration-data-observability.md:181` ("Unknown scoped objects return 404") holds for GET details only. PATCH of an unknown/foreign error or failed-record id and POST of any operation on an unknown/foreign target return **400** `["Lifecycle target does not belong to this business."]` / `["Operation target does not belong to this business."]` (`obs/lifecycle.py:19-20`, `obs/operations.py:145-146`).
- D4 `integration-data-observability.md:201` ("exponential backoff beginning at 30 seconds, capped at 300"): `next_retry_at = now + min(300, 30 * 2**previous.count())` is evaluated AFTER the new attempt row exists (`obs/operations.py:172,231`), so the first failure waits 60 s (then 120, 240, 300). Derived by code reading; NOT RUN; the test only asserts `next_retry_at is not None` (`test_observability_operations.py:74`).
- D5 `integration-data-observability.md:179` ("malformed ranges return 400"): `timestamp()` (`obs/services.py:34-39`) calls `parse_datetime` unguarded, and Django's `parse_datetime` raises `ValueError` for well-formed but impossible values such as `2026-02-30T00:00:00Z` (`django:utils/dateparse.py:104-129`); `BusinessView.handle_exception` converts only `DjangoValidationError` (`obs/views.py:52-55`). Expected result: HTTP 500 for `from`/`to` of that kind on every observability route that accepts them (NOT RUN). Garbage like `not-a-time` correctly yields 400 (`test_observability_api.py:107`). Overview guards this (`ovw/windows.py:8-14`).
- D6 `integration-data-observability.md:124` says `summary/` returns "grouped errors and failed events"; the code returns only counts (`failed_webhooks`, `active_error_groups`) plus `problems[]` (`obs/selectors.py:171-173`). No grouped-error list exists there.
- D7 `integration-data-observability.md:128` says statistics report "mean completed duration"; code averages `completed_at - started_at` over every run having both timestamps, any status (`obs/views.py:320-324`).
- D8 `integration-data-observability.md:177` omits that `freshness/` applies `entity_type`/`status` after pagination (G-F below).
- D9 `overview-monitoring.md:47` ("`GET issues/<id>/` ... associated stored recommendations"): only ACTIVE, non-expired recommendations are attached (<=20), not historical ones (`ovw/findings.py:83-84`).
- D10 `integration-data-observability-progress.md:156-172` (checks "PASS", 96 tests) and `overview-monitoring-progress.md:101-125` (147 tests, `manage.py check` clean) cannot hold on origin/main: unmounted routes (tests use the old prefix), broken settings/models imports (ground truth #3) and the three-leaf migration graph. Historical claims only.
- D11 Progress-doc traceability names `services.py: connection_check` / `provider_request` as functions; they are `kind` branches of `record_observation` (`obs/services.py:320-350`). Cosmetic.
- D12 No conflict found for: 403 for cross-business access, 400 on unknown/repeated overview filters, 409 on version conflict, hashing/redaction claims, 25/100 pagination, DRF default authentication.

#### 6.3 Behavioural oddities and unverified edges
- F-1 `freshness/` post-pagination filtering (section 5 item 6).
- F-2 `PATCH` on a failed record always overwrites `resolution` with the supplied note (and sets `resolution_method` to `""` unless RESOLVED -> `MANUAL`) even for OPEN/REVIEWING transitions (`obs/lifecycle.py:35-37`). Error records keep no resolution text; notes appear only in `history[]`.
- F-3 `RETRYING` (FailedRecord) is a legal filter/DB value no code ever sets or transitions out of.
- F-4 `syncs/statistics/`: runs with `started_at IS NULL` (PENDING) are included in totals but `TruncDay('started_at')` over NULL likely yields a bucket with `day: null` in `daily` (`obs/views.py:325-326`; inferred, NOT RUN).
- F-5 Retry cap counts SUCCEEDED attempts too (`obs/operations.py:165-168`), and an idempotency key consumed by an UNSUPPORTED or FAILED attempt cannot be reused for a fresh try (returns the stored attempt).
- F-6 `failed-records/` accepts `group_by` but ignores it; only `failed-records/groups/` uses it.
- F-7 `policies/` PUT: coerces `error_ratio` through model `full_clean` (string "0.5" probably accepted); `connection_id: null` = business-wide row; `entity_type: ""` = all entities; no way to delete a policy (admin forbids delete, `app/admin.py:21-22`).
- F-8 `summary/?status=` is the connection LIFECYCLE status, not the computed health.
- F-9 `catalog` `domain` may be `null` (custom BusinessMetric with unrecognised category, `ovw/catalog.py:38-41`); KPI/health logic drops such metrics when `config.domains`/`domain` filter excludes `None`.
- F-10 `health/` and `summary/` iterate ALL active BusinessIssue rows (`ovw/health.py:45-46`, `.iterator(chunk_size=500)` at line 46), unbounded; latency on large tenants is unmeasured.
- F-11 Activity `event` strings are open-ended (copied from source status/event_type columns); severity is only meaningful for issues/recommendations (their own severity/priority) and for the six `HIGH_EVENTS`.
- F-12 `entities/<str:entity_type>/<int:pk>/` returns 404 (not 400) for unknown entity types, so the UI cannot distinguish "bad type" from "not found".
- F-13 Observability errors and syncs carry `connection_id` only (no provider id/name/connection name); the UI must join with `connections/` (`provider_id`, `provider`, `name`).
- F-14 No endpoint lists OperationAttempts globally, raw ConnectionCheck/ProviderRequest/ObservationReceipt rows, or the audit trail beyond the latest 20 per target (admin-only, read-only).
- F-15 Overview `activity` default window is the last 30 days and `from`/`to` are capped at 366 days; older activity is unreachable.

#### 6.4 NOT VERIFIED
- Any runtime behaviour: nothing was executed; response bodies are derived from code, not captured.
- HTTP status of impossible-date `from`/`to` (D5), backoff arithmetic (D4), `day: null` bucket (F-4), 404 for unmatched URLs (section 5 item 1), shadowing analysis (section 2.7), and migration conflict (G2) are static inferences.
- Database-dependent behaviour (SQLite vs PostgreSQL ordering of UNIONs, NULLs in `ORDER BY`, `select_for_update(of=('self',))` on SQLite) was not examined.
- `app/configuration/*` internals other than the three cross-referenced views, and the LIVE `businesses/<id>/contracts/monitoring/freshness/` stub, were not analysed.
- NO ENDPOINT FOUND for: creating/editing/deleting connections inside observability (exists only as UNMOUNTED `configuration/connections/`), triggering a new sync, webhook/payload/observation ingestion, listing OperationAttempts or audit rows, reading the caller's `BusinessAccess` role, granting `BusinessAccess`, deleting policies, approving/rejecting/executing recommendation actions from Overview (live AI routes exist at `app/urls.py:25-30`, not analysed), listing available KPI dimension values, historical as-of reads, push/stream (websocket/SSE) delivery.

# Part D — Business Configuration and Prototype Simulation (UNMOUNTED / CLI only)

## scan_D: Business Configuration, Prototype Simulation, management/demo tooling

Checkout: <repo> at 5f176a2 (= origin/main). Read-only scan. Nothing executed except one standalone regex snippet (copy of SECRET_TEXT) and `git show|log|diff`. Line refs are `path:line` relative to the repo root unless prefixed `DRF:` (DRF 3.15.2 source read from the scratchpad venv, `rest_framework/...`).
"verified-by-reading" = derived from code, not executed (project cannot boot).

### 1. SCOPE

Covers (a) the Business Configuration API: `app/configuration/*` plus the views it borrows (`OperationView`, `PolicyView` from `app/observability/views.py`; `ConfigurationView` from `app/overview/views.py`), its models, selectors/services, secrets handling and authorization; (b) Prototype Simulation: whether any HTTP endpoint, command or script exists for registry/scenarios/seeds/events/payloads/flaws/expectations/validation; (c) `app/management/`, `scripts/`, `app/events/`, `app/integrations/`.

**Headline facts**
- Business Configuration = 29 URL patterns, ALL **UNMOUNTED** on origin/main. `app/urls.py` has no `include()`; the `include('app.configuration.urls')` line existed only in fd41ea7 and is absent from the merge 396593f (parents fd41ea7 [has the include] and e487b0b [lacks it]); verified with `git show <sha>:app/urls.py | grep -c configuration.urls` => fd41ea7:1, e487b0b:0, 396593f:0, 5f176a2:0, 41c5446:0 (`git diff fd41ea7 5f176a2 -- app/urls.py` shows the three include lines removed). `app/configuration/` is byte-identical between fd41ea7 and 5f176a2 (diff stat lists only events/handlers.py + 3 management commands).
- **ORIGINAL PREFIX (from fd41ea7)**: `path('businesses/<int:business_id>/configuration/', include('app.configuration.urls'))` in `app/urls.py` (`git show fd41ea7:app/urls.py` line 9), under `bizora/urls.py` `path('api/', include('app.urls'))` => original live URL `/api/businesses/{business_id}/configuration/<route>`. This is NOT the current live path.
- If re-mounted by adding the same line to `app/urls.py` (which `bizora/urls.py:22,25` mounts at both `api/` and `api/v1/`): `/api/businesses/{business_id}/configuration/<route>` AND `/api/v1/businesses/{business_id}/configuration/<route>`. Route collision with the generic `businesses/<int:business_id>/<slug:name>/...` domain patterns in app/urls.py looks impossible for these shapes (second segment after the id is non-numeric or the segment counts differ) but NOT VERIFIED by running the resolver.
- Prototype Simulation: **NO ENDPOINT FOUND** for any of its 11 feature headings. Only CLI demo runners + 3 unused-by-HTTP models + mock adapter classes exist (section 4B).
- Authorization for configuration uses `BusinessAccess` (roles READER/MANAGER), a different table from the `BusinessMembership` (OWNER/MANAGER/OPERATOR/VIEWER) used by the live domain APIs. Nothing in the API assigns BusinessAccess (only Django admin + demo runners). See 5/6.

### 2. ENDPOINT TABLE

Paths RELATIVE to `ORIGINAL PREFIX (from fd41ea7)` = `businesses/{business_id}/configuration/`. Composed if re-mounted under api/v1: `/api/v1/businesses/{business_id}/configuration/<Path>`. All rows Status = **UNMOUNTED** unless stated.

Legend
- **A-R** = authenticated AND (superuser OR `BusinessAccess(user,business,role in READER|MANAGER)`); **A-W** = authenticated AND (superuser OR role MANAGER). Rule: any method not in GET/HEAD/OPTIONS needs A-W. `app/observability/access.py:6-15`, applied in `app/configuration/views.py:23-27`, `app/overview/views.py:57-63`, `app/observability/views.py:47-50`. Services re-check (`services.py:94`, etc.).
- **PAGE** = `?page=<int>=1..` `&page_size=<1..100>` (default 25). Response `{"count","next","previous","results":[...]}` (DRF PageNumberPagination, `app/observability/views.py:35-38`; shape `DRF:pagination.py:230-236`; `next/previous` are absolute URLs). Out-of-range page => 404 `{"detail":"Invalid page."}` (DRF:pagination.py:195). Config views validate page/page_size themselves first (`views.py:32-44`).
- **NOQ** = no query params allowed; any query param (even `?format=json`, cache-busters) or repeated key => 400 `["Unsupported or repeated query parameters."]` (`views.py:32-34`).
- Error shapes: **E-PERM** 403 `{"detail":"No permission for this business operation."}` (`access.py:15`) or `"Authentication is required."` (`access.py:8`); unauthenticated at DRF layer => 403 `{"detail":"Authentication credentials were not provided."}` (first authenticator is SessionAuthentication, no WWW-Authenticate => 403 not 401; tests accept 401/403, `app/tests/test_configuration_api.py:32`). **E-404** `{"detail":"Not found."}` (or custom detail, e.g. `"Configuration record not found."`). Non-superuser hitting a nonexistent business gets 403 (access check runs before `get_object_or_404(Business)`, `views.py:25-26`); superuser gets 404. **E-LIST** 400 body is a bare JSON array `["message"]` (view/service-level `ValidationError('msg')`, DRF:exceptions.py:156-161). **E-MAP** 400 `{"query":"msg"}` string value (not list) from observability `params()` (`observability/views.py:61`). **E-SER** 400 serializer shape `{"field":["msg"]}`, `{"non_field_errors":["msg"]}`, `{"unknown":["fieldA"]}` for unrecognised keys (`overview/configuration.py:20-27`), nested `{"contact":{"email":["..."]}}`. **E-409** `{"detail":"Configuration version changed. Read the current version and retry."}` (`overview/configuration.py:109-111`) or custom detail. No custom exception handler is configured (grep EXCEPTION_HANDLER: none), so these are DRF defaults.
- Success timestamps: config views run output through `public()` => `datetime.isoformat()` (`+00:00`, `configuration/presentation.py:13-14`); `freshness/` GET and `connections/{id}/test/` use DRF encoders/fields => `...Z` (DRF:utils/encoders.py:30-33). Decimals => strings (`presentation.py:15-16`). TIME_ZONE=UTC, USE_TZ=True (`bizora/settings.py:151,155`).

| # | Method | Path (relative) | Auth | Query | Request body | Success response | Errors | Source |
