Method/URL mismatch hazards (verified-by-reading, not executed): the same View class serves collection and detail URLs. `POST connections/{id}/` or `POST sources/{id}/` or `POST rules/{key}/` pass an unexpected `pk`/`key` kwarg to `post(self, request, business_id[, section])` => `TypeError` => HTTP 500; `PATCH connections/`, `PATCH sources/`, `PATCH rules/` lack the required `pk`/`key` => 500 (`views.py:86,95,160,165,182,187`). `PUT` on rules/policies/connections/sources, any `DELETE`, and `PATCH/POST` on sections => 405 (no handler). There is NO delete anywhere; retire via `status`.

`slug:key` URL converter accepts `[-a-zA-Z0-9_]+` (any case) but POST only creates keys matching `^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,99}$` (`views.py:90`).

#### 2.1 Request-body blocks

**B-SECTION (singletons profile/general/ai/knowledge; `save_record`, `services.py:93-152`)**
- Body must be a JSON object else 400 `["Expected an object."]` (services.py:98).
- Control fields: `version` (optional int>=0, bool rejected; must equal current stored version, `0` when no row yet, else 409; omit => last-write-wins merge; services.py:104-108), `reason` (optional str<=500 and must not match credential-looking text; services.py:126-128), `key` (optional, must equal `"default"`). `status`,`priority`,`valid_from`,`valid_to` are REJECTED for singletons => 400 `["Singleton configuration is applied immediately."]` (services.py:111). Every other key is a settings field.
- Merge semantics: the body is deep-merged onto `defaults + stored data` (dicts recursive, lists/scalars replaced; `services.py:20-24,129-134`). PUT therefore behaves as a PARTIAL update; omitted fields keep prior values; nested keys cannot be deleted. Then the full object is validated by the section serializer; any unknown key (at any nesting) => 400 `{"unknown":[...]}`.
- Side effects per successful write: row lock on Business (`select_for_update`), version+1 (even when nothing changed, no change detection), new append-only `ConfigurationRevision`, new `ConfigurationEvent` (dedupe `record:{pk}:v{n}`) in the same transaction (services.py:138-145, 32-37). PROFILE and GENERAL also copy `currency`, `timezone` (+ `name` for PROFILE) into the `Business` row and bump `Business.updated_at` (services.py:146-151). On GET, Business is authoritative for those 3 fields (services.py:67-76).
- Section fields (types/validation; `app/configuration/schemas.py`):
  - **profile** (93-103): `name` str<=255 non-blank; `description` str<=2000 (blank ok); `industry` str<=100; `contact{email(EmailField, blank ok), phone str<=50, website URLField}`; `location{country ^[A-Z]{2}$, city<=150, address<=500}`; `operating_regions` list[str<=255] max 50; `channels` list of enum (section 3, PROFILE channels) max 5; `currency` ^[A-Z]{3}$; `timezone` valid IANA name (`ZoneInfo`, 85-90); `metadata` JSON object (bounded_json: depth<=5, <=50 keys per object, <=50 list items, strings<=2000 chars, total<=32000 chars, keys must not match `token|secret|password|authorization|api.?key|cookie|credential`, values must not look like credentials; 35-66).
  - **general** (122-142): `locale`,`language` ^[a-z]{2,3}(-[A-Z]{2})?$; `timezone`; `currency`; `date_format`; `time_format`; `decimal_places` int 0..6; `decimal_separator`; `thousands_separator`; `currency_display`; `reporting_period`; `week_start` int 0..6 (Monday=0, `overview/windows.py:33-35`); `operational_defaults{order_status, conversation_priority, delivery_region str<=100}`; `features{calling,marketing,website,support,delivery,graph,ai_recommendations: bool}`; `metadata`. Cross-check: decimal_separator != thousands_separator => `{"non_field_errors":["Decimal and thousands separators must differ."]}`. Enum values in section 3.
  - **ai** (268-304): required in effect (defaulted): `enabled` bool; `provider` str<=50, must be a key of `settings.BIZORA_AI_PROVIDERS` (NOT defined in settings.py => only `mock`; schemas.py:27-28); `models` object {task: model} with task in TASKS and model in the provider's list (default mock models, section 3); `features{business_assistant,customer_support,marketing,summarization,recommendations,suggested_replies: bool}`; `context_categories` list[CONTEXTS] max 10; `response{language str<=30, detail, tone}`; `confidence_threshold` Decimal(5,4) 0..1 and >= platform minimum `settings.BIZORA_AI_MIN_CONFIDENCE` (default `0.6`; not in settings.py); `escalation{on_low_confidence bool (False => 400 "Low-confidence escalation is mandatory."), topics list[str]}`; `allowed_actions` list[ACTIONS] max 4; `execution{approval_required (must be true; false => 400 `{"execution":{"approval_required":["Platform approval requirements cannot be disabled."]}}`), disabled_actions list[ACTIONS]}`; `parameters{temperature Decimal(3,2) 0..2, max_output_tokens int 1..8192}`; `workflows` object {task: {enabled, context_categories (must be subset of top-level), confidence_threshold (>= top-level), allowed_actions (subset of top-level), response}}. The key `guardrails` is NOT accepted on write (400 `{"unknown":["guardrails"]}`; tested `test_configuration_api.py:76`).
  - **knowledge** (334-358): `knowledge_types` list[KNOWLEDGE_TYPES] max 8; `ingestion{allowed_types list[SOURCE_TYPES] max 4, max_document_bytes int 1024..52428800}`; `chunk_size` 100..8000; `chunk_overlap` 0..2000 and < chunk_size; `embedding_model` str<=150 in `settings.BIZORA_EMBEDDING_MODELS` (default `["mock-embedding"]`; not in settings.py); `vector{enabled, limit 1..100, minimum_similarity Decimal(5,4) 0..1}`; `keyword{enabled, limit 1..100}`; `hybrid{enabled, vector_weight, keyword_weight Decimal(5,4) 0..1, must sum to exactly 1; enabled requires vector.enabled and keyword.enabled}`; `retrieval_limit` 1..100; `workflow_scopes` {task: list[KNOWLEDGE_TYPES]} (task in TASKS; values subset of knowledge_types); `include_archived_for_history` bool.
  - **Defaults returned before first write** (schemas.py:428-443): profile `{description:'',industry:'',contact:{},location:{},operating_regions:[],channels:[],metadata:{}}` + Business name/currency/timezone; general `{locale:'en',language:'en',date_format:'YYYY-MM-DD',time_format:'24H',decimal_places:2,decimal_separator:'.',thousands_separator:',',currency_display:'CODE',reporting_period:'DAY',week_start:0,operational_defaults:{},features:{},metadata:{}}`; ai `{enabled:false,provider:'mock',models:{},features:{5 tasks:false},context_categories:['profile','facts','approved_policies'],response:{language:'en',detail:'NORMAL',tone:'PROFESSIONAL'},confidence_threshold:'0.7',escalation:{on_low_confidence:true,topics:[]},allowed_actions:[],execution:{approval_required:true,disabled_actions:[]},parameters:{temperature:'0.2',max_output_tokens:1000},workflows:{}}`; knowledge `{knowledge_types:all 8, ingestion:{allowed_types:['MANUAL','UPLOAD'],max_document_bytes:10485760}, chunk_size:500, chunk_overlap:50, embedding_model:'mock-embedding', vector:{enabled:true,limit:10,minimum_similarity:'0.7'}, keyword:{enabled:true,limit:10}, hybrid:{enabled:true,vector_weight:'0.6',keyword_weight:'0.4'}, retrieval_limit:10, workflow_scopes:{}, include_archived_for_history:false}`.

**B-RULE (POST/PATCH rules/; kind RULE; schemas.py:209-222)** Control: `key` (POST required, regex above; PATCH optional but must equal URL key else 400 `["Stable configuration keys cannot be renamed."]`), `status` (STATES; default `DRAFT` on create, existing on PATCH), `priority` int 0..10000 (default 0; 400 `["Priority must be an integer from 0 to 10000."]`), `valid_from`/`valid_to` ISO-8601 datetime WITH explicit tz offset (else 400 `["Times must be ISO 8601 datetimes with an explicit timezone."]`; explicit `null` clears; stored UTC), `version`, `reason`. Rejected: `valid_to <= valid_from`, or status ACTIVE with `valid_to <= now` => 400 `["Policy/rule end must follow its effective start."]` (services.py:119-125). Settings: `category` (required, enum), `conditions` (required) = exactly one of `{"all":[Condition...]}` or `{"any":[...]}` (1..30 items; else `{"conditions":{"non_field_errors":["Supply exactly one all or any condition list."]}}`), Condition `{field ^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,99}$, operator, value}` (gt/gte/lt/lte need a finite decimal scalar |x|<1e18, <=6 dp, stored as string; in/not_in need list of 1..50 scalars; eq/ne need a scalar), `scope` optional `{product_id,campaign_id,customer_id (must exist in this business), region<=150, courier<=100, channel<=100, customer_type<=50, category<=100}`, `sample_metric` ^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,99}$, `min_samples` int 1..1e9 (requires `sample_metric`), `window_days` 1..366, `outcome` (required) `{type, code ^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{0,99}$, severity}`, `description<=2000`. Because PATCH deep-merges, switching `all`<->`any` on an existing rule yields both keys and fails validation; scope keys cannot be removed via PATCH (verified-by-reading).
**B-POLICY (kind POLICY; schemas.py:361-423)**: same control fields; settings `category` (enum, required), `title` str<=255 (required), `terms` str<=8000 (required key, may be blank for non-ACTIVE), `scope` (same as rule), `ai_available` bool (default false), `parameters` object validated per category (typed contracts below). When resulting status != ACTIVE the parameters serializer runs `partial` (drafts may be incomplete); ACTIVE requires non-blank `terms` and non-empty complete `parameters` (400 `{"non_field_errors":["Active policies require terms and complete parameters."]}`).
- DELIVERY: `coverage_regions` list[str] non-empty (req), `estimated_days` 0..366 (req), `charge` Decimal(18,2)>=0, `preferred_couriers` list, `cod_enabled` bool, `cod_limit` Decimal(18,2)>=0, `failure_instructions`<=2000.
- RETURN: `window_days` 0..366 (req), `eligibility` list non-empty (req), `exclusions` list, `procedure`<=2000 (req).
- REFUND: `window_days` (req), `methods` list non-empty (req), `approval_required` bool (req), `eligibility` list non-empty (req), `procedure` (req).
- CANCELLATION: `allowed_statuses` list non-empty (req), `cutoff_hours` 0..8760 (req), `procedure` (req).
- SUPPORT: `response_time_seconds` 1..2592000 (req), `escalation_conditions` list non-empty (req), `instructions`<=2000 (req).
- PRODUCT: `constraints` list non-empty (req), `instructions`<=2000 (req). (lists: str<=255 each, max 50)

**B-CONN (connections; integrations.py:60-158)** Allowed keys: `provider_id` (POST required int, must be an active IntegrationSource; PATCH may repeat it only if unchanged, else 400 `["Create another connection to change providers."]`), `connection_id` (must equal pk if sent), `name`<=150, `enabled` bool, `is_mock` bool, `entities` list[str]<=20, `scopes` list[str]<=20, `sync{mode, interval_seconds 60..2592000}`, `webhooks{enabled, events list of ^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,100}$ max 50, endpoint URL<=1000 (blank ok)}` (enabled requires non-empty events and an `https://` endpoint), `provider_settings` JSON object (same bounded_json rules; credential-like keys rejected), `credentials` object, `mock{connection_outcome, processing_outcome (optional), product_name_field ^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,99}$}`, plus `version`, `reason`. `status`,`priority`,`valid_from`,`valid_to`,`key` => 400 `["Connection setup accepts settings, version, and reason."]` (integrations.py:124). Status is DERIVED: `enabled` true => record status ACTIVE and connection `CONNECTED` only if it was `DISABLED` (other runtime states like ERROR/EXPIRED preserved); `enabled` false => `DISABLED` (integrations.py:145-157). POST defaults when only `provider_id` is sent: name=provider name, enabled=true, is_mock=true, entities+scopes = ALL capabilities of the provider category, sync `{MANUAL,900}`, webhooks disabled, provider_settings `{}`, credentials `{}`, mock `{connection_outcome:'SUCCESS', product_name_field:'name'}` (integrations.py:96-106).
- Validation: provider category must have capabilities (see 3); every selected entity needs its READ scope (`ENTITY_SCOPES`, CUSTOMER on communication providers needs `READ_CONVERSATIONS`); live (`is_mock:false`) + enabled needs >=1 credential; live + webhooks.enabled needs `credentials.webhook_secret` (integrations.py:74-93).
- **Secrets**: `credentials` keys limited to `api_key, access_token, refresh_token, client_id, client_secret, webhook_secret`; each value MUST match `env:BIZORA_BUSINESS_{business_id}_[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{0,100}` (a reference to a deployment env var), otherwise 400 `{"non_field_errors":["Credentials must be business-scoped secret references, never secret values."]}`. Merge semantics mean a credential slot can be added/replaced but NOT removed through the API.
- Writes: `IntegrationConnection` row created (POST) or updated (`name`,`is_mock`,`status`,`config` merged,`updated_at`), plus ConfigurationRecord(kind INTEGRATION, key=str(connection pk)) + revision + event. Whole save is atomic (failed validation rolls the new connection back).

**B-SRC (sources; knowledge.py:13-72)** Allowed keys: `name`<=255, `source_type`, `knowledge_type`, `source_reference`<=1000 (WEBSITE type must start `https://`; credential-looking text rejected), `source_id` (must equal pk if sent), `status`, `version`, `reason`. Forbidden: `priority, valid_from, valid_to, key` => 400 `["Source configuration accepts settings, status, version, and reason."]`. POST first creates a `KnowledgeSource` draft row (`name 'Draft source'`, `MANUAL`, `DRAFT`) then applies the body over defaults (`knowledge_type 'INTERNAL'`, `source_reference 'manual:unconfigured'`); status defaults `DRAFT`. Setting `status:"ACTIVE"` requires `source_type` in knowledge `ingestion.allowed_types` and `knowledge_type` in `knowledge_types` else 400 `["Source type and knowledge category must be enabled before approval."]` (rolled back). KnowledgeSource row mirrors the latest effective ACTIVE revision (a later DRAFT edit does not displace the approved version, knowledge.py:65-71).

**B-REPROC (knowledge.py:75-122)** `{idempotency_key ^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,100}$ (req), action 'REPROCESS'|'REEMBED' (req), exactly one of source_id|document_id (int>=1)}`; unknown keys => `{"unknown":[...]}`; both/neither => `{"non_field_errors":["Choose exactly one source or document."]}`. Source/document must belong to the business (400 `["Source does not belong to this business."]` / `["Document does not belong to this business and source."]`); source must have an effective ACTIVE approval (400 `["Source must be approved before requesting processing."]`). Side effects: inserts a `KnowledgeReprocessingRequest` pinning the effective KNOWLEDGE config revision, source revision and document version; if no KNOWLEDGE row exists yet it **creates one with defaults** (version 1, revision, event; knowledge.py:116). Nothing executes: status stays `REQUESTED`; no code anywhere updates it (grep: only app/configuration references the model).

**B-MON (monitoring PUT; overview/configuration.py:61-85)** Optional keys (strict; unknown => `{"unknown":[...]}`): `version` int>=0 (409 if != stored, 0 when none), `period`, `inherit_reporting_period` bool, `comparison_mode`, `snapshot_ttl_seconds` int 60..31536000, `domains` list[DOMAINS] non-empty max 7, `kpis` list max 50 of KPI rule, `issue_severities` list[SEVERITIES], `recommendation_priorities` list[SEVERITIES], `cross_domain` list of CROSS_DOMAIN keys max 9. KPI rule: `key` ^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,99}$ (must be in the display catalog or an active BusinessMetric of this business; unique per list), `direction` (default NEUTRAL), `target_min,target_max,warning,critical,target_value,baseline_value` Decimal(24,6), `warning_severity` LOW|MEDIUM|HIGH, `critical_severity` HIGH|CRITICAL, `goal`<=255, `sources[{connection_id (must be this business's), entity_type ^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{0,99}$}]` max 20. Rules: target_min<=target_max; warning/critical need HIGHER|LOWER; critical at least as adverse as warning (lines 35-58, 74-84). Top-level merge is shallow: `kpis` etc. REPLACE the whole list. Giving `period` without `inherit_reporting_period` sets inherit=false (overview/configuration.py:125-128). Writes MonitoringConfiguration + MonitoringConfigurationRevision + ConfigurationEvent(section `MONITORING`); NO ConfigurationRecord row (test_configuration_api.py:94).

**B-FRESH (freshness PUT; observability/views.py:254-285)** Keys allowed: `connection_id` (int or null; must belong to business), `entity_type` (str<=100, default ''), `delayed_seconds, stale_seconds, observation_ttl_seconds, window_seconds, min_samples, min_connections, latency_ms, max_retries, sync_timeout_seconds` (ints, bool rejected; model rules: all >0, 0<delayed<=stale), `error_ratio` float in (0,1], `enabled` bool. Unknown key (incl. `version`, `id`) => 400 `["Unknown policy fields."]`. Upsert keyed on (business, connection_id, entity_type); only supplied fields change. No optimistic concurrency. Also writes ConfigurationRecord(kind FRESHNESS, key=str(policy pk)) + revision + event (`record_native_change`, services.py:79-90). Model defaults: delayed 900, stale 1800, ttl 3600, window 900, min_samples 3, min_connections 2, error_ratio 0.25, latency_ms 2000, max_retries 3, sync_timeout 3600, enabled true (observability/models.py:21-37).

#### 2.2 Response blocks

- **R-RECORD** (`services.py:53-58`): `{id, kind, key, version, status, priority, valid_from, valid_to, settings{...}, updated_at, updated_by_id}`. Singleton GET with no row: `{kind, key:"default", version:0, status:"ACTIVE", settings, updated_at:null, updated_by_id:null}` (NO `id/priority/valid_*` keys; services.py:75-76). `settings` for kind AI always has extra read-only `guardrails` (below); for kind INTEGRATION `credentials` is replaced (see R-CONN). **Do not echo `settings` back unchanged on PUT (unknown `guardrails`/`credentials` shapes are rejected).**
- **R-REV** (`services.py:60-65`): `{id (revision id), key, kind, version, status, priority, valid_from, valid_to, settings, actor_id, reason, created_at}`; order `-version`; append-only (no delete).
- **guardrails** (schemas.py:22-24), constant: `{external_execution_requires_approval:true, ai_may_create_verified_facts:false, ai_may_create_authoritative_relationships:false, automatic_external_execution:false, evidence_required:true}`.
- **R-CONN** (`integrations.py:112-117`): R-RECORD fields (with `id` OVERRIDDEN to the IntegrationConnection pk) + `observability: "/api/businesses/{business_id}/observability/connections/{id}/"` (hard-coded `/api/`, points at an UNMOUNTED route). `settings` = `{connection_id, provider_id, name, enabled, is_mock, entities, scopes, sync, webhooks, provider_settings (keys matching SENSITIVE => "[REDACTED]"), credentials:{configured: bool, fields: [slot names sorted]}, mock}`. Placeholder when the connection has no ConfigurationRecord: `version:0`, `status` `ACTIVE`/`DISABLED`, no priority/valid_*/updated_*.
- **R-SRC** (`knowledge.py:30-36`): R-RECORD (+`id` = KnowledgeSource pk). Placeholder for legacy sources with no config record: `{version:0, kind:'SOURCE', key, status:'UNAPPROVED', settings:{name,source_type,knowledge_type,source_reference}, id}` - `UNAPPROVED` is not one of STATES.
- **R-REPROC** (`knowledge.py:125-130`): `{id, source_id, document_id, action, status:"REQUESTED", configuration_revision_id, source_revision_id, document_version, created_at, requested_by_id, execution_owner:"BUSINESS_MEMORY_KNOWLEDGE", worker_available:false}`.
- **R-ATTEMPT** (`observability/serializers.py:105-109`): `{id, action:"connection_test", target_id, status (RUNNING|SUCCEEDED|FAILED|UNSUPPORTED), actor_id, result{...}, created_at, completed_at, next_retry_at:null}`. `result` for mock: `{health_outcome: SUCCESS|DEGRADED|ERROR|AUTH_ERROR|EXPIRED, is_mock:true}`; status SUCCEEDED only for SUCCESS, FAILED otherwise (operations.py:178-184,199-200). Live connection without a registered adapter: status UNSUPPORTED, result `{code:"UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY", message, is_mock:false}` (operations.py:201-203; `integrations/operations.py:91-97`). Replaying the same `idempotency_key` returns the stored attempt (200/409 again) without re-running (operations.py:151-155). Side effects: OperationAttempt, ObservationReceipt+ConnectionCheck, IntegrationError rows on non-SUCCESS, ObservabilityAudit; it does NOT change `IntegrationConnection.status` (grep services.py: no connection.status write).
- **R-EFF** (`views.py:132-136`): `{business_id, as_of (server now), sections:{profile,general,ai,knowledge: R-RECORD-like}, rules:[CONTRACT], policies:[CONTRACT], approved_sources:[CONTRACT], guardrails}`. `sections.ai.settings` is replaced by the EFFECTIVE view (`selectors.py:93-115`): stored AI settings with `confidence_threshold` raised to max(stored, platform min, workflow), `allowed_actions` filtered by `disabled_actions`/workflow, `response` merged with workflow response, plus `effective_context_categories`, `workflow_enabled` (only when `workflow` given), `unavailable_reason:"PROVIDER_OR_MODEL_NOT_REGISTERED"` + `enabled:false` if provider/model not registered. `sections.knowledge.settings` = stored + `effective_categories` + `include_archived` (selectors.py:124-130). CONTRACT = `{id (REVISION id), key, version, settings (raw stored data), priority, valid_from, valid_to, rule_reference:"config:{kind lower}:{key}:v{version}"}` (selectors.py:28-31). Rules/policies/sources included only if latest non-DRAFT revision is ACTIVE, `valid_from<=now`, `valid_to` null or >now, and scope matches the supplied scope keys (a rule's scope keys must ALL be supplied and equal) (selectors.py:13-25). Order: `-priority,key` (policies additionally by scope specificity). No `as_of` query param exists (always server now).
- **R-MON** (`overview/configuration.py:97-107`): `{period, snapshot_ttl_seconds, domains, kpis, issue_severities, recommendation_priorities, cross_domain, inherit_reporting_period, comparison_mode, version, updated_at, updated_by_id, week_start}`; defaults at lines 14-17; `period` and `week_start` come from `general` when inheriting; before first save `version:0`, inherit true. Passed through overview `public()` (key-based redaction on SENSITIVE names, `overview/views.py:28-39`).

#### 2.3 Related LIVE endpoint (adjacent, not part of config urls)
| Method | Path | Status | Auth | Body | Response | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/businesses/{business_id}/contracts/configuration/policy/` (also under `/api/v1/`) | **LIVE** | BusinessMembership (domain `authorize`, `app/domains/common.py:123-130`) | `{"request_id": str<=180, "payload": {"policy_key": str<=2000}}` exactly these two keys | 200 envelope `{version:"bizora.domain.v1", business_id, owner:"Configuration", capability:"policy", request_id, status, result, simulated, reason?}`; with no `BIZORA_DOMAIN_ADAPTERS` entry (settings.py defines none) => `status:"UNAVAILABLE", result:null, simulated:false, reason:"No owner adapter is configured."` Not wired to `app/configuration` selectors. | app/urls.py (last line), app/domain_views.py:344-352, app/domains/boundaries.py:8-9,36-52,55-110 |

### 3. ENUMS (exact strings from code)

- ConfigurationRecord.kind (`configuration/models.py:6`): `PROFILE GENERAL AI KNOWLEDGE RULE POLICY INTEGRATION SOURCE FRESHNESS`. ConfigurationEvent.section (free text <=20) additionally `MONITORING` (overview/configuration.py:137).
- STATES / status (models.py:7): `DRAFT ACTIVE DISABLED SUPERSEDED ARCHIVED`. Singletons always `ACTIVE`; connections derive `ACTIVE|DISABLED`; sources may show `UNAPPROVED` (placeholder only). Defaults on create: rules/policies/sources `DRAFT`.
- KnowledgeReprocessingRequest.action: `REPROCESS REEMBED`; status `REQUESTED` (only value written; models.py:71-72).
- `section` URL kwarg values (views.py:16-17): `profile general ai knowledge rules policies connections sources freshness` (last three only via history).
- TASKS (schemas.py:17): `business_assistant customer_support marketing summarization recommendations`. CONTEXTS (18): `profile facts metrics issues relationships graph approved_policies knowledge goals`. ACTIONS (19): `CREATE_SUPPORT_DRAFT PROPOSE_COURIER_CHANGE PROPOSE_CAMPAIGN_CHANGE CREATE_TASK`. KNOWLEDGE_TYPES (20): `PROFILE POLICY PRODUCT SUPPORT BRAND INTERNAL GOALS DECISIONS`. SOURCE_TYPES (21): `MANUAL UPLOAD WEBSITE INTEGRATION`. PERIODS (16): `DAY WEEK MONTH ROLLING_7D ROLLING_30D`.
- PROFILE channels (100): `WEBSITE SOCIAL_COMMERCE MARKETPLACE PHYSICAL DIRECT`.
- GENERAL: date_format `YYYY-MM-DD DD/MM/YYYY MM/DD/YYYY`; time_format `12H 24H`; decimal_separator `. ,`; thousands_separator `. , " " ""` (space and empty string); currency_display `CODE SYMBOL`; operational_defaults.order_status `PENDING CONFIRMED`; conversation_priority `LOW NORMAL HIGH` (schemas.py:107-133).
- AI: response.detail `BRIEF NORMAL DETAILED`; response.tone `PROFESSIONAL FRIENDLY FORMAL`; default provider/models `mock` / `mock-business mock-support mock-marketing mock-summary mock-recommendation` (schemas.py:28); embedding default `mock-embedding` (31-32).
- RULE: category `ORDERS CUSTOMERS DELIVERY MARKETING SUPPORT PAYMENTS INTELLIGENCE WEBSITE`; operator `gt gte lt lte eq ne in not_in`; condition list `all|any`; outcome.type `CLASSIFY ALERT REQUIRE_APPROVAL`; outcome.severity `LOW MEDIUM HIGH CRITICAL` (schemas.py:174-206,210).
- evaluate_rule results (service-only, selectors.py:40-83): `MATCH NO_MATCH UNKNOWN INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE`.
- POLICY category: `DELIVERY RETURN REFUND CANCELLATION SUPPORT PRODUCT` (362).
- Integration: provider capability groups (integrations.py:16-24): COMMERCE entities `PRODUCT CUSTOMER ORDER PAYMENT REFUND` / scopes `READ_PRODUCTS READ_CUSTOMERS READ_ORDERS READ_PAYMENTS`; MARKETING `CAMPAIGN CAMPAIGN_METRIC CONVERSION` / `READ_CAMPAIGNS READ_METRICS`; ANALYTICS `WEBSITE_VISITOR WEBSITE_SESSION WEBSITE_EVENT` / `READ_ANALYTICS`; COMMUNICATION `CONVERSATION MESSAGE CUSTOMER` / `READ_CONVERSATIONS SEND_MESSAGES`; COURIER `SHIPMENT SHIPMENT_EVENT` / `READ_SHIPMENTS CREATE_SHIPMENTS`; CALLING `CALL CALL_EVENT TRANSCRIPT` / `READ_CALLS INITIATE_CALLS`. Category mapping (uppercased `IntegrationSource.category`): `ECOMMERCE->COMMERCE`, `COURIER_DELIVERY->COURIER`, `MESSAGING->COMMUNICATION`, `SUPPORT->COMMUNICATION`; any other value not in the six groups => empty capabilities => connection cannot be configured (line 33-35, 79). Entity->scope map `ENTITY_SCOPES` lines 25-30.
- sync.mode `FULL INCREMENTAL SCHEDULED MANUAL`; mock.connection_outcome `SUCCESS DEGRADED ERROR AUTH_ERROR EXPIRED`; mock.processing_outcome `SUCCESS TIMEOUT`; credential slots `api_key access_token refresh_token client_id client_secret webhook_secret` (integrations.py:38-90).
- IntegrationConnection.status values seen: `CONNECTED DISCONNECTED DISABLED EXPIRED SYNCING ERROR` (observability/views.py:84); config writes only `DISABLED`, `CONNECTED` (from DISABLED).
- OperationAttempt.status (observability/models.py:211): `RUNNING SUCCEEDED FAILED UNSUPPORTED`; action `connection_test`.
- Monitoring: DOMAINS `sales customers marketing website support delivery payments`; SEVERITIES `CRITICAL HIGH MEDIUM LOW INFO`; comparison_mode `PREVIOUS_PERIOD TARGET BASELINE NONE`; KPI direction `HIGHER LOWER NEUTRAL`; warning_severity `LOW MEDIUM HIGH`; critical_severity `HIGH CRITICAL`; CROSS_DOMAIN keys `marketing_revenue marketing_delivery website_sales product_support product_delivery customer_retention support_customer_outcome courier_revenue campaign_customer_quality`; KPI catalog keys per domain (overview/catalog.py:3-14), DEFAULT_KPIS (15-16).
- BusinessAccess.role (observability/models.py:15): `READER MANAGER`.
- ChangesView `delivery_state`: constant `AVAILABLE_FOR_CONSUMERS`.

**What DOES exist (exact invocation; all require a working settings module and a DISPOSABLE DB):**
- `python manage.py simulate_configuration --at 2026-10-25T12:00:00+00:00 [--seed 77001] [--business-slug config-demo-...]` (`app/management/commands/simulate_configuration.py`; `--at` required, must be tz-aware ISO; slug must start `config-demo-` and be <=140 chars; creates business, 2 users `config-demo-manager-{id}`/`config-demo-reader-{id}` WITHOUT passwords, BusinessAccess MANAGER/READER, a second tenant `{slug}-other`; drives the real views through `APIRequestFactory`; prints ONE JSON line `{"as_of","business_slug","checks":[{"actual","expected","key","passed"}],"passed","seed"}` sort_keys; exits non-zero via CommandError if any check fails; idempotent re-run revalidates). Docs say run with a throwaway `BIZORA_DB_PATH` (settings.py:108).
- `python manage.py simulate_observability --at <ISO> [--seed 75001] [--business-slug obs-demo-...]` and `python manage.py simulate_overview --at <ISO> [--seed 76001] [--business-slug overview-demo-...]` (same JSON/exit contract; `simulate_overview` imports `app.overview.demo`).
- `python manage.py demo_domains --username <existing active user>` : refuses if DB is not SQLite or is the repo `db.sqlite3` (demo_domains.py:23-25); creates business slug `bizora-domains-demo` + `BusinessMembership(role OWNER)` for that user, a mock WooCommerce-style connection, customer 52, product 101, order 1001, payment, conversation, shipment, identity link; prints IDs and `/api/businesses/{id}/customers/{pk}/360/`.
- `python manage.py demo_completion --username <user>` : calls `demo_domains`, then verifies identity matching, login journey, support priority; prints `/api/businesses/{id}/conversations/{pk}/context/`, `/visitors/{pk}/journey/`, `/ops/business/{id}/overview/`. README flow: `DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE=bizora.local_settings`, `BIZORA_DATA_DIR=$(mktemp -d ...)`, `migrate`, `createsuperuser`, `demo_completion --username ...` (README.md:17-27).
- `python manage.py preflight_domains` (read-only schema/duplicate audit; refuses repo db.sqlite3).
- `python scripts/verify_domains_postgres.py` (Docker pgvector container, runs the test suite; not a simulator) and `python scripts/audit_domain_requirements.py` (rebuilds docs audit files for specs business-data / identity-resulation / business-oparations only; DB-free).
- Library-only (no HTTP): see rows 6 above; `app/domains/boundaries.py FixtureAdapter` (requires `settings.BIZORA_DOMAIN_ADAPTERS` + `BIZORA_ALLOW_FIXTURE_ADAPTERS`, boundaries.py:34-45); `app/events/` in-process pub/sub (`subscribe/dispatch`, deferred to `on_commit`, handler exceptions logged and swallowed; handlers: `integration.observation` wired in `AppConfig.ready` (apps.py:7-9), `health.checked` no-op in events/handlers.py which nothing imports); `ConfigurationEvent` is DB-poll only and does not use `app.events`.
=> Frontend plan: mark ALL Prototype Simulation UI (registry, scenario list/builder, seeds, event/payload viewers, flaw/edge toggles, expected results, validation report) as **backend-blocked**; at most a CLI-run report viewer could be built from the JSON the commands print, but nothing persists a report (only `MockExpectedResult` rows).

### 5. FRONTEND NOTES

1. NOTHING in section 2 is reachable today (UNMOUNTED). A frontend cannot call it until the backend adds `path('businesses/<int:business_id>/configuration/', include('app.configuration.urls'))` to app/urls.py (and settings are fixed). Plan against `/api/v1/businesses/{id}/configuration/...`.
2. Auth: DRF default Session + Basic (no token/JWT, no login API, no CORS app). Session writes need CSRF (SessionAuthentication). Only login routes in repo: `/ops/login/` (Django LoginView, `app/operations_urls.py`) and `/admin/`. Unauthenticated => 403 (not 401).
3. Authorization gotcha: every config call requires a `BusinessAccess` row (READER/MANAGER) or superuser. Users who are `BusinessMembership` members of a business (the model used by all LIVE domain APIs and /ops UI) get 403 `"No permission for this business operation."` here unless they also have BusinessAccess. BusinessAccess can only be created in Django admin (superuser, `app/admin.py:36-39`) or by demo runners; there is no API to list/grant it and no "who am I / my role" route in app/urls.py or the configuration urls (grep). Domain APIs answer 404 `Business not found.` for inaccessible businesses (app/domain_views.py:28-33) while configuration answers 403 - the UI needs per-family handling. A UI cannot know in advance whether to show edit controls; use READER vs MANAGER 403 on first write, or have the backend add a role endpoint.
4. Concurrency: send the last-read `version` in every PUT/PATCH/POST body (`version:0` when a GET returned version 0). 409 `{"detail":...}` => refetch and retry. `version` is optional (omitted = last write wins). Not available for `freshness/` PUT and reprocessing. Every successful write bumps the version even if unchanged; avoid no-op saves.
5. PUT = partial deep merge. Send only changed fields. Lists replace. Nested objects cannot have keys removed. Never round-trip a GET `settings` object wholesale: AI `settings.guardrails` and INTEGRATION `settings.credentials{configured,fields}` are output-only and would be rejected (`{"unknown":["guardrails"]}` / credential-reference error). Strip them.
6. Errors are heterogeneous: arrays (`["msg"]`), `{"detail":"msg"}`, `{"query":"msg"}`, `{"field":["msg"]}` with nested objects, and `{"unknown":[...]}`. Build one normaliser; do not assume `field -> list`. Business-rule messages are not machine-coded (no `code` field).
7. Query hygiene: configuration endpoints reject ANY unknown query param, so never append cache-busters or `?format=`. Allowed: PAGE (page>=1, page_size 1..100), `status` on rules/policies, the scope+`workflow`+`historical` set on `effective/`, `connection`/`entity_type` on `freshness/`.
8. Lists: server-paginated, DRF `count/next/previous/results`, `next/previous` absolute URLs. Orders fixed (not configurable): rules/policies `-priority,key`; connections/sources/freshness/providers `pk`/`key`; reprocessing `-pk`; changes `-created_at`; history `-version`. No search or filter beyond the above (no category filter, no text search).
9. History 404 vs empty: `*/history/` returns 404 until the record exists (singletons before first save; connections/sources never configured through the API). Treat 404 as "no history yet". `monitoring/history/` returns an empty page instead.
10. Ids: R-CONN/R-SRC `id` is the connection/source pk while `key` is `str(pk)`; R-REV `id` and CONTRACT `id` are revision ids; `freshness/{policy_id}/history/` uses MonitoringPolicy.pk. `changes/` items give `section`+`key`+`version` to correlate.
11. Secrets: responses NEVER contain a credential reference or value, only `credentials:{configured:bool, fields:[slot names]}` (see section 6 for the exact redaction rules). The UI cannot enter a secret; it can only enter the reference string `env:BIZORA_BUSINESS_{business_id}_NAME` (uppercase `[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{0,100}`), and ops must set that env var on the server. Credential slots cannot be removed once added.
12. Redaction false positives: all string values in config responses pass `SECRET_TEXT` redaction (`presentation.py:22`). Verified with a standalone copy of the regex: `"Basic delivery applies"` => `"Basic [REDACTED] applies"`; `"bearer xyz"` => `"bearer [REDACTED]"`; `"token: abc"` => `"token: [REDACTED]"`; `"Password: x y"` => `"Password: [REDACTED] y"`. Free-text fields such as policy `terms`, rule `description`, profile `description`, names are NOT rejected on input (only metadata/provider_settings/source_reference/`reason` are) but ARE rewritten on output. Show a hint or avoid those words; stored data is unchanged.
13. Time: `valid_from`/`valid_to` must be ISO-8601 with an explicit offset; stored and returned in UTC (`+00:00`). ACTIVE with future `valid_from` is scheduled (not effective until then); expiry is manual or by `valid_to`; "effective now" = `GET effective/` only (no as-of param).
14. Effective vs stored: `GET ai/` returns stored settings; `GET effective/` returns post-processed values (raised confidence, filtered actions, `workflow_enabled`). Use `effective/` for "what will actually apply" displays.
15. Draft semantics: editing an ACTIVE rule/policy/source with `status:"DRAFT"` stores a draft revision while the previous published version stays effective (selectors.py:13-21; tests `test_configuration_rules_policies.py:58-72`). The R-RECORD for that key then shows the draft's `status`/settings; the live version is only visible through `effective/` or history. Source rows: record `status` may be DRAFT while the underlying KnowledgeSource stays ACTIVE with old values (knowledge.py:65-71).
16. Connection UX: POST needs only `provider_id`; show `providers/` first. `connections/{id}/test/` is synchronous; HTTP 200 does not mean healthy: read `status`/`result.health_outcome`; 409 = UNSUPPORTED capability (body is still the attempt). Use a fresh `idempotency_key` per click or the stored result is replayed. A test does not change connection status.
17. Reprocessing: UI should label rows "requested, not executed" (`worker_available:false`). 201 vs 200 distinguishes new vs replay.
18. Enum/option lists are NOT served by any endpoint (including valid AI providers/models/embedding models, rule categories, KPI catalog for monitoring is only in unmounted `overview/catalog/`). The frontend must hard-code section 3 lists; AI provider/model choices are effectively `mock` only until `BIZORA_AI_PROVIDERS` is set in settings.
19. The `observability` link inside R-CONN points at an unmounted route; do not follow it.
20. No async/background behaviour exists in this domain; all calls are synchronous DB transactions (PostgreSQL row-locks the Business row to serialise writes; SQLite skips locking).

### 6. GAPS AND AMBIGUITIES

**Secrets handling (exact)**
- Inputs: raw secrets are rejected by `IntegrationSchema` (reference regex, integrations.py:85-88). Persistence: `ConfigurationRecord.data`/`ConfigurationRevision.data` and `IntegrationConnection.config['credentials']` store the REFERENCES (not values). Output: `safe_settings('INTEGRATION')` pops `credentials` and emits `{configured: bool(refs), fields: sorted(slot names)}` (services.py:40-47); `provider_settings, mock, sync, webhooks` pass through `safe_details` (keys matching `token|secret|password|authorization|api.?key|cookie|credential` become `"[REDACTED]"`, depth>8 `"[TRUNCATED]"`, strings credential-scrubbed and cut to 2000 chars, non-JSON types `"[UNSUPPORTED]"`; observability/policies.py:6-43). Applies to GET, POST/PATCH responses and history alike (`revision_data` uses the same path). Whole-response string scrub via `public()` (presentation.py:22). No endpoint returns resolved secrets; `resolve_credentials()` is trusted-code only (integrations.py:183-197, reads `os.environ`).
- Not redacted: `webhooks.endpoint` and `source_reference` URLs are returned as stored (URLs with embedded `user:pass@` or `?token=` would be scrubbed only if they match the SECRET_TEXT patterns, e.g. `token=...`; `user:pass@host` is not matched). `bounded_json` rejects credential-looking text in `source_reference` and `provider_settings`, but `webhooks.endpoint` (URLField) and free CharFields are not checked on input. (verified-by-reading)
- Legacy `IntegrationConnection.config` content is shown only through the fixed key list in `connection_settings` (unknown legacy keys are not echoed).

**Docs (docs/implementations/business-configuration*.md) vs code**
1. Impl doc "Paths are relative to /api/businesses/<business_id>/configuration/": true for fd41ea7 only; on 5f176a2 these routes are not mounted (and the "legacy Overview/Observability endpoints" it says share state are unmounted too). Docs never mention the lost mount.
2. Impl doc "Active business READERs and MANAGERs may read": `BusinessAccess` has no active flag (only `user.is_active` is checked, access.py:7); README says "business endpoints require explicit membership" (BusinessMembership) which is a different table - docs do not reconcile the two authorities. The only route to grant BusinessAccess is Django admin.
3. Impl doc "Version mismatches return 409. Supplying the last read version prevents overwrites": true for singletons/rules/policies/connections/sources/monitoring; NOT available on `freshness/` (strict field list rejects `version`) or `reprocessing/`.
4. Impl doc lists `GET / PUT freshness/` without noting PUT has no concurrency and returns 200 for create.
5. Impl doc says unknown/invalid query parameters "return 400": true, but the body shape differs per view (array vs `{"query":...}`).
6. Impl doc "Reads redact legacy credential-like fields": true for credentials/provider_settings/mock/sync/webhooks; not mentioned that unrelated text (e.g. "Basic ...") is also rewritten.
7. Progress doc test counts (197 PostgreSQL / 186 SQLite), "simulate_configuration 21/21 on SQLite and PostgreSQL", "makemigrations --check: no changes": NOT VERIFIED (cannot run; settings.py currently broken). The 21 checks are verified by counting `expectations` in `app/configuration/demo.py:125-147`; the 90-feature matrix row count (90) matches the spec's 10+9+11+10+14+12+12+12 sub-features.
8. Progress item 9 "expired-policy archiving": the code has no automatic archiving; the test `test_expired_policy_can_be_archived...` archives manually via PATCH (`test_configuration_rules_policies.py:95-100`).
9. Impl doc demo env uses `BIZORA_DB_PATH` with default settings; README uses `bizora.local_settings` + `BIZORA_DATA_DIR` (local_settings.py:9). Both exist; both import the currently broken `settings.py`.
10. Impl doc "Providers: public/provider catalogs expose provider definitions": `ProvidersView` returns every active IntegrationSource and fabricates `mock_supported:true` for all (views.py:146).

**Functional gaps / ambiguities**
- No seeded providers, no admin or API to create/disable `IntegrationSource`; `providers/` is empty on a fresh install. Spec providers (WooCommerce, Shopify, Meta, WhatsApp, GA4, Steadfast, Dialyra) exist only if rows are created manually; category strings must map to the six capability groups or the provider is unusable (integrations.py:33-35).
- No DELETE; no way to remove a stored credential slot, nested setting key, rule scope key, or switch rule `all`<->`any` via PATCH (deep-merge). No bulk operations.
- No endpoint for: AI provider/model/embedding option lists, rule dry-run/evaluate, as-of effective view, applying/consuming events, executing reprocessing, triggering sync, OAuth, secret provisioning, BusinessAccess management, current-user role.
- Mismatched verb/URL combinations produce HTTP 500 (TypeError) instead of 405 (section 2 note); a consumer error-boundary is needed until fixed.
- Authorization oddity: `require_access` runs before the Business existence check, so non-superusers cannot distinguish missing from foreign business (always 403); `404` only for superusers.
- `ConfigurationView`/`PolicyView`/`OperationView` come from other modules with their own `BusinessView.initial`; behaviour is equivalent (same `require_access`) but their `Pagination`/error handling differ (see E-MAP, Z-timestamps).
- `GET freshness/` response is not passed through `public()`/`safe_details` (plain values only; no secrets present).
- If remounted, `businesses/<id>/overview/` (domain OverviewView, live) and an overview include with the same prefix would conflict; not part of this scan. Configuration prefix itself has no empty-path route, so `businesses/<id>/configuration/` alone would fall to the generic domain `ResourceList` (name='configuration') and return a domain error (NOT VERIFIED).
- `settings.py` does not define `BIZORA_AI_PROVIDERS`, `BIZORA_EMBEDDING_MODELS`, `BIZORA_AI_MIN_CONFIDENCE`, `BIZORA_DOMAIN_ADAPTERS`; defaults apply (mock-only).
- Demo users are created without passwords (`create_user(username=...)`); they exist for in-process validation, not for UI login.
- `app/tests/test_configuration_*.py` hit `/api/businesses/{id}/configuration/...` and would 404 on origin/main for the same unmounted reason (NOT run).
